Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.9

Chapter 3205
The other King Monkey sat motionless on their horses, looking down with playful eyes.
The strength of Thanos was also one of the best in their line of work. This stick was enough to smash that kid into meat paste!
When those Bug Masters saw Thanos jump down, they were frightened and quickly scattered in all directions. The old Bug Master advised, "Benefactor, quickly, quickly leave …"
The Bug Master actually came forward and planned to push Luo Zheng away and bear the blow himself.
However, all the Bug Masters were very clear that no matter what they did, this human man and woman would undoubtedly die.
Luo Zheng supported the Bug Master, and at the same time, stretched out his hand to grab above.
Thanos saw Luo Zheng grabbing his double-pointed long stick with his bare hands, and his face showed a mocking expression. He had determined that Luo Zheng's previous arrogant words were completely brainless. This guy who came out of nowhere was not only arrogant, but also stupid!
This stick was enough to smash him into a pool of mud, and he dared to take it with his bare hands …
Just as Thanos was thinking about this, the double-pointed long stick had already smashed into Luo Zheng's hands.
Luo Zheng just took the opportunity to grab and drag. The power of his right arm suddenly erupted, and he forcibly stopped the stick from smashing down on his shoulders.
The other end of the long stick bore the power of Thanos itself. The whole stick bent like an arch bridge, but it never broke!
"Nice stick," Luo Zheng praised.
Thanos looked at Luo Zheng dumbfoundedly, not knowing what had happened …
Suddenly, he felt the stick in his hand suddenly turn, and there was a burning pain in his hands. The double-pointed long stick had been taken away by Luo Zheng.
Then Thanos felt a shock in his chest, and his chest had been penetrated by the double-pointed long stick.
When the stick rotated, Thanos's burly body also rotated, and a trace of powerful Nirvana Realm power was transmitted along the stick. In the process of this rotation, Thanos's body also directly disintegrated!
A grab, a drag, a turn, a few simple movements killed Thanos. The whole process only took three breaths.
Luo Zheng's other hand was even supporting the trembling insect breeder …
The entire valley was silent. The Bug Masters had never thought that these two visitors who had come from afar would possess such terrifying strength that they could kill the King Monkey so easily.
Only Min Qi and Min Mou's eyes lit up. They knew that Luo Zheng was very strong, but the strength that Luo Zheng had just displayed had once again refreshed their understanding of him!
The King Monkey on top of the col also had a serious look on their faces. Even their mounts could feel the blood in the col and remained motionless.
Killing Thanos so easily showed that this person's strength was far beyond their imagination!
Among the King Monkey, Mie Jie reacted the fastest. He pulled the horse's mane and shouted, "Let's go!"
The King Monkey turned their horses around and planned to escape. At this time, Luo Zheng's voice came again, "You all came back and you still want to leave? Is it that easy?"
"What makes you think you can stop us?" Mie Jie turned back and sneered at Luo Zheng in the col.
But at this time, the horse he was sitting on suddenly neighed. It was extremely uneasy and fearful, and even a King Monkey was directly thrown off by the horse.
These horses in the dark realm were not ordinary breeds, especially the King Monkey's mounts. They were Demon Blood Horses, which were very brave and had extremely strong combat power. There were very few things that they were afraid of.
Mie Jie looked over and only saw a human woman blocking in front of him.
It was still puzzled. What did this human girl do to scare its mount to this extent?
This confusion did not last long. Mie Jie saw the woman's hands slightly open, and two blazing white lights were constantly moving like a snake's tongue.
Seeing this white light, Mie Jie's pupils suddenly shrank.
As a member of the King Monkey Clan, he had stayed in the 14th Layer of Heaven for so many years. Naturally, he had seen the Lord of Heaven and the Infinite Heavenly Light of Brahma. He knew even more clearly that everything that was illuminated by this light disappeared completely, as if they had never existed in this world.
Mie Jie also knew that the west of the dark realm seemed to have been purified by the Infinite Heavenly Light once. Everything within a hundred miles had disappeared completely. This matter was quickly spread through the grapevine, and many versions of this matter were deduced.
Some said it was the Lord of Heaven who did it, while others said there was something wrong with the 14th Layer of Heaven.
When Mie Jie saw the white light blooming from the human woman's hands, he naturally associated these two things together.
It seemed that it was not the Lord of Heaven who did it. Perhaps there was an extra Pure Witch in the 14th Layer of Heaven.
Unfortunately, he did not have the chance to bring this news back to the King Monkey Clan.
"Buzz …"
Feng Ge's fingers fluttered, and the white light sword lightly flipped over the horse and the King Monkey. The light only flew a few rounds before the monkey and the horse were cut into pieces.
With Feng Ge's ability, she could completely eliminate these guys. However, she had already overused her own ability, so she had been very frugal since entering the dark realm.
After Luo Zheng climbed out of the valley, the Bug Trainers also climbed up one after another. They looked at the broken pieces on the ground, and did not expect such a thing to happen. The high and mighty King Monkey was destroyed by these two people in front of them. Who exactly were they …
"Clean this place up," Luo Zheng said.
Several Bug Trainers took out small round boxes and released a group of black bugs. These bugs were like a large piece of black sand, covering the King Monkey and the horse's body parts. In a short while, these body parts were completely devoured, and even the bloodstains on the ground disappeared.
"Even if we do this, I'm afraid the King Monkey Clan won't let us off," a Bug Master said bitterly.
"I'm afraid we will all die in two days …" Min Qi also looked depressed.
With more than ten King Monkeys dead, the King Monkey Clan would be angry and all the Bug Trainers would be exterminated.
"This will not happen," Luo Zheng comforted.
"Why not?" Min Qi asked.
"Because all the Black Monkeys will die," Luo Zheng answered calmly. Luo Zheng might be able to compromise on some things, but using Luo Yan to threaten him was not something Luo Zheng would allow.
No matter what kind of power was behind these Black Monkeys, Luo Zheng would not back down.
When the Bug Trainers heard this, they had an indescribable expression on their faces. Although Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had just killed more than ten King Monkeys, the King Monkey Clan had far more powerful people.
Now that Luo Zheng could sense the direction of the Shapeshifting Magic Block, there was no need for him to stay here. He and Feng Ge planned to follow it along the way.

Chapter 3206
The two brothers, Min Qi and Min Mou, were ready to risk everything.
Now that the situation had escalated, they might as well place their hopes on these two humans. Who knows, they might be able to create a miracle.
With the guidance of the Shapeshifting Magic Block, Luo Zheng did not really need the help of the Bug Masters.
However, these Bug Masters were more familiar with the Dark Domain than them. There were no disadvantages to bringing them along.
Luo Zheng thought for a while, then nodded and agreed. He only said, "It's a pity that Feng Ge was too ruthless. He didn't even leave a horse behind."
"These Demon Blood Horses are the exclusive mounts of the King Monkey. Even if they weren't killed, they wouldn't let us ride them." The old Bug Master climbed up the col shakily. At the same time, he took out a small bamboo flute and handed it to Min Qi.
Min Qi took the bamboo flute and blew it lightly. In the cave below the col, a few black beetles crawled out.
"Those are … Sacred Beetles!" Min Qi was a little excited when he saw these beetles.
The Bug Masters reared all kinds of snakes, insects, and ants. Among them, the Sacred Beetles were the hardest to tame. Juniors like Min Qi originally did not have the opportunity to control these Sacred Beetles.
"These Sacred Beetles will be our benefactor's mounts. Min Qi, Min Mou, you two must take good care of them," the old Bug Master instructed.
Luo Zheng also had a way to fly in the Dark Domain, but the process was more troublesome.
This was because the Divine Path energy conflicted with the Dark Domain. If he formed the Lightning Form Manifestation, his lightning wings would be quickly eroded by the darkness.
Since the Bug Masters were willing to give him flying mounts, Luo Zheng did not refuse.
After that, Luo Zheng, Feng Ge, Min Qi, and Min Mou all sat on the backs of the Sacred Beetles. As Min Qi blew the bamboo flute, the Sacred Beetles carried them into the dark sky.
Visibility in the Dark Domain was not good, especially after the Sacred Beetles flew up. The originally dim ground quickly became a blur.
The pitch-black sky was like a bottomless black hole. When Luo Zheng raised his head, he could faintly feel the pressure coming from the darkness. It was as if something was spying on him from the endless darkness.
"Has the Bug Master Clan explored the sky above the Dark Domain?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"I haven't seen it before," Min Qi replied truthfully. "However, it's recorded in the legends of our Bug Master race that our Great Ancestor rode a Sacred Beetle into the Dark Sky!"
"Did you find anything?" Luo Zheng asked.
"No." Min Qi shook his head and said, "The Great Founder never returned. Although the other Bug Masters said that the Clan Leader stayed in the Dark Sky, I think he most likely died there …"
As a native race, the Bug Master Clan had always been struggling to survive in the cracks of the Dark Domain. They were too weak.
The Great Ancestor he mentioned was the greatest Bug Master, and it was because he tamed a powerful Earth Nether Worm that he led the Bug Master race to a small glory. However, after the Great Ancestor left, the remaining Bug Masters were still not enough to gain a place in the Dark Realm, and had been declining over the years.
Come down.
"Dark Domain …"
According to Luo Zheng's understanding of the Paramita Realm, the Heavens were stacked together. When he left the Color Realm, Luo Zheng could verify this with his eyes.
However, Feng Ge opened a crack in the Heavens. Did that not mean that he opened a crack in the Fifteenth Heaven? The top of the Dark Domain should be the Fifteenth Heaven?
It did not make sense in terms of space …
Just as Luo Zheng was thinking, the white light in Feng Ge's pupils flashed slightly. He reminded Luo Zheng in a low voice, "Something is following us from above."
Hearing Feng Ge's words, Luo Zheng, Min Qi, and the others all turned their heads to look. How could they see anything in the darkness?
But soon, they heard the sound of flapping wings. Then, several huge black shadows came down from above. They were giant birds thousands of feet in size!
"It's the Ghost-winged Birds!"
Min Qi's eyes widened when he saw the huge black shadows above his head. His face was full of fear. Min Mou's face was also pale.
As one of the strongest Spirits of the Dark Domain in the Fourteenth Heaven, the Ghost-winged Birds had almost no natural enemies.
They floated in the sky all year round and almost never came down. No one had ever found the nest of the Ghost-winged Birds. Even a force like the King Monkey Clan would die if they encountered the Ghost-winged Birds!
Min Mou and Min Qi had seen the Ghost-winged Birds fly over the mountain valley. All the Bug Masters did not even dare to breathe loudly. After the Ghost-winged Birds captured a few of their clansmen, the other Bug Masters could only watch as the Ghost-winged Birds left!
That scene left a deep impression on Min Qi and Min Mou.
The two brothers did not expect that they would encounter such a powerful existence not long after they set off.
"Lower the altitude," Luo Zheng's voice sounded in Min Qi's ear.
Min Qi was originally at a loss. He did not know how to deal with it. After hearing Luo Zheng's reminder, he suppressed the fear in his heart and blew the bamboo flute in his hand. Those Sacred Beetles quickly lowered their altitude.
Luo Zheng's group descended from the sky, and the Ghost-winged Birds followed closely behind.
Their huge wings were like sails, covering the ground from above. As they got closer and closer, the faces of the Ghost-winged Birds gradually became clearer. Their slender heads were covered with brown feathers, their eyes were slanted, and their faces were extremely repulsive.
"Ah —"
The closest Ghost-winged Bird stretched out its slender beak and bit Min Qi.
Compared to the Ghost-winged Birds' huge bodies, Min Qi was no different from an insect.
"Feng Ge, drive them away," Luo Zheng shouted.
Feng Ge's Infinite Light was too dazzling in the Dark Domain. Luo Zheng asked Min Qi to lower the altitude, not to escape, but to make it easier for Feng Ge to drive these birds away.
"Buzz!"
As soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, Feng Ge raised his hand. Rays of light broke through the air and extended upwards for a distance of a hundred feet.
"Puff, puff, puff …"
The wings of the Ghost-winged Birds were like worn-out sails. They were instantly pierced by the rays of light, leaving holes in them.
Feng Ge was obviously not satisfied with this little damage. After he gently flipped his palm, the light cocoon cut towards the roots of the Ghost-winged Birds' wings. He actually intended to cut off the wings of the Ghost-winged Birds.
The huge Ghost-winged Birds felt the pain and wanted to escape.
However, the light cocoon released by Feng Ge was too fast. It couldn't avoid it at all. With a light slash, the wings of the Ghost-winged Birds were directly cut off. The huge body of the Ghost-winged Birds directly fell from the sky.
"Boom …"
When the other Ghost-winged Birds saw this scene, how would they dare to stay? Fearing that their wings would be cut off by the blazing white light sword, they spread their wings and flew high, disappearing into the darkness.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
On the back of the Sacred Beetles, Min Qi and Min Mou wiped the sweat from their foreheads and gasped for breath.
They thought they were dead for sure this time, but the Ghost-winged Birds that dominated the Dark Domain were still easily killed by the white light tip of the human woman.

Chapter 3207
To the west of the tenebrous domain, there was a pass between Chen Jue Mountain.
After passing through this pass, it was the territory of the King Monkey clan.
With the status of the Bug Masters, it was naturally impossible for them to pass through this pass.
When they were about ten miles away from the pass, Min Qi saw the Sacred Beetle land in a forest. At the same time, he cautiously said, "There should be many King Monkey garrisons at the pass of Chen Jue Mountain. I'll send out an eyelid worm to investigate."
As Min Qi spoke, he lightly patted the pocket at his waist. A worm the size of a finger flew out.
This worm was the size of a mosquito and as light as a speck of dust. It was difficult to detect.
The two Bug Masters were not good at fighting. They could only help Luo Zheng in this aspect. Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng gently waved his hand and said, "There's no need to investigate."
"No need?" Min Qi's eyes narrowed.
"Just follow me," Luo Zheng said as he nodded at Feng Ge.
He and Feng Ge could sense that the Shapeshifting Magic Block was already far away. This meant that the King Monkey's destination was very far away from this pass.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had been very careful to avoid alerting the enemy. After all, Luo Yan was still in the hands of the Black Monkeys.
However, they had just killed the team of Black Monkeys. The King Monkey would know sooner or later. Therefore, Luo Zheng was racing against time with the Black Monkeys. Naturally, he had no scruples now.
"There are probably dozens of King Monkey garrisons …"
Min Qi silently said in his heart. However, when he thought of how Feng Ge killed the Ghost-winged Bird, he forcefully swallowed his words.
Above the pass, a team of King Monkey patrolled back and forth.
This pass of Chen Jue Mountain was the only way to enter the King Monkey's territory. This place had always been heavily guarded. Recently, more people had been sent to this pass.
As for why more people were sent, the higher-ups did not say in detail. However, the leader of the King Monkey guarding the pass was faintly uneasy.
For more people to be sent for no reason, there must be trouble …
Were the One-eyed Beasts planning to cause trouble again?
A few years ago, the King Monkey had taught the One-eyed Clan a harsh lesson. Now, the King Monkey was one of the top existences in the Fourteenth Heaven. It was impossible for those One-eyed Clan to come here now.
As for the Pure One, she was well taken care of by the King Monkey.
"Boss! Something's coming! "A King Monkey interrupted the leader's thoughts.
"What?"
The King Monkey leaped up from the house at the bottom of the pass. It climbed to the top of the pass and looked ahead.
"Sacred Beetles?" The King Monkey was surprised to see the Sacred Beetles in the air.
Aren't these Sacred Beetles a rare treasure of the Insect Master Clan?
Why did those Bug Masters come to the pass? Had he gone mad?
The King Monkey's eyes narrowed and a trace of anger appeared on its face. It roared, "The green-skinned people who caught the beetles, if they come any closer to the pass, kill them without mercy!"
In the hearts of the King Monkey, Bug Masters were cowardly by nature. In this kind of situation, a simple threat would be enough.
However, after the King Monkey's roar, the two Bug Masters did not turn back. Instead, they led the Sacred Beetles closer. This seemed a little strange.
"Courting death!"
The King Monkey gave a look to the two subordinates beside it. The two subordinates gently pulled out two black javelins from their backs.
Min Qi and Min Mou saw the King Monkey's actions at the pass. They swallowed their saliva and suppressed the urge to move forward.
Just as the two subordinates were about to throw the javelins, a whistle suddenly sounded from the side. This sound immediately attracted the attention of all the King Monkey.
The King Monkey looked over and saw Luo Zheng's figure hanging from the wall. Its monkey-face was slightly stunned. "A human? Why would a human come to the hidden realm? And this person seems to have a physical body? "
When it saw Luo Zheng, the King Monkey's heart skipped a beat. It recalled that a few days ago, a team of King Monkeys had escorted the Yang souls of two human girls through the pass. That team was the best of the best in the King Monkey Clan.
At the time, it had been somewhat puzzled. Could it have something to do with this human?
This thought only lasted for a moment before it made a decision. "Trespassers, kill!"
The two subordinates raised their arms and threw two javelins at Luo Zheng.
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
Although the javelins were extremely fast, Luo Zheng's body twisted slightly and he easily dodged them. The javelins were firmly nailed to the mountain wall.
He flipped over and sat on the javelins. At the same time, he asked, "Have you confirmed? Are there no other black monkeys around? "
When the King Monkey heard the insulting name "black monkey", their faces darkened. At the same time, they wondered who Luo Zheng was talking to. Just as they were looking around, a voice leisurely said, "It's confirmed. All the black monkeys are here."
"Then let's do it," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Above the heads of the black monkeys above the pass, beams of light descended and enveloped the black monkeys.
With the power of the Great Brahma's Boundless Heavenly Light, the black monkeys were wiped out completely in the blink of an eye. Even the ground beneath their feet was melted into a circular hole. The white light entered the passageway and eliminated all the black monkeys inside!
The only black monkey that survived in the entire Chenjue Pass was the King Monkey who gave the orders.
The King Monkey was responsible for guarding the Chenjue Pass and was a veteran of hundreds of battles. When it saw the white light descending from the sky, it only had one thought in its mind. The Pure Witch had made her move!
But the Pure Witch had attacked the King Monkey? There was no reason for this …
While it was confused, it nimbly turned over and was about to escape below the pass.
Luo Zheng had spared its life for the sake of obtaining information. He was already prepared.
Seeing the King Monkey jump up, Luo Zheng pulled out a javelin and swooped down from the mountain wall. At the same time, he turned around and hit the King Monkey with the stick at the end of the javelin. Then, he forcefully pushed it up and smashed it above the pass.
"I want to ask about something."
After jumping over the pass, Luo Zheng gently inserted the javelin beside the King Monkey and asked, "As far as I know, you have bound the Yang Soul of a woman from the Human Tribe?"
Hearing this question, the King Monkey's eyes suddenly flashed. Sure enough, it was because of this …
"I just …" Just as the King Monkey was about to answer, dozens of thin light cones descended from above and nailed to its feet, legs, shoulders, wrists, chest and other parts.
On the other side of the city wall, the figure of another woman from the Human Tribe emerged. Thin light cones were still jumping in her hands.
"The pure witch's light was released by this woman …" The corner of Wang Hou's eyes kept twitching. After being restricted by these light cones, it would be cut into pieces by the light cones as long as it moved a little.
"Tell me the truth and I can spare your life. Otherwise, you will end up like them," Luo Zheng said, pointing to the void.

Chapter 3208
The King Monkey looked as if it had seen a ghost.
It had no other choice now. Besides, no one had asked it to keep that matter a secret.
In order to survive, it did not hesitate for too long and said, "I'll tell you, I'll tell you … but you have to promise not to take my life!"
"I promise you," Luo Zheng said.
"She … she also promised me?" The King Monkey pointed at Feng Ge.
Feng Ge nodded expressionlessly. The King Monkey said, "A few days ago, there were humans who passed, and it was not just one human woman who passed, but two. These two human women were escorted back by our King Monkey Clan's Hanjiang Guards."
"What are the Hanjiang Guards?" Luo Zheng asked.
"They are the elites of our King Monkey Clan, the team that guards the King Monkey." The King Monkey continued, "The King Monkey is the commander of our Fourteenth Heaven. At that time, I also found it strange that they would bring two human Yang souls in …"
The King Monkey spilled the beans like a bamboo tube and told everything it knew. It even asked Luo Zheng to take out a map from its pocket and point out the location of the Hanjiang Guards.
"Min Qi," Luo Zheng called out.
Min Qi slowly flew up on the Sacred Beetle and cupped his hands at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng said without turning his head, "Kill it."
"You, you promised me …" The King Monkey's eyes widened.
"But he didn't promise you," Luo Zheng said expressionlessly. Then, he jumped down from the pass with Feng Ge.
Min Qi had never thought that he would be able to take the life of a King Monkey with his own hands, but since Luo Zheng had given the order, he would naturally do as he was told.
Although the King Monkey was powerful, the King Monkey leader's hands and feet were bound by the cone of light. He could not resist at all and was at his mercy.
After thinking for a while, Min Qi took out a few black bugs from his waist pocket. When the King Monkey saw these bugs, his face immediately showed a look of despair.
Those were Blood-Devouring Bugs. These bugs would drill into the host's body and reproduce crazily, hollowing out the host's body …
…
…
After passing through the Chenjue Pass, the surrounding terrain gradually opened up.
Even in the dark, he could see a cluster of low houses in the distance. That was the city where the King Monkey lived.
Luo Zheng raised the javelin and looked into the distance, comparing the terrain in the distance with the terrain in his hand. Then he pointed at the map and said, "The Hanjiang Guards that Min Qi mentioned are here, which is in that direction!"
Feng Ge's eyes swept over and he nodded. "It's in the same direction as the Shapeshifting Magic Block, but … how do we sneak in?"
Luo Zheng's purpose was to save people. The best result was to find Luo Yan without alerting the King Monkey and rescue Luo Yan. There could not be any accidents during the whole process. Once the enemy took Luo Yan as a hostage, Luo Zheng would have to hold back.
"Now we can use these …" A few small eyeliner bugs danced in Min Qi's hand.
Luo Zheng smiled and nodded at Min Qi.
Min Qi gently threw the eyeliner bugs. These little bugs instantly disappeared in the darkness and flew toward the city where the King Monkey lived.
About 30 minutes later, Min Qi could finally see the situation in the city through the eyeliner bugs. Soon, a puzzled expression appeared on his face. At the same time, he said, "Nothing …"
"What do you mean nothing?" Luo Zheng asked.
"There's not a single King Monkey. This city is empty!" Min Qi said with certainty.
"How is that possible?" Luo Zheng asked seriously. "Did you see wrongly?"
"The eyeliner bugs are my eyes. How can I see wrongly?" Min Qi's eyes were fixed in front of him as he answered with certainty.
"Let's go take a look." Luo Zheng frowned. His figure flashed and disappeared into the darkness. Feng Ge followed closely behind him.
After the two entered the city, they looked around and looked at each other. Their faces were indescribably strange.
Such a big city was really empty!
"The King Monkey guarding Chenjue Pass doesn't seem to know the situation in the city …" Luo Zheng said with a frown.
Feng Ge frowned, then said, "Let's find the Shapeshifting Magic Block first."
The two went straight to the north side of the city. They searched according to the soul imprint. Not long after, the two stopped in front of a spacious building.
Luo Zheng stepped forward and gently pushed open the door of the building!
"Creak …"
The moment the door was opened, countless sounds of something breaking through the air could be heard.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Countless fine needles shot out from the inside.
Feng Ge raised his eyebrows. The light in his hand shot out!
"Weng!"
Under the blazing white light, the thin needles were completely eliminated. When the light poured into the building, the Shapeshifting Magic Block's screams came from inside. "Don't, don't hurt me. I'm the Shapeshifting Magic Block …"
Feng Ge didn't care at all. The light in his hand shot out continuously, firmly protecting her and Luo Zheng.
When the sound of the thin needles completely stopped, Feng Ge stopped. The two finally saw the situation inside the building.
The Shapeshifting Magic Block had already returned to its original appearance. It hung upside down in the air like a piece of jerky.
Luo Zheng suddenly threw the javelin, cutting the rope above the Shapeshifting Magic Block. He then asked, "What happened? Why are you hanging here? "
"When I was sent to this city, I was already discovered …" the Shapeshifting Magic Block said with an aggrieved expression.
Although the Shapeshifting Magic Block was adept at transforming, it was destined to be a pet. Naturally, it wasn't strong enough.
After being beaten up by the King Monkeys, the Shapeshifting Magic Block was hung upside down here.
"They didn't kill me because they used me as bait to lure you here," the Shapeshifting Magic Block said.
Luo Zheng nodded. He didn't need the Shapeshifting Magic Block to explain this. Luo Zheng could understand. It was just a simple trap. "But why did all the King Monkeys disappear?"
"They said you were some Pure Ones and couldn't defeat them. They also told me that if you didn't die and wanted to find that human girl's Yang Soul, you can go there to find them." The Shapeshifting Magic Block pointed at the wall with a corner.
There was a temporary map on the wall. The map clearly marked a location.
After seeing the map, Luo Zheng let out a long breath. At the same time, he smiled at Feng Ge and said, "Earlier, when you erased everything within a hundred miles of the Hidden Realm, you probably scared the King Monkeys. They knew they couldn't defeat you, so they retreated first!"
With Feng Ge's strength, it wouldn't be difficult for him to wipe out the entire King Monkey race in a fit of rage. At the very least, it wouldn't be difficult for Feng Ge to wipe out the city where the King Monkeys lived.
"So what if they retreated? They can't hide forever," Feng Ge said expressionlessly.
"But there's a creature in the Hidden Realm that doesn't fear the Infinite Light," Luo Zheng said.

Chapter 3209
North of the Dark Domain, at the foot of the You Ming Mountain, was a vast black swamp.
The tranquil pond did not fluctuate at all throughout the year. Occasionally, a bubble would rise and burst, emitting a deadly poisonous mist from within.
Unless it was necessary, no creature of the Dark Domain would be willing to approach this black swamp.
"Da da da …"
More than ten horses were galloping in the black swamp.
To be able to move at such a speed, they must be very familiar with the black swamp.
The ones sitting on the horses were the elites of the King Monkey Clan, the Hanjiang Guards. The one leading the Hanjiang Guards was the commander of the King Monkey Clan, the Big Flag Monkey.
"Hiss hiss …"
Just as this group of black monkeys was galloping, a small black snake suddenly rushed out of the swamp beside them. At the same time, the small snake jumped out and bit at the leading Big Flag Monkey.
"Swish!"
The Big Flag Monkey's reaction was extremely sharp. With a casual wave of his hand, the small black snake was cut into several pieces.
He grabbed the small snake's head in his hand and identified it. He frowned and said, "It's a Single Reed Snake! Everyone, be careful! "
The black swamp was originally a highly poisonous place. The poisonous creatures that could survive in it were definitely not to be underestimated.
After the Big Flag Monkey reminded them, he pulled the horse's mane and led the team forward again.
In the middle of this team was a horse carrying Luo Yan and Ling Yun.
With the strength of an ordinary Yang Soul, it was difficult to even fight against an ordinary Nirvana Realm creature, not to mention that the Dark Domain creatures were stronger than them. They had no room to resist at all.
"I only know that the Dark Domain is boundless darkness. I didn't expect there to be an intricate and complicated force …" Ling Yun sized up the black swamp.
Luo Yan frowned and said, "I wonder why they brought us here from so far away?"
She had nothing to do with the Star Falcon Clan, so why did she choose to make a move now?
Along the way, Luo Yan and Ling Yun tried to get an answer from these black monkeys, but these black monkeys treated them as air and did not pay any attention to them.
"Ga —"
At this moment, an extremely ear-piercing cry suddenly sounded.
Luo Yan, Ling Yun, and the black monkeys all looked up.
Several huge birds swooped down from the sky. Clearly, they had taken these King Monkeys as their targets.
"Commander! It's a Ghost-winged Bird! "A King Monkey calmly reminded.
The Hanjiang Guards were the strongest group of King Monkeys. Whether it was in terms of temperament or strength, they far surpassed the average King Monkey.
If a normal King Monkey had seen the Ghost-Winged Bird, they would have panicked.
"These bastards … they actually came to join in on the fun at such a crucial time!" A cold expression appeared on the big flag monkey's face. At the same time, he ordered, "Scatter!"
"But this is the Black Marsh …" A King Monkey hesitated.
Although the Ghost-Winged Birds were dangerous, with so many King Monkey scattered in the Black Marsh, it was no different from seeking death.
"It's too late. Those Ghost Wing Birds are too fast!" Another King Monkey said loudly.
If it was outside of the Black Swamp, the King Monkeys would have a way to deal with the Ghost Wing Birds. However, in the Black Swamp, all creatures would have their hands and feet tied, except for the Ghost Wing Birds that could fly in the air.
"Then I'll bring up the rear. You go first!"
The big flag monkey suddenly reined in the horse. The armor on its arms began to shine with golden light, and it began to accumulate strength.
This time, it was an order from the higher-ups to lead the human woman named "Luo Yan" into the Dark Zone and send her to the side of the Pure Witch. The big flag monkey had to complete it even at the cost of its own life.
"Ga ga ga …"
When those huge Ghost-winged Birds swooped down at high speed, the Black Marsh in the distance suddenly shook. A slender snake head rolled out of nowhere. With a "whoosh", the slender snake head crossed a distance of thousands of feet and accurately rolled up a Ghost-winged Bird!
The huge Ghost-winged Bird didn't even have time to resist. It was dragged away by the tongue at an incredible speed and disappeared into the depths of the marsh.
"Ga ga ga ga ga …"
Seeing this scene, the other Ghost-winged Birds screamed nervously and spread their wings to escape.
However, the tongue that was originally rolled back bounced back at an extremely fast speed again and instantly rolled up another Ghost-winged Bird …
"Da!"
"Da!"
"Da!"
In just a few breaths, seven or eight Ghost-winged Birds floating in the sky were rolled up by the tongue.
Ling Yun and Luo Yan were completely stunned when they saw this scene.
In their eyes, these King Monkeys were extremely powerful creatures in the Dark Zone. They didn't expect that they also had such a natural enemy as the Ghost-winged Birds. When Ling Yun was still rejoicing that the Ghost-winged Birds couldn't leave the Dark Zone, a big tongue swept over and swallowed them all.
What kind of existence was the owner of this tongue?
Although Ling Yun and Luo Yan were extremely pessimistic about their fate, they still couldn't help but sigh at this scene. They really didn't know what secrets were hidden in the Nirvana Realm.
"It's the Master of Heaven!"
"The Master of Heaven has taken action!"
"That was close …"
The King Monkeys were also excited when they saw the big tongue.
The big flag monkey rode his horse to the front of the group and ordered, "Take out the tribute Darkflybugs!"
The Master of Heaven of the Fourteenth Heaven didn't belong to any faction. The King Monkey race had tried all these years to mend their relationship with this "Master of Heaven". However, the Master of Heaven's temper was extremely irritable. Over the years, the Master of Heaven had swallowed many King Monkeys as tribute.
Soon, the King Monkeys dismounted from their horses and raised a metal plate over their heads. Above the metal plate, there was a curled up Darkflybugs. There were a total of six of them.
"Slow down a bit!"
"Don't show any hostility. The Master of Heaven is very sensitive!"
"Slow down a bit more!"
The big flag monkey reminded the King Monkeys of their movements.
The King Monkeys carried the metal plates and moved step by step. If their movements were any bigger, they would end up like the Darkflybugs above their heads and become food for the Master of Heaven.
It was only a few li, but the King Monkeys took two incense sticks' worth of time to travel.
When they reached the edge of the Black Marsh, the tongue shot out again and wrapped around one of the King Monkeys. Even the King Monkey and the Darkflybugs above its head were dragged into the darkness!
The other King Monkeys were shocked by this sight, but as Cold River Guards, they had all trained themselves to be fearless of death. Not a single one of them thought about throwing away the Darkflybugs and fleeing.
"Your Excellency Master of Heaven!"
The big flag monkey hurriedly walked to the edge of the Black Marsh and cupped his hands respectfully.
The Master of Heaven had already tasted the delicacies of the Darkflybugs, so he probably wouldn't attack them again.
Glug, glug, glug …

Chapter 3210
When Luo Yan and Ling Yun saw the Heaven Master's true appearance, they both frowned, an ominous premonition creeping into their hearts.
These black monkeys had brought them in front of this fellow, so they definitely had a goal …
This was not the first time the banner monkey had seen the Heaven Master. It knew that the Heaven Master could not refuse the Darkflybug Insects, but the Darkflybug Insects were notoriously difficult to catch in the Hidden Realm.
Whether it was the Insect Master race or the Earth Sniffer race, the 'candles' used to catch the Darkflybug Insects were all provided by the King Monkey race.
They had persisted for so many years in order to use this' Heaven Master 'at a crucial moment.
"Your Excellency Heaven Master, this one has worked hard to collect these Darkflybug Insects. Your Excellency Heaven Master can eat them without worry," the banner monkey respectfully said.
"Hmph, tell me, what … is your goal? And what's the deal with those two Yang Souls? " The Heaven Master's gaze fell on Ling Yun and Luo Yan. As a Pure One, he could instantly sense that Luo Yan and Ling Yun came from the Light Realm.
The banner monkey indifferently glanced at the two women and then replied, "These two are also consecrated by the King Monkey race to Your Excellency Heaven Master."
"Heh," the Heaven Master sneered, "You want me to devour their Yang Souls? That's better than nothing … "
As he spoke, he stuck out his tongue at Luo Yan and Ling Yun. Luo Yan and Ling Yun also closed their eyes at this moment.
But the banner monkey was startled by this scene and immediately blocked the Heaven Master's path. "Your Excellency Heaven Master, please wait!"
"What?" A dangerous glint appeared in the Heaven Master's eyes. He hated it when others obstructed him from foraging. If not for the fact that the banner monkey had repeatedly consecrated the Darkflybug Insects, he would have already swallowed him.
"I consecrated these two Yang Souls to Your Excellency Heaven Master, not for Your Excellency Heaven Master to eat," the banner monkey explained.
The Heaven Master looked at the banner monkey strangely and asked, "Then what do you want to do?"
"These two are only bait. They will attract a Pure One of the same status as Your Excellency Heaven Master. I believe that Your Excellency Heaven Master will be more interested!" the banner monkey continued.
"A Pure One …"
As a Pure, the Heavenly Master naturally understood the rules of the Pure.
When it became a Pure One, it would be bound to the first layer of heaven, and no second Pure One would be born in the fourteenth layer of heaven.
"You know the price of deceiving me." The Heavenly Master rolled his tongue, and the killing intent in his eyes seemed to condense into a tangible mist.
As the commander of the King Monkey Clan, the flag monkey knew that he could not show the slightest bit of panic at this moment. He said with confidence, "I dare not deceive Lord God. The other party came from the 13th Heaven. We don't know how they crossed the Desire Realm, but just the day before yesterday, that Pure Witch.
Unleash the boundless heavenly light and erase everything within a radius of a hundred miles! "
The Heaven Lord stared at the big flag monkey. Seeing how confident it was, a strange look appeared in his eyes. At the same time, he said faintly, "You guys can't deal with the Pure Witch, so you want me to deal with her?"
Although the Heavenly Master rarely walked outside, he was not stupid. The King Monkey clearly wanted to kill someone with a borrowed knife.
The big flag monkey replied without a change in expression, "I thought Lord God would be interested in the other Pure Witch, and … we really can't deal with her. If Lord God is unwilling to face that Pure Witch, I can take these two Yang Souls away."
The big flag monkey was very sure that the Heavenly Master would definitely not be willing to let that Pure Witch go.
"Since they've already been sent here, then leave them here," the Heavenly Master said.
The big flag monkey sneered in his heart, then turned his head and said, "Take the two human Yang Souls down and seal them here!"
After the King Monkeys put down the Shadow Beetles, they went forward and escorted Luo Yan and Ling Yun down. Another King Monkey drew a trap array on the ground. Luo Yan and Ling Yun were pushed into the trap array by the King Monkeys.
"Lord God, please enjoy the food." The big flag monkey bowed with a polite smile on his face, then led the King Monkeys away quickly.
The big flag monkey did not care about the strength of the two Pure Witches. It was only afraid that if the Pure Witches fought, it would really destroy the world. It was not worth it to get involved and lose its life.
Luo Yan and Ling Yun sat in the trap array, silently looking at each other.
From the King Monkey's words, they understood that the two of them were not important. They were just bait to lure the other Pure Witch over.
"Who is that Pure Witch?" Ling Yun asked in a low voice.
Luo Yan also shook his head in confusion.
The news that Feng Ge was a Pure Witch had only spread within a small circle. The people of Li Mountain did not know, so how could the two of them understand?
"Could it be your brother?" Ling Yun suddenly asked.
In view of Luo Zheng's level of performance, it was not strange for anything to happen to him. In order to lure him over, it was also reasonable for Luo Yan to be kidnapped and brought to the Dark Domain.
"Impossible …" Luo Yan subconsciously shook his head.
Although they were in danger, she still did not want her brother to be involved in the Dark Domain.
Swish!
Just as the two women were whispering to each other, the big tongue suddenly rolled up, sucking a Darkbug near them into its mouth. After chewing for a while, it swallowed, revealing a satisfied expression.
This scene made Ling Yun and Luo Yan tremble with fear, but under the trap array, they simply could not escape.
In a short while, the God Lord had swallowed all the Darkbugs into his stomach. He lay on the side of the swamp, staring at the two women with his huge eyes, at the same time revealing a strong interest. "The Fruit of Darkness on your bodies can only last for about six hours. It seems that those monkeys believe that.
Will the Pure Witch arrive within six hours? "
"I'm not sure," Ling Yun coldly replied.
The irascible God Lord showed patience and no longer asked any other questions. Instead, he quietly waited.
Half an hour later …
The God Lord's eyes darted around, and then he suddenly raised his head from the swamp and looked into the distance, a sinister smile in his eyes.
Without any warning, he opened his mouth and swept his tongue into the distance.
Luo Yan and Ling Yun both turned their heads and looked into the distance, but it was too far away for them to see what was in the distance.
Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …
The tongue swept out, then retracted, then swept out again, then retracted again ….
After several attempts, it was all for naught. Not only that, but there were also bloodstains on its tongue. It had been wounded by a sharp weapon.
Luo Yan and Ling Yun were also perplexed.
Just who were these people? How could they be so formidable that even a First Heaven Heaven Lord was helpless against them, and their tongue had even been wounded by them?

Chapter 3211
The Heavenly Master's attacks failed several times. Instead, his snake head was injured by the two in the distance.
That head floating above the Black Swamp abruptly rose. More than half of his body crawled out of the Black Swamp.
Only then did the enormous figure of the Heavenly Master appear in front of Luo Yan and Ling Yun.
"Those monkeys are right. The newcomers do have some skills." The Heavenly Master lowered his head and asked: "They are here to save the two of you, right?"
Luo Yan wanted to deny it. But in this situation, her attitude was meaningless.
The Heavenly Master extended his enormous claws and dug towards Luo Yan and Ling Yun.
The two women thought that the Heavenly Master was about to kill them, so they closed their eyes and waited for death to claim them. However, when the claw reached them, it suddenly opened up and grabbed the entire trapping array, as well as the soil beneath their feet. With a light toss, it placed the two women on its back.
"It … doesn't intend to kill us?" Ling Yun was puzzled.
Luo Yan looked into the distance and said: "We don't even have any value in being killed by it. Its interest should be flying over there!"
Two figures were flying over from the distant Black Swamp. Their speed was extraordinarily fast.
One of the figures was blurry. One could not see the original appearance of the figure. And the other figure was Feng Ge of the Great One Heaven Palace!
In the Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Yan and Ling Yun had seen her before.
Feng Ge's performance in the Chaotic Origin Great World was not outstanding. But now, she could enter the Paramita World. Furthermore, she could see her appearance clearly!
"Could she be that Pure Witch?" Ling Yun blurted out.
"She should be. I wonder who the other person is …" Luo Yan softly said.
When they were in the Chaotic Origin Great World, Feng Ge had been following behind her big brother. It seemed that it was highly possible that her big brother had entered the Dark Domain.
As the distant figure got closer, Luo Yan focused her gaze. That figure and movement were indeed 70-80% similar to her big brother.
"Ge ge ge …"
When the two figures got closer, the Heavenly Master crawled on the ground and let out a series of muffled laughter.
This laughter was like muffled thunder that reverberated throughout the Black Swamp. The poisonous creatures hiding in the Black Swamp panicked and fled in all directions.
Luo Zheng frowned when he saw the huge monster in the distance.
The Pure Witch of the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven was actually a giant lizard?
After looking around, his gaze quickly landed on the back of this "large lizard". On its back, there were two human figures. Even though they were far away, Luo Zheng was certain that these two human figures were Yang Souls!
Yan 'er …
Luo Zheng's body emitted a trace of cold anger, and he sped up a bit.
Feng Ge, who was walking side by side with him, naturally felt Luo Zheng's abnormal reaction.
Obviously, Luo Zheng's concern for his sister was more important than anything else. After this small thought arose in Feng Ge's mind, she raised her eyebrows slightly.
Several breaths later, the two of them finally arrived in front of the Heaven Master.
"Kekeke …"
The Heavenly Master's muffled laughter continued for a long time before stopping. He pointed a claw at Feng Ge and said, "You are the Pure Witch those monkeys spoke of?"
Feng Ge coldly looked at the Heavenly Master, not saying a word.
Although the Heavenly Master's appearance was worlds apart from hers, Feng Ge could still feel a sense of familiarity from him. This familiarity reminded her that the Heavenly Master was the same as her.
Feng Ge was extremely displeased that such an ugly thing was considered her own kind.
"Answer me!"
The originally calm Heavenly Master suddenly became irritable.
In the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven, he was the ruler of this world. Neither the creatures of the Dark Domain nor the Light Domain dared to slight or disobey him.
But Feng Ge was completely ignoring him …
At this time, Luo Zheng stood in front of Feng Ge and pointed behind the Heavenly Master. "Those black monkeys tied up my sister and brought her here for the sole purpose of using someone else to do their dirty work. If Your Excellency does not wish to be used by the black monkeys, please release them."
The moment Luo Zheng spoke, Luo Yan's body trembled. Ling Yun was also pleasantly surprised. The one who had come was really Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng's figure was reflected in the Heavenly Master's eyes. That human girl was a Pure Witch, but this brat was not. The Heavenly Master found it hard to understand how someone who was not a Pure Witch had managed to injure his tongue.
"The black monkeys want to use someone else to do their dirty work," the Heavenly Master repeated Luo Zheng's words.
"That's right," Luo Zheng nodded. "Your Excellency, please do not be fooled by those monkeys."
"With just those ugly monkeys?" As the Heavenly Master spoke, he extended a claw and pointed at Feng Ge. "If you want me to release the two Yang souls behind me, I can do that as long as she stays. Do you agree to this condition?"
When Feng Ge heard this, a cold smile appeared on his face as he looked at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng directly asked, "And if I don't agree?"
"Then I'm afraid that all of you will have to stay behind," the Heavenly Master opened his mouth and spoke in a muffled voice. Right now, he was not only interested in Feng Ge, but also Luo Zheng.
The scales on his massive body suddenly unfurled, and a myriad of colors blossomed from the edges of his scales.
The light that this Pure Witch released at this moment was not limited to white, but a riot of colors.
The originally pitch-black and lifeless swamp suddenly seemed to come alive. It was like the first spring breeze, capable of changing the world with a single thought.
These rays of light could not harm Luo Yan, Lingyun, Luo Zheng, and Feng Ge, but to the other creatures in the swamp, they were fatal.
Whether it was the wriggling Withered Water Vines or the Watersoaked Grass that had taken root in the water, they all turned into nothingness the moment they came into contact with the rays of light …
What was even more frightening was that all the creatures in the Black Swamp seemed to have a unique attachment to the colorful rays of light that it emitted. The Rain Serpent that was more than ten thousand feet long and the Extreme Dark Crocodile that could stay in the Black Swamp for thousands of years seemed to be possessed at this moment as they rushed into the rays of light.
They were annihilated into wisps of energy and disappeared into the colorful rays of light.
They were actually sacrificing themselves!
"The Heavenly Master is indeed very powerful …"
Luo Zheng's hand that was holding the javelin tightened slightly.
Feng Ge had just become a Pure Witch not long ago, but his strength had already far surpassed the average Nirvana Realm creature. Even the Dark Domain creatures were not inferior to him.
The Heavenly Master before him was an old monster that had lived for who knew how many years. Its understanding of himself, the Nirvana Realm, and the Brahma Immeasurable Heavenly Light was not something that Feng Ge could compare to.
"Wuu …"
The Heavenly Master let out a roar as his sharp claws came crashing down on Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng had long since been on guard, so he naturally could not allow the Heavenly Master's attack to hit him. His figure flitted to the side like a ghost.
Boom!
When the claw slammed into the mud of the Black Swamp, the mud of the Black Swamp began to ripple and spread in all directions.

Chapter 3212
The javelin shrieked as it pierced through the darkness.
Thwish!
Caught off guard, the javelin nailed itself into the Heaven Lord's forehead.
This javelin was not weak. If it could pierce through, it would have blasted the Heaven Lord's head apart.
But this did not happen. The javelin had not even managed to pierce through the Heaven Lord's skin. Only the tip of the javelin had managed to pierce through.
Thwish!
While Luo Zheng was still in the air, the Heaven Lord swung his other claw at Luo Zheng and slapped Luo Zheng's body.
Bang!
With this heavy blow, Luo Zheng was directly smashed into the swamp.
Feng Ge's expression changed. She wanted to go forward to check, but before she could take a step, the Heavenly Master's other claw had already landed on her side. The Heavenly Master said, "I spent a long time in the Fourteenth Celestial Layer. I've devoured all the living beings here at least once, but the most delicious of them all is still the Dark Moth Bug. But you should taste pretty good too …"
Whoosh!
The claw swiped at Feng Ge.
Feng Ge reacted with extreme vigilance. Seeing the claw coming at her, she somersaulted backward and jumped out of the way.
Whoosh, whoosh …
The Heaven Lord's claw followed, continuing to swipe at Feng Ge. Meanwhile, Feng Ge maintained her nimble footwork, retreating like a dragonfly skimming the surface of the black swamp.
But this retreat was also extremely dangerous.
After Feng Ge became a Pure Witch, her strength had greatly improved by relying on this body. When she was at the Thirteenth Layer of Heaven, she could easily burst out the strength of more than three thousand Divine Jun, which was far more powerful than Luo Zheng.
Now that she had reached the Fourteenth Heaven, her physical body was still as powerful as ever, but she was now facing the Heaven Lord of the Fourteenth Heaven, who had existed for countless years. The gap between them was obvious.
Whoosh!
Whoosh!
Whoooah!
As Feng Ge retreated, she twisted and turned, only a few inches away from the Heaven Lord's claw.
"Let me see how far you can retreat!"
A hint of excitement appeared in the Heaven Master's eyes.
As the ruler of the Fourteenth Heaven, it had stood at the top of this layer for far too long.
In the entire Fourteenth Heaven, it seemed that there was nothing worthy of its attention and action …
Now that he had finally encountered an extraordinary existence, the Heavenly Master was naturally very excited.
As its body rushed forward, its other front claw joined in. As the two claws intersected, the sharp claws left behind colorful afterimages in the darkness.
Feng Ge clenched his teeth and dashed left and right in the afterimages.
"Pfff …"
A sharp claw flashed across Feng Ge's chest, and a deep gash appeared under her collarbone, revealing her bone.
Feng Ge's pupils constricted and she tried to flip to the other side, but the Heavenly Master had already guessed her direction and grabbed at her.
"It's over …"
Feng Ge's heart skipped a beat. He suddenly realized that if he was caught, his journey to the Nirvana Realm would probably come to an end.
She did not know why, but even in the face of death, she did not have any regrets.
From the Thirteenth Heaven to the Fourteenth Heaven, she had already gotten to know herself all over again.
But if she died, then Luo Zheng …
Before this thought could fully emerge from her mind, she saw that the claw had stopped a foot in front of her and was no longer moving forward.
Feng Ge blinked, feeling a little strange.
Not only was the Heavenly Master's claw not moving forward, it was actually slowly retreating. Meanwhile, a look of deep anger appeared on its lizard-like face.
As it retreated inch by inch, the Heavenly Master let out a low roar.
Only now did Feng Ge notice that Luo Zheng was tightly gripping the Heavenly Master's tail and pulling it back!
"Damn human …!"
The Heavenly Master roared and gave up on pursuing Feng Ge. Instead, he turned around and lunged at Luo Zheng.
Bang!
Mud splattered everywhere as the Heavenly Master tackled Luo Zheng.
"Big Brother!"
"Luo Zheng …!"
Luo Yan screamed from the back of the 'giant lizard', but there was nothing she could do. All she could do was worry helplessly.
Feng Ge, who had been retreating, revealed a trace of viciousness when she saw this scene. Her long legs stomped on the ground as she shot toward the Heavenly Master in a parabola.
But before Feng Ge could get close, the Heavenly Master's tail swept through the air.
Slap!
This light sweep of the tail contained a strength of four thousand divine jun.
Feng Ge let out a muffled groan as a fishy taste came from his throat. His entire body rolled onto the swamp. His entire body went limp and sore, and for a moment, he couldn't move at all.
The Heavenly Master ignored Feng Ge and continued to bombard Luo Zheng.
In this situation, Luo Zheng had no room to dodge. He could only lie on the ground and raise his arms to block.
"Hong!"
After the first strike, the light spots in Luo Zheng's body became active. They helped Luo Zheng to bear the weight of the Heavenly Master's strength.
But the Heavenly Master's strength was too strong. As the Chaotic Origin Spirit bore the weight of the attack, it actually showed signs of fatigue!
"Weng …"
At the same time, the golden light in Luo Zheng's body was released. That was the unknown True Dao Intent. This True Dao Intent rapidly cleansed the Chaotic Origin Spirit, restoring it back to its original state!
That pair of enormous eyes of the Heavenly Master flashed with a strange light.
He had never left the Fourteenth Heaven before. Naturally, he had never seen something like the 'Chaotic Origin Spirit'. He only thought that Luo Zheng's fleshy body was tough.
But no matter how tough something was, there was ultimately a limit!
The Heavenly Master did not believe in heresy. He sent out a second strike at Luo Zheng.
"Hong!"
Luo Zheng's Chaotic Origin Spirit that had just been cleansed by the True Dao Intent was once again on the brink of fatigue. But at the same time that Luo Zheng was bearing the weight of the attack, the True Dao Intent rapidly restored the Chaotic Origin Spirit …
After being struck twice by the Heavenly Master, Luo Zheng was not injured at all. That Chaotic Origin Spirit of his was still brimming with vitality. Luo Zheng revealed a strand of joy on his face.
Recently, when Luo Zheng was tempering the Chaotic Origin Spirit in his fleshy body, he had this conjecture in his heart.
When the Chaotic Origin Spirit reached a certain level of strength, the True Dao Intent in the fleshy body would form a cycle with the Chaotic Origin Spirit, greatly reducing the fatigue rate of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Hence, Luo Zheng's Chaotic Origin Spirit could withstand ten times, twenty times more attacks than others …
What if the Chaotic Origin Spirit continued growing stronger?
The True Dao Intent formed by the attacks Luo Zheng received could completely restore the Chaotic Origin Spirit to its original state. As long as he was not killed in a single strike, Luo Zheng's fleshy body was practically an undying existence!
And now, the situation was as such.
This Heavenly Master was indeed strong. But since he could not destroy the Chaotic Origin Spirit in a single strike, it meant that the Chaotic Origin Spirit would never become fatigued.
"Hong!"
"Hong!"
"Hong!"
Every time this Heavenly Master sent out a strike, the disbelief in his eyes would increase.

Chapter 3213
The Heaven Master was inwardly shocked, but the other three women felt very uncomfortable.
Luo Yan looked down at the black mud below, unable to distinguish Luo Zheng's figure.
Under such a bombardment, Luo Zheng would probably be crushed into a part of the mud …
Feng Ge struggled to climb up from the swamp. Her body was dirty from head to toe. She tried to move forward, but her body was seriously injured. It was very difficult for her to move even a single step.
Boom, boom, boom …
After the Heaven Master blasted more than ten times, Feng Ge's anxious face gradually revealed a strange expression.
In a situation where Luo Zheng is unable to resist, how can he withstand so many attacks from the Heaven Master?
Why is the Heaven Master still attacking endlessly …?
After the Heaven Master blasted more than twenty times, Luo Yan and Ling Yun also saw some clues.
They didn't know what was going on, but they also felt that this giant lizard's actions were somewhat abnormal.
There was a limit to the Heavenly Lord's strength, and after striking about thirty times with all his strength, he finally grew tired and took a step back, breathing heavily from his nostrils.
Feng Ge, Luo Yan, and Ling Yun all looked in the direction of Luo Zheng. The Heaven Master had smashed a huge hole in the ground, and the hole was already filled with black mud.
After a while, the mud gently rippled, and Luo Zheng's figure climbed out of it. He really looked unharmed!
"Big Brother, I'm fine …" Luo Yan's voice trembled.
Ling Yun looked as if she had seen a ghost. "This … isn't very possible, right?"
Luo Zheng being able to enter the Paramita Realm with his physical body was already an inconceivable miracle in Ling Yun's eyes. Miracles were miracles. Perhaps Luo Zheng had some special encounter. As the omnipotent big brother that Luo Yan spoke of, Ling Yun could accept it.
But facing the Heaven Master of the Fourteenth Heaven, he had withstood dozens of full-strength attacks and was completely unharmed?
Ling Yun felt that such an absurd situation could only occur in a dream. But how could she dream when her Yang Soul had entered the Paramita Realm?
The worry on Feng Ge's face was swept away and replaced with shock and joy! "
"You don't seem strong enough?" Luo Zheng raised his head and smiled at the Heavenly Master.
"You … how did you do it?" The Heaven Master stared at Luo Zheng in confusion.
It was certain that this human brat's body couldn't possibly be this strong. Something inexplicable seemed to have happened to him.
No matter how intelligent the Heavenly Lord was, it was impossible for him to guess something that was beyond the scope of his understanding. Moreover, no one had been able to discern the origin of Luo Zheng's True Meaning of the Dao. This strange cycle had been formed by a freak combination of factors.
"You don't need to know," Luo Zheng said. "If this is all you can do, then you've already lost."
As a Pure One, Feng Ge didn't have any other abilities other than being able to control the Great Brahma Infinite Heavenly Light at will and having a strong physical body.
Therefore, Luo Zheng speculated that this "giant lizard" might not have any other abilities.
Ever since this giant lizard had become the Heavenly Master, when had it ever been treated like this?
Whether it was its physical body, Yang Soul, or the beings that had taken root in the Fourteenth Heaven, it had always been treated with both respect and fear. It had long since gotten used to these reverent gazes.
Upon hearing Luo Zheng's mockery, it tilted its head back and roared.
White light blossomed from the bottom of its scales. As the scales refracted this light, it turned into a rainbow.
This light traveled an extremely great distance …
After handing over Luo Yan and Ling Yun to the Heavenly Master, the Great Banner Monkey immediately left the Black Marsh.
When the Heavenly Master began to roar, the King Monkeys had just left the edge of the Black Marsh.
At this moment, they suddenly saw a flash of light.
"That's the light of a Pure One …" one of the King Monkeys yelled.
"It hurts! Run, run! "Another King Monkey covered its face and turned to flee.
When the King Monkeys saw the light, it meant that the light had already reached their location.
Although this location was far away, the Great Brahma Boundless Heavenly Light was simply unreasonable. It swallowed everything in the Hidden Domain.
When the Great Banner Monkey realized that something was wrong, it rode its horse and rushed away. However, the demonic plants and demonic grass along the way were all swallowed up by the light. Then, the galloping horse and the King Monkey on the horse were also swallowed up by the light.
After this Great Banner Monkey was promoted to commander of the King Monkeys, it led the King Monkey Race to develop by leaps and bounds. It allowed the King Monkey Race to become the number one race in the Fourteenth Heaven. It even trampled its old enemy, the One-Eyed Race, under its feet.
In the end, this commander and the Cold River Guards that he had personally nurtured died in such an inexplicable manner.
On the other side …
The Bug Master Race's Min Qi and Min Mou were even further away from the Black Marsh.
The two of them had originally intended to follow Luo Zheng without any regard for their lives. Therefore, when Luo Zheng and Feng Ge entered the Black Marsh, the two of them also planned to enter. However, they were rejected by Luo Zheng, so the two of them waited outside the Black Marsh.
"It's light!"
"The light is coming!"
Min Qi and Min Mou's faces were filled with fear. Even the fully domesticated Sacred Beetle was trembling non-stop. The creatures of the Dark Domain all had an instinctive fear of the Brahma Infinite Heavenly Light.
However, the two Bug Masters didn't escape. After they turned around, they saw a huge pit not far away. The two of them didn't hesitate at all and directly jumped into the huge pit.
No matter how powerful the Brahma Infinite Heavenly Light was, it still had to melt the surface layer before it could reach them. Hiding in the pit allowed them to avoid disaster.
When the light gradually faded, the entire Black Marsh disappeared.
Along with the Black Marsh, thousands of snakes, insects, and ants disappeared along with it. There were even some Nirvana Realm races …
Luo Zheng, Feng Ge, Luo Yan, and Ling Yun were creatures of the Light Domain. The Brahma Infinite Heavenly Light didn't affect them in the slightest.
As a layer of the entire Black Marsh was melted away, the solid rocks below were exposed.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge stood on two huge rocks and looked up at the Heavenly Master.
A pair of wings of light grew out of the Heavenly Master's back. The white light twisted into patterns that covered his entire body, illuminating the surrounding sky.
"You know too little about the Pure Witches. I can show you what a Son of Light is," the Heavenly Master said with a dull smile.
As soon as he finished speaking, those twisted patterns gathered from around his body to his chest, forming a spiral pattern.
Boom!
A white light wave shot out from his chest and hit Luo Zheng's head.
Kacha, kacha, kacha …
Before the light wave hit Luo Zheng's body, just the pressure from it caused the rocks under Luo Zheng's feet and around him to crack.
"The power has increased by a lot!"
Luo Zheng's pupils constricted slightly, and the lightning wings on his back suddenly appeared.
If he used the lightning wings in the Dark Domain, the Dao essence of the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao would be directly devoured by the darkness. However, the darkness had been completely expelled by the Heavenly Master, so there was no problem in using it now.
Crackle!

Chapter 3214
The ball of light that contained a terrifying might fell straight down, and the shockwave turned into a visible circle that surged out in all directions.
The beam of light pierced through the sky, forming a pillar of light that pierced through the Darkness Domain from the bottom to the top!
Before this circle could spread, Luo Zheng grabbed Feng Ge and flew away, finally avoiding this attack.
Feng Ge clung to Luo Zheng, her eyes filled with surprise. "What kind of technique was that? How could it be so powerful?"
She and the Heaven Master were both Pure Ones, but she didn't seem to understand this technique at all. Although she could control the Boundless Heavenly Light as she wished, it had no power to speak of …
"If even you don't know, how could I?" Luo Zheng gripped Feng Ge tightly and bitterly smiled.
The Heaven Master coldly snorted after his attack failed. The wings of light on his back rippled, and his massive body flew toward Luo Zheng. At the same time, rays of light transformed into spiral patterns that converged on his chest.
"It's coming again!"
Feng Ge pointed to the sky and warned.
She had barely spoken when the light from the Heaven Master's chest gushed out once more.
Whoosh!
As this light descended, the entire space trembled as it headed straight for Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng didn't need to turn around to feel that terrifying pressure. He clenched his teeth, and the lightning wings on his back crackled. His body blurred as he sped up once more. The light once more descended behind Luo Zheng.
"Pop …"
The shockwave rapidly spread, and countless substances of the Darkness Domain were annihilated into nothingness.
Luo Zheng lifted Feng Ge in front of him and used his back to block the shockwave. He continued to move forward with the momentum of the shockwave, greatly increasing his speed.
The Heaven Master spread his wings of light and chased closely behind.
As the chase continued, the creatures of the Dark Realm along the way were in for a great misfortune.
Once that wave of light crashed down, the resulting shockwave would be enough to annihilate all living beings within a radius of five kilometers. Moreover, the Heavenly Master's wings of light were constantly releasing boundless heavenly light.
Wherever it swept across, the substances in the dark realm would be forcibly melted away. The pair of wings of light had forcibly plowed out a deep trench that was over two hundred feet wide and over thirty miles long in the dark realm, and it was still extending.
Luo Zheng was also under a lot of pressure as he faced the light waves crashing down from the sky.
The power of this light wave would most likely exceed the limits of Luo Zheng's fleshy body. His fleshy body wasn't an unparalleled existence. If the Spirit of Chaotic Origin could not recover in time, Luo Zheng would still be heavily injured and die!
"Watch out for the left!"
"Behind you on the right!"
Feng Ge reminded Luo Zheng attentively.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to fly to the left to avoid the wave of light behind him, the Heavenly Master suddenly flipped in the air and arrived in front of Luo Zheng. At the same time, he opened his mouth.
"Xiu –"
That thin and long tongue of his swept towards Luo Zheng at a speed that the naked eye could not capture.
Luo Zheng did not expect that the Heavenly Master would use his tongue to attack him. He was slightly startled and immediately threw Feng Ge away. Even if he was pulled into the mouth of the Heavenly Master, this Heavenly Master might not be able to eat him!
But the Heavenly Master did not intend to pull Luo Zheng into his mouth. The moment that tongue swept over, it was like a tight whip that ruthlessly lashed out at Luo Zheng.
"Peng!"
Caught off guard, Luo Zheng was sent flying backwards. And the path he took just happened to overlap with the wave of light behind him. This wave of light actually smashed onto Luo Zheng's body, enveloping Luo Zheng within it.
"Bo!"
The light wave blossomed and illuminated the horizon once again.
The Heavenly Master narrowed his eyes and stared at the rapidly spreading clump of light that was slowly descending. A trace of coldness appeared in his heart. He was extremely dissatisfied that he had to spend so much effort to deal with a human brat.
"Luo, Luo Zheng … there's no saving him now …" Ling Yun's face was contorted in distress.
Luo Yan did not say a word as he stared blankly at the ground below.
Feng Ge, who had just been thrown away by Luo Zheng, had just landed on the ground. Her beautiful eyes were incomparably grave, and her pupils flashed with intense worry.
The clump of light flickered for three or four breaths of time before gradually fading away.
A large pit had appeared where it had been, and at the bottom of the pit was a strange thing.
This thing was about the size of a human, but its appearance had undergone a complete transformation. Sharp spikes extended from its back, and at the bottom of the spikes was a thick tail.
"What is this thing? Where's Luo Zheng?" Ling Yun asked curiously.
"Where's my big brother …?" Luo Yan stared at the monster in confusion.
Even Feng Ge had never seen Luo Zheng in this form before. She blinked her eyes in confusion.
"Whoosh!"
While the Heavenly Master was still in shock, the monster's four thick limbs suddenly sprang up.
The Heavenly Master had already landed above the pit and was not far from the monster.
When the monster suddenly attacked, the Heavenly Master felt a sense of danger. Just as he was about to spread his wings and fly away, the monster stuck close to him and raised its tail.
"Pfff!"
The Heavenly Master's skin was thick and tough. Luo Zheng's javelins had not been able to break through its skin, but this monster's tail had instantly pierced through!
Panic finally appeared in the Heavenly Master's eyes. He swatted at the monster with one of his sharp claws, but the monster was extremely agile. After pulling out the tail, it leaped up once more and stabbed the black tail into the Heavenly Master's neck.
After this stab, the monster charged down the Heavenly Master's back. It swept past Luo Yan and Ling Yun, circled around the Heavenly Master's abdomen, and stabbed the black needle again.
After three stabs, the monster left the Heavenly Master's body and landed firmly on the ground.
Very soon, an extremely bizarre scene appeared on the Heavenly Master's body.
Three green dots of light began to spread from the Heavenly Master's right chest, neck, and abdomen.
The black leather armor had completely transformed into a green poison mist …
"What is this? What kind of poison is this …?"
The Heavenly Master's face contorted in shock as he watched his body transform.
He pushed the Boundless Heavenly Light to its maximum in an attempt to fill his body with energy.
But no matter how strong the Boundless Heavenly Light was, it could not help him expel this poison.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The Heavenly Lord tumbled through the air, jumping up and down as he let out a low roar.
This futile struggle lasted for more than ten breaths before it finally ran out of strength and fell to the ground. The pair of light wings and the white light circulating on the surface of its body quickly dimmed, and its aura disappeared completely in the blink of an eye.
The darkness that had been driven away gradually returned. Alas, before the darkness could completely engulf the Heavenly Master, Feng Ge waved his hand again. A white light descended from the sky, illuminating the surrounding several hundred zhang. The majority of this light gathered on the monster's body.
She walked up to the monster prostrating on the ground and cautiously asked, "You, are you Luo Zheng?"

Chapter 3215
The monster prostrating on the ground suddenly leaped up, its body standing upright. It looked somewhat similar to the Ghost Scorpion.
However, its appearance was even more arrogant and domineering, especially that thick scorpion tail. It glowed with a dark green light, as if it would take the life of anyone who approached it at any time.
Seeing the monster stand up, Feng Ge took several steps back.
This monster's figure was somewhat blurry, and he couldn't make out its appearance. It was exactly the same as when Luo Zheng's body entered the Paramita Realm, so it was very likely to be Luo Zheng himself. But Feng Ge was still somewhat wary and fearful.
"Is it because its appearance is too terrifying?" Luo Zheng's voice suddenly drifted out.
Upon hearing this familiar voice, Feng Ge heaved a sigh of relief and said reproachfully, "You should have spoken earlier. Why bother scaring people?"
"This is the first time I've seen such an expression on your face," Luo Zheng chuckled.
Feng Ge was both angry and embarrassed. He snorted and replied, "This is also the first time I've seen such an expression on your face!"
"Then it's fortunate that we're in the Paramita Realm. If we were in the Heavenly Palace, I might have really scared you."
As Luo Zheng spoke, he collected the bones and folded them back into Luo Zheng's body. The tail, the spikes on his back, and his broad arms all returned to their original state. Then, he rushed over to the Heavenly Master's side.
The Heavenly Master's vitality was severed inch by inch. His entire body was covered in a layer of green mud.
"Brother! Are you alright? "Luo Yan shouted from within the trap formation.
The trap formation beneath her feet isolated the outside world, so Luo Yan and Ling Yun were unaffected.
Luo Zheng nodded at his sister. The lightning wings on his back flashed, and he flew to the back of the Heavenly Master's corpse. He enveloped the entire trap formation in his arms and carried it down.
"Retreat," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Yan and Ling Yun retreated to the other side of the trap formation. Luo Zheng raised his hand and lightly chopped at the trap formation. The entire trap formation emitted an explosive sound, and then it collapsed.
Then Luo Zheng stepped forward and hugged his sister in his arms, but he kept apologizing, "I'm sorry, I dragged you into this for no reason …"
This time, Luo Yan had suffered an unexpected calamity. He was indeed unlucky to be kidnapped into the Dark Domain without anyone's knowledge.
Luo Yan pursed her lips, but comforted her in a soft voice, "It's not for no reason. Brother's enemy is my enemy."
Although the siblings had chosen different superpowers, they stood on the same side.
The accident this time was entirely due to the complexity of the Nirvana Realm.
"Speaking of which, I'm the one who was dragged into this for no reason. Luo Zheng, shouldn't you apologize to me?" Ling Yun said with a smile.
At that time, the Star Falcon Clan couldn't tell who was the Luo Yan, so they kidnapped both of them. Ling Yun could be considered unlucky.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to speak, Feng Ge's voice drifted over. "You're lucky that we rescued you. Apologizing to you is wishful thinking."
Luo Yan was Luo Zheng's biological sister. No matter what emotions she showed, Feng Ge pretended not to see Feng Ge. However, Ling Yun's words were mercilessly suppressed by Feng Ge.
Ling Yun was also a proud and arrogant person in Li Mountain. Her face stiffened when Feng Ge rebuked her so harshly. "How can you say that?"
"So what if I'm like this?" Feng Ge retorted.
"Feng Ge!"
Luo Zheng frowned and stopped Feng Ge.
Feng Ge didn't get angry, nor did he argue with Luo Zheng. He only smiled and retreated to the side, leaving Ling Yun to sulk.
Luo Zheng was just about to console Ling Yun when he suddenly felt a light in the corner of his eye. He looked over and saw a halo of light floating above the Heavenly Master's corpse. After the halo appeared, it circled in the air and floated directly toward Luo Zheng.
"What is that …" Ling Yun asked.
As soon as she finished speaking, the halo of light enveloped Luo Zheng with lightning speed.
Enveloped by the halo of light, Luo Zheng disappeared in front of Feng Ge and the others like a magic trick. Only the three girls were left standing in place, stunned, completely clueless as to what had just happened.
Luo Zheng also didn't expect such a situation to occur. The halo of light flashed, and he had no time to dodge. He felt the world spin, and when he opened his eyes again, all he could see was light. It was as if he had fallen into a world of light, and everything around him was a vast expanse of whiteness.
Aside from that, there was nothing else.
"Where is this?"
"Feng Ge?"
"Luo Yan?"
Luo Zheng called out a few times, but didn't get any response. It seemed that the halo of light had moved him to another place.
He squatted down and reached out to touch the ground for a while. What he touched was a cold and smooth floor.
"This isn't an illusion, but a real place …"
After coming to this conclusion, Luo Zheng began to walk forward. His footsteps suddenly echoed in the empty space.
After walking aimlessly in this snow-white world for a while, Luo Zheng felt his eyes adapt a little. Gradually, he could make out some white outlines.
