In the distance, white pillars gradually appeared. There seemed to be something between the pillars, but it was still very difficult for Luo Zheng to distinguish it. Even if Luo Zheng walked very close, he could only see a white expanse. He couldn't see anything.
"9527, Mr. Green Jade, where is this place?" At this time, Luo Zheng could only ask the two for help.
"I don't know," 9527's voice immediately came back.
When Luo Zheng used the Ancient God Bloodline, 9527 was already alarmed. As for this snow-white world, 9527 had never seen it before.
After being called by Luo Zheng, the Spirit of Green Jade observed for a while and asked in great surprise, "You didn't kill a Pure Witch, did you?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied affirmatively.
"Then I know," said the Spirit of Green Jade. "This is the Land of Inheritance, the Land of Inheritance of the Pure Witches."
"The Land of Inheritance?" Luo Zheng asked doubtfully.
"Well, when a Pure Witch dies, the killer will replace the Pure Witch and become the new Lord of Heaven," the Spirit of the Green Jade replied. "However, the Land of Inheritance is just the name of our Green Jade civilization. I have never been to this place, so I don't know what it should be called."
"The new Master of Heaven?" Luo Zheng was shocked and asked, "If I become the Master of Heaven, will I be bound to the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven?"
The Masters of Nirvana Realm would be fixed to a certain Layer of Heaven.
Among so many Masters of Heaven, Feng Ge was the only exception. She only encountered trouble when crossing the energy vortex.
But the situation of the Lust Realm and the Lust Realm was probably different. Luo Zheng's worry was justified.
"It is indeed possible. At least, none of the Masters of Heaven I have seen left that Layer of Heaven like Feng Ge," replied the Spirit of Green Jade truthfully.
Hearing this, a trace of caution appeared on Luo Zheng's face.

Chapter 3216
Luo Zheng wandered around this white world for a while, but he still could not distinguish anything apart from those pillars.
In desperation, Luo Zheng could only sit on the smooth floor and say with a wry smile, "I'm afraid that's not what I need to worry about now. It seems that leaving this place is a problem."
Both the Green Jade Spirit and 9527 remained silent. Under their observation, they could not even see those faint pillars clearly, so they could not provide Luo Zheng with any help.
There were a total of 19 Pure Witches in the entire Desire Realm. These Pure Witches rarely participated in the struggles of many large races. They were either independent individuals or chose to protect their own people in a corner. Even though the Green Jade Civilization once stood among the top ten civilizations, their understanding of the Pure Witches was still limited.
There was still not much.
However, Feng Ge was taking care of Luo Yan and Ling Yun in the Hidden Realm, so the creatures of the Hidden Realm should not dare to provoke them.
In fact, the abnormal behavior of the "Heaven Master" had already caused a great uproar in the Hidden Realm. Those creatures hiding in the Hidden Realm risked their lives to send people to investigate the whereabouts of the Heaven Master.
When these creatures of the Hidden Realm saw the corpse of the Heaven Master from afar, they were extremely shocked.
But under Feng Ge's Boundless Heavenly Light, those creatures of the Hidden Realm did not dare to approach at all.
In the vast white world, Luo Zheng was still sitting quietly in the same place.
Since the Green Jade Spirit said that this was the Land of Inheritance, there must be a method of inheritance …
He gently stretched out his hand and waved it a few times in the air, but did not feel the slightest abnormality. This Boundless Heavenly Light should also be a kind of energy, and judging from the light released by the big lizard, there were different levels of energy utilization. At least, Feng Ge could not release light waves of that level.
The process of Feng Ge becoming a Pure Witch was also very strange. She did not enter this Land of Inheritance. The one who entered the Land of Inheritance should be the pig-faced monster "Hu Yun". However, Hu Yun had just become a Pure Witch and was about to appear out of nowhere, but he had accidentally mixed with Feng Ge.
"If that pig-faced monster can do it, so can I. Since the Boundless Heavenly Light is a kind of energy, there must be a way to control it …" Luo Zheng sat cross-legged and focused on comprehending.
The time it takes for an incense stick to burn passed …
Ten incense sticks of time passed …
Two hours …
That whiteness was still like a piece of snow-white paper, unable to be seen through or touched.
Luo Zheng was not anxious at all. He calmly stared at the white screen in front of him.
After observing quietly for six hours, he finally noticed something different. The white picture seemed to have moved, as if the white clouds in the sky were rolling.
"Eh? The image moved? "
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows, and the corners of his mouth curled up into a smile. He turned his gaze to the pillars again, and the outlines of the pillars became deeper!
"Continue!"
Another four hours passed, and the number of times those rays of light rolled like white clouds increased.
The white light was no longer a static picture, but was constantly rolling. At the same time, Luo Zheng also noticed that there were some brighter spots in the white light, but Luo Zheng hadn't noticed them before.
These specks of light were like aimless bugs, floating around at a slow speed.
"Could it be that the key lies in these specks of light?"
When this thought appeared, Luo Zheng stood up and walked towards one of the specks of light. However, as soon as Luo Zheng took a step, the aura in the entire space became chaotic. All the specks of light fluctuated along with the aura, and moved away from Luo Zheng.
"These specks of light are almost weightless. With the slightest fluctuation of energy, they will float …" Luo Zheng thought for a moment, and with a thought, he took out the green feather of the Wing Monarch.
When Luo Zheng moved again, the effect on the surrounding space was minimized by the feather. To be on the safe side, Luo Zheng even used the True Meaning of Nature.
"Hu …"
When he approached the specks of light again, the specks of light still fluctuated slightly, but the amplitude of the fluctuation was very slight. At least, it was in a controllable state.
Luo Zheng walked towards the specks of light, and tried to merge them with his eyes.
When the specks of light merged into Luo Zheng's eyes, he suddenly felt that everything around him had become a bit clearer.
"Sure enough …"
If he wanted to see this world clearly, he had to merge these specks of light into his eyes.
After Luo Zheng had entered this world, he had remained motionless. There would always be specks of light that would inadvertently enter his eyes, allowing him to see more and more clearly.
However, this process was too slow, and absorbing the specks of light was completely based on luck.
Now that he had found the pattern, how could Luo Zheng be polite?
He walked forward with light steps, constantly absorbing the specks of light.
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, over a hundred specks of light had entered his eyes. The originally roiling white clouds gradually dimmed, and the outline of his surroundings became clearer. Luo Zheng discovered that he was standing in a white palace.
On the left side of the palace, there was a huge pillar, and in the center of the pillar, there was a white stone slab. Abstract pictures were drawn on the stone slab. Although these pictures were abstract, they could be clearly distinguished.
"This is a jellyfish?"
"This is a wolf …"
"This is a large lizard. Is it that God?"
After seeing this large lizard, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed, seeming to understand something.
He skimmed through the pictures in the pillar, counted, and then said, "There are a total of twenty stone slabs here. Are there twenty Pure Witches in the entire Desire Realm?"
"No, there are only nineteen Heavens in the Desire Realm. In theory, there are nineteen Pure Witches," the Green Jade Spirit said.
Although it couldn't see these pictures, it understood a little from Luo Zheng's muttering. At the same time, it added, "But don't forget, there is one more Pure Witch in the Desire Realm."
There were no Pure Witches in the thirteen Heavens, and Feng Ge was the first one.
After hearing the reminder of the Green Jade Spirit, Luo Zheng looked to the far side. The stone slab on the far side was actually a picture of a pig-faced monster, but the picture of this pig-faced monster was somewhat special. There was a human woman's head on its swollen body.
Seeing this picture, Luo Zheng couldn't help laughing.
Because of the possession, even this Land of Inheritance was a little chaotic.
After all, the Pure Witch herself was a pig-faced monster, and Feng Ge was just an occupant. He wondered what Feng Ge's expression would be when he saw this picture.
After scanning the "portraits" of these Pure Witches, Luo Zheng looked to the other side of the palace, and a trace of shock suddenly appeared on his face.
On that side, there was a huge stone slab, and on the stone slab was densely drawn Sanskrit.

Chapter 3217
There was a lot of information recorded in a single Sanskrit tablet. What was in front of him that required so many Sanskrit records?
The Spirit of Green Jade sensed Luo Zheng's emotional fluctuations and asked, "Luo Zheng, what did you see?"
"I saw a lot of Sanskrit!" Luo Zheng said excitedly.
"A lot?" The Spirit of Green Jade was also surprised.
It was also very interested in the Land of Inheritance of the Pure Witches. After all, it might involve the secrets of the higher level of the Instant Civilization.
"Yes," Luo Zheng glanced over, roughly estimated and replied, "There are about three or four hundred Sanskrit, and these Sanskrit are very unfamiliar. I'll let Nian …"
Now Luo Nian was getting more and more skilled at deciphering Sanskrit. Every time Luo Zheng saw a new Sanskrit, he subconsciously thought of Luo Nian.
But halfway through, Luo Zheng remembered that this was the Nirvana Realm. He could not throw Luo Nian here to decipher these Sanskrit. And because of the white light, he could not let Luo Nian "see" these Sanskrit. After all, even 9527 and the Spirit of Green Jade could not see these Sanskrit.
It was even more unrealistic to use stone rubbing to copy the Sanskrit. Luo Zheng could not bring those stone rubbing to the Nirvana Realm at all.
Just as the Spirit of Green Jade was thinking of a way, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly stared at the slate. The first Sanskrit tablet on the slate seemed to jump.
When the first Sanskrit tablet jumped, the second Sanskrit tablet also flipped fiercely, followed by the third and the fourth. This row of Sanskrit tablets began to flip one by one like rolling waves. Luo Zheng's eyes were quickly attracted by the changes of the Sanskrit tablets.
"I have an idea. You can give your eyesight to Luo Nian and let your eyesight replace his. Maybe it will work," the Spirit of Green Jade said while thinking. But Luo Zheng did not respond to the Spirit of Green Jade's words.
"Luo Zheng? Luo Zheng? "
After calling a few words, the Spirit of Green Jade naturally found that Luo Zheng was a little abnormal.
He stood there like a statue, staring blankly in front of him. He had completely fallen into some sort of trance.
In Luo Zheng's eyes, the Sanskrit characters on the stone slab had completely come to life. They were like little mice that were rotating. When they turned, they would release a trace of information, and as the Sanskrit characters turned one after another, they would continuously release information.
These messages were not "seen" by Luo Zheng, but the Sanskrit messages were automatically understood by Luo Zheng when he was reading them.
The Sanskrit that Luo Zheng had seen before required Luo Nian to decipher it before he could read the contents. However, the Sanskrit on the stone slab was different from any other time Luo Zheng had seen before. It could automatically release the hidden meaning.
When the Sanskrit characters reached the last one, they began to roll forward again. The angle of each Sanskrit character was different, and the information it released was also different.
Luo Zheng just stood there, looking at the Sanskrit words turning over and over …
Two hours later, the Sanskrit words on the stone slab finally stopped rolling.
"Luo Zheng!"
"Luo Zheng!"
When Luo Zheng heard the Spirit of Green Jade's voice from afar, he gradually regained consciousness and responded.
"What happened to you just now?" the Spirit of Green Jade asked.
Luo Zheng pointed to the stone slab with a strange expression and said, "I have already understood those Sanskrit characters."
"Understood? You can also decipher Sanskrit characters? "the Spirit of Green Jade asked in surprise.
He shook his head and told the Spirit of Green Jade what had happened on the stone slab. At the same time, he said, "The information released by these Sanskrit characters should be a kind of unique magic. When this information appeared, I seemed to have fallen into a very strange state."
This was the first time Luo Zheng had experienced this kind of cultivation method.
As he spoke, he flicked his hand. As his eyes flashed, a white light gathered at his fingertip, forming a dense ball of light. Just like Feng Ge, Luo Zheng could freely control the Boundless Brahma Heavenly Light.
As Luo Zheng flipped his hand again, this light was sucked into his palm, forming winding lines. At the same time, a spiral pattern appeared on Luo Zheng's palm.
"Whoosh …"
A small wave of light drilled out of this spiral. With a light flick of Luo Zheng's finger, this wave of light shot upward.
The power of this wave of light was far inferior to that of the giant lizard, but it was already in its embryonic form.
"So the wave of light that the giant lizard released was also learned from the stone slab. It is called the Brilliant Light Form. Feng Ge has never been to this world, so he naturally has never cultivated it, nor does he know how to use it."
Most importantly, the method of using the Boundless Brahma Heavenly Light was indescribable.
After observing the Sanskrit characters, Luo Zheng naturally grasped it. However, Luo Zheng couldn't express the way this magic operated. It was as if after observing the Sanskrit characters, it had naturally become his instinct!
"Then do you know how to leave this place?" the Spirit of Green Jade asked again.
"Leave …"
Luo Zheng's gaze swept across the palace. Apart from the pillars, there was no "door" on the surrounding walls. There was no exit. It was a completely sealed space.
However, with a slight thought, the magic embedded in the depths of his mind activated.
"Buzz …"
A small circle of light enveloped Luo Zheng's body, and he instantly disappeared into the circle of light.
In the next moment, Luo Zheng returned to the hidden domain.
Feng Ge, Luo Yan, and Ling Yun had been waiting in the hidden domain for several hours. At first, they were bored to death. However, the three women quickly surrounded Luo Zheng and chatted casually. Even the little bit of conflict between Ling Yun and Feng Ge disappeared.
Under the strong request of Feng Ge and Ling Yun, Luo Yan told them about Luo Zheng's childhood and even some of the things that happened in the Eastern Domain.
Feng Ge and Ling Yun, who grew up in the mother world, found it hard to understand.
Feng Ge was still okay. After all, she had also learned from Xun and the others. However, Ling Yun's face was full of disbelief.
After all, the people born in the mother world were strong. As long as their aptitude wasn't too bad, it wasn't difficult for them to become a True God.
However, Luo Zheng was considered the lowest level of living creature in the inner world. Ling Yun was naturally amazed that he could reach such a level. She thought it was unbelievable …
At this moment, a circle of light suddenly appeared beside them. Luo Zheng's figure also appeared from the circle of light.
"He really came back!" Luo Yan's eyes lit up when she saw her brother in the circle of light.
"What exactly is this circle of light? Why can it move you away?" Feng Ge asked.
"This circle of light, you will know once you enter it." Luo Zheng smiled and pointed at Feng Ge. He wanted to send Feng Ge into the palace of light. After she studied the Sanskrit on the stone slab, she would understand.
However, the circle of light didn't appear. Instead, the three women stared at Luo Zheng with a strange expression.

Chapter 3218
Pure Witches could not enter the Palace of Light infinitely. Otherwise, the Lord of Heaven could hide in the Palace of Light if he could not defeat Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had just mastered the Brahma Limitless Light and could not use the halo. He could only wait for the next opportunity.
Now that Luo Yan had been rescued, there was no need to stay in the Dark Realm.
When Luo Zheng and the others were about to leave, some of the creatures hidden in the Dark Realm surrounded them and knelt down in front of Luo Zheng, worshipping him very sincerely.
Pure Witches were called "Lords of Heaven". Not only did they have absolute strength, but they were also the spiritual symbol of many creatures.
When the giant lizard fell, it meant that there was a change of dynasty in the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven.
Luo Zheng and the others paused for a moment. If they continued to move forward, the white light would annihilate these creatures of the Dark Realm.
"Get up. You can disperse," Luo Zheng said.
When the creatures of the Dark Realm heard Luo Zheng's words, they got up one by one and bowed respectfully to Luo Zheng before retreating in all directions.
During this process, Luo Zheng thought of something and suddenly pointed to a Cyclops not far away.
Luo Zheng had seen this Cyclops Race in the Thirteenth Layer of Heaven. These Cyclops Race had even snatched the Weapon of Civilization. They seemed to be widely distributed in the Nirvana Realm, comparable to the King Monkey Race.
This Cyclops Race was the leader of that race.
A new Master of Heaven was not something that the Nirvana races were happy to see, but this was not something that they could decide. They had to re-understand and ponder over the temperament and preferences of the new Master of Heaven.
However, the previous Lord of Heaven was too violent. Moreover, after so many years of pleasing the King Monkey, the Lord of Heaven was inclined to help the King Monkey.
The birth of a new Lord of Heaven was a good thing for the Cyclops Race.
The leader of the Cyclops Race came forward and cupped his hands toward Luo Zheng. "What orders does the Lord of Heaven have?"
"I have some friends in the Dark Realm. Help me take care of them," Luo Zheng said lightly.
"May I know who this friend of yours is?" The Cyclops leader's eyes lit up. This was an excellent opportunity to curry favor with the Heavenly Master. No matter what Luo Zheng requested, the Cyclops would do their best to fulfill it.
"They are Insect Masters," Luo Zheng said.
"Bug Master …"
The one-eyed monster leader was stunned for a moment before realizing that the Bug Master tribe was a small, unknown tribe. Moreover, it belonged to the territory controlled by the King Monkey.
If it had been someone else's request, the Cyclops might have had to consider the King Monkey's attitude, but this was an order from God!
"This one will definitely arrange it properly!"
The one-eyed monster leader was overjoyed. Not only would he be able to do the God Master a favor, but he would also be able to suppress the King Monkey. This was a great opportunity for the one-eyed monsters.
After receiving the order, the one-eyed monster leader retreated.
Luo Zheng and his group headed west. When they reached the edge of the Dark Realm, Luo Zheng asked Feng Ge to take out the black box. At the same time, he released the heads of all the monsters in the black box. Naturally, this included the Shapeshifting Magic Block.
"Since we've reached the Dark Realm, I'll fulfill my promise and let you go," Luo Zheng said. These monsters had been imprisoned in the iron house for countless years. Now that they were finally free, they were very excited as they looked around. Some of the monsters even came from the upper echelons of the Nirvana Realm. It was just that their strength had been suppressed after coming down. As long as they were given enough time, they could still survive in the Dark Realm.
The wind blew and the water rose.
Soon, these monsters scattered in all directions. Only the Shapeshifting Magic Block did not leave.
"Why didn't you leave?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
The Shapeshifting Magic Block stood in place with a complicated expression in its eyes.
This Shapeshifting Magic Block had originally been a pet for a lifetime. It was not impossible for it to survive independently outside. However, it felt that following Luo Zheng was an opportunity. People who could open the iron house were destined to be extraordinary!
"I, I want to follow you," the Shapeshifting Magic Block suddenly said.
"Follow me?" Luo Zheng was stunned. However, he smiled and said, "It's not a problem to follow me. However, I won't stay in the Dark Realm most of the time. Usually, in the Light Realm, you'll have to stay in this small space."
Luo Zheng was naturally referring to the box that was emitting black light.
"Of course." The Shapeshifting Magic Block shook its body.
It had endured countless years in the iron cage. Staying in the black box made no difference to it.
The Shapeshifting Magic Block could transform into any creature. It could still come in handy at critical moments.
Luo Zheng let it enter the black box. After putting it into Feng Ge's Sumeru Ring, everyone stepped out of the Dark Realm.
…
Min Qi and Min Mou waited for a long time before they dared to climb out of the cave. The battle between the Pure Witches was beyond their scope of participation.
When Luo Zheng did not appear for a long time, the two Bug Masters could only order their Sacred Beetles to escape. The journey back was peaceful.
When the two reported what happened in the Black Swamp, all the Bug Masters' faces were full of fear.
"Didn't our benefactor go to find trouble with the King Monkey? Why did he have a conflict with the God! "A Bug Master said with some agitation.
On the surface, the tragedy of the Bug Masters was caused by the King Monkey Clan. In fact, it was because the God needed to devour the Dark Swarm.
However, the God was the belief of most creatures in the Dark Realm. The Bug Masters were no exception. Although they had resentment in their hearts, these resentments would only be recorded on the account of the King Monkey Clan.
"I'm afraid challenging the God is courting death …" Another Bug Master with some status shook his head.
"Soon, I'm afraid the King Monkey Clan will blame us. I'm afraid …"
The news brought by Min Qi and Min Mou instantly suppressed the Bug Masters' emotions to the extreme. It could be imagined that they would soon face the slaughter of the King Monkey Clan.
Just when the Bug Masters were feeling depressed, a series of noisy footsteps suddenly came from afar.
The Bug Masters subconsciously thought that it was the King Monkey Clan coming and nervously looked into the distance.
In the darkness, more than ten figures filed out. They were actually from the Cyclops Clan!
The Cyclops Clan was a big clan that could fight the King Monkey head-on. They were also an existence that the Bug Masters absolutely could not associate with. It was just that the Bug Masters and the Cyclops Clan's territories were very far apart. The two clans did not have much interaction.
"Bug Master Clan?" A Cyclops Clan asked in a low voice.
"Yes!"
A few Bug Masters hurriedly went up to greet them.
"There's no need to be so courteous." The one-eyed monster said rather politely, "The new Heaven Master has ordered us to take good care of your Insect Master tribe. This place is too close to the King Monkey tribe, please follow us and leave!"
"The new God?"
"The God has changed?"
"Who is it then?"

Chapter 3219
It wasn't difficult to verify their conjecture from the one-eyed monster.
When the one-eyed monster personally told the Bug Master clan that the new Heaven Master was a human, all the Bug Masters boiled over at this moment.
Min Qi and Min Mou finally understood why these lofty one-eyed monsters would come in person, and with such a polite attitude …
That night, all the Bug Masters packed up their bags, drove all kinds of poisonous insects and ants, and moved away with these one-eyed monsters.
What awaited them was a different fate. Although the Bug Master clan's strength wasn't enough, and even if they were given more time, they probably wouldn't be able to do much in the dark realm. However, under the protection of the one-eyed monsters, they naturally wouldn't be as miserable as before.

The light realm of the Fourteenth Heaven seemed a little calm these days.
Previously, the interior of Tianshui City was in imminent danger. Even without the threat of the Giant clan and the Luring Weasel clan, the interior of Tianshui City would probably collapse first.
However, the Giant clan and the Luring Weasel clan were aggressive at the beginning. After being persuaded by Luo Zheng, they had been retreating to Wanling City. Many large clans in Tianshui City thought that the two clans were showing weakness to the Star Falcon clan.
This move naturally deepened the prestige of the Star Falcon clan, intimidating many large clans in Tianshui City, and helping the Star Falcon clan stabilize the situation.
Early in the morning, thousands of white apes had already gathered outside Tianshui City.
The White Ape clan used to be a member of Tianshui City, but they were later expelled by the Star Falcon clan.
This time, Bai Sheng helped the Star Falcon clan find Luo Yan, and even successfully led Luo Zheng into the dark realm. It could be considered a great contribution.
The Star Falcon clan re-granted the White Ape clan the right to enter the city and rewarded them with a large piece of territory in Tianshui City.
The chief of the White Ape clan walked at the front, followed by Bai Sheng.
Bai Sheng held his head high, as if he was a hero returning with honor.
The White Ape clan being able to have today was all thanks to him alone!
Even the chief had appointed Bai Sheng as his successor. He only had to wait until they settled in Tianshui City to pass on the chief position to him.
On top of the city wall, two huge Star Falcons were looking down at the activity below. These two Star Falcons were the two elders of the Star Falcon Clan who had stayed behind to guard the city. One was called Ling Yu, and the other was called Kong Luo.
After the defeat of the Star Falcon Clan, it was these two Elders who took charge of the overall situation, including the six Star Falcons that kidnapped Luo Yan.
"Lingyu, why did you let these trash into the city?" Kong Luo stared at the white apes with disdain in his eyes.
The White Ape Clan was a trash race that had been expelled from Tianshui City. They were not qualified to enter the city at all.
"The White Ape Clan ultimately helped our Star Falcon Clan at a critical moment," Ling Yu replied earnestly.
"So what if I helped? Just reward them casually. If we let these cats and dogs into Tianshui City, wouldn't the other big clans see us as a joke? "Kong Luo said.
"The joke is not important." Ling Yu's eyes revealed a shrewd light. "Now we have to let the major clans of Tianshui City understand that even now, there are still forces that choose to join the Star Falcon clan! We have nothing to lose by giving the White Ape Clan a piece of territory. It's killing two birds with one stone. "
Kong Luo nodded and then asked, "Is there still no news from the dark realm?"
Ling Yu shook his head. "Compared to these trivial matters, I'm more concerned about news from the dark realm. That woman named Feng Ge is a pure person. I'm afraid even the King Monkey Clan won't be able to deal with her."
The creatures of the dark realm were generally stronger than the creatures of the light realm, but that woman was actually a pure person. She was the nemesis of the creatures of the dark realm.
"Those two people haven't returned these days. They most likely died in the dark realm!" Kong Luo said. "Don't forget that there's another Heaven Master in the dark realm!"
At the mention of the Heaven Master, Ling Yu's confidence increased.
The Star Falcon Clan did not know much about the dark realm, but they understood the general situation. The King Monkey Clan would most likely borrow the Heaven Master's hand to kill those two humans. If the Heaven Master really took action, those two would probably have died in the dark realm …
"I'll send someone to the dark realm to inquire …" Ling Yu nodded.
At this moment, Ling Yu suddenly felt a tremor under his feet. Then, the entire city wall began to shake.
"Rumble …"
"What's going on?" Kong Luo's eyes flashed with a strange light.
The White Ape Clan that entered Tianshui City in succession also raised their heads and looked at the constantly shaking city wall.
"What's going on?" The White Ape Clan Leader asked.
Bai Sheng was also not very clear. He turned around and shouted, "My people, quickly enter the city!"
"Crack …"
At this moment, a loud tearing sound was heard. Not far away, a huge crack extended from the bottom of the city wall.
"Boom!"
A section of the city wall collapsed, and four figures appeared next to the city wall. It was Luo Zheng and the other three.
When Bai Sheng saw these four people, his expression changed abruptly. A bad premonition arose in his heart. When the Star Falcons saw these four people, their expressions also became abnormally ugly.
"Yan 'er, you guys withdraw from Paramita first," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Yan and Ling Yun's Yang Souls were still too weak. In a chaotic battle, they might be in danger.
"Mm." Luo Yan nodded.
"Remember! It's that white ape! It's that white ape that killed our big sister Yan Ling! "Ling Yun pointed fiercely at Bai Sheng below.
Previously, Ling Yun and Luo Yan did not understand why Bai Sheng would choose to betray the Jiuli Tribe. The Jiuli Tribe and the White Ape Clan had a good relationship. It turned out that it was to return to Tianshui City!
After Ling Yun and Luo Yan finished speaking, they withdrew from Paramita.
"Yo —"
More than a dozen Star Falcons spread their wings and flew straight to Luo Zheng.
These Star Falcons were the guards of Tianshui City. They did not know the inside story of Luo Zheng and the others. They only regarded Luo Zheng and Feng Ge as ordinary intruders.
After seeing these Star Falcons, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. With a sudden stomp of his legs, he turned into an electric arc and flew out.
"Crackle!"
This electric arc kept bouncing between the Star Falcons. In the blink of an eye, more than a dozen Star Falcons were charred and fell from mid-air into Tianshui City.
"Crackle!"
The arc of lightning flashed through the air in a huge arc, striking directly in front of Bai Sheng's body, bursting with lightning, causing the ears of the White Ape Clan members to ring.
Luo Zheng's figure quietly appeared. His eyes were full of coldness. "In order to have a foothold in Tianshui City, you actually cooperated with the Star Falcon Clan to kidnap my sister …"

Chapter 3220
After hearing Luo Zheng's words, Bai Sheng's jaw dropped.
Not only Bai Sheng, but the White Ape Clan's patriarch and elders were also dumbfounded.
The White Ape Clan had been away from Tianshui City for too long and didn't know much about the situation in the city. They never thought that there would be such a secret.
"No, impossible …" Bai Sheng's face was full of disbelief.

The city wall smashed into a huge Star Falcon statue in the square not far away. This Star Falcon statue had stood here for countless years. The moment the city wall smashed down, it turned into ruins.

"He is the new Lord of the Heavens?"

The Pure Witch's range of activity was not limited to the Dark Realm. The creatures in the Light Realm naturally knew about it. After all, it was the most powerful existence in the First Heaven.

After Ling Yu and Kong Luo heard this, they looked at each other at the same time. Their eagle eyes were full of disbelief.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's appearance in Tianshui City was the worst news for the Star Falcon Clan.
This meant that the King Monkey Clan had already failed.
However, Ling Yu and Kong Luo deduced that the King Monkey Clan should have been unable to ask the Lord of the Heavens to take action. If the Lord of the Heavens had taken action, the man and woman of the Human Clan would have been defeated in the end.
Therefore, the Star Falcon Clan still had a last chance to turn the tables. They would have asked the Lord of the Heavens to take action at all costs.
However, according to Feng Ge, they actually killed the Lord of the Heavens and even replaced him!
"Is that human woman telling the truth?" Ling Yu's voice was trembling.
Kong Luo shook his head and said, "It's fake. He must be bragging!"
"Even if it's fake, we won't be able to protect Tianshui City. If it's real, I'm afraid our Star Falcon race will be completely annihilated. Let's take the other Star Falcons and abandon the city …" Ling Yu muttered.
While Bai Sheng was still in a daze, Ling Yu and Kong Luo had already flown into the sky, and at the same time, they let out a loud bird cry.
All the remaining Star Falcons in Tianshui City rose from the ground and followed the two elders. They had completely abandoned Tianshui City.
The Star Falcon Clan had given up on Sky Water City so cleanly. All the clans and powers in the city were dumbfounded. Even the White Ape Clan was dumbfounded.
Today was a joyous occasion for the White Ape Clan to enter the city, but the Star Falcon Clan had chosen to abandon the city as soon as they entered. This was truly ironic …
"Your White Ape Clan is on good terms with Li Shan, so you could have returned to Tianshui City openly. Unfortunately, you made the wrong choice …" Luo Zheng said as he reached out and placed his palm on Bai Sheng's head.
At this moment, Bai Sheng didn't dare to resist at all. He didn't even have the courage to run away. He just stood there trembling, his heart filled with endless regret.
Seeing how the Star Falcon Clan had fled so decisively, Luo Zheng was probably telling the truth. At that time, he shouldn't have taken the extra step to curry favor with the Star Falcon Clan!
However, there was no medicine for regret in this world. Bai Sheng only heard a crackling sound from above, and dazzling arcs of electricity had already enveloped his entire body …
In a moment, Bai Sheng had turned into a piece of charcoal in front of Luo Zheng.
When the other White Ape Clan members saw this scene, their hearts were also filled with fear, waiting for Luo Zheng to deal with them.
The main reason for this matter was still Bai Sheng. The White Ape Clan wasn't at fault. However, after learning the truth, the White Ape Clan still enjoyed the fruits of Bai Sheng's labor with a clear conscience — entering Tianshui City.
From this point of view, everyone in the White Ape Clan had offended Luo Zheng.
"All of you, withdraw from Tianshui City," Luo Zheng said coldly.
The leader of the White Ape Clan had been waiting for Luo Zheng's decision. When he heard that they were to withdraw from Tianshui City, he was immediately overjoyed.
Under such circumstances, it was already a great fortune to be able to preserve the lives of their clansmen!
After the White Ape Clan withdrew from Tianshui City, Luo Yan and Ling Yun returned to the other side.
Ling Yun looked at Bai Sheng, who had been struck by lightning and turned into charcoal, and her voice was still cold. "This kid got off too easy. He killed our Senior Sister Yan Ling. Even if he was cut into a thousand pieces, it wouldn't be enough!"
Luo Yan also sighed. "I hope he can comfort Senior Sister Yan Ling's soul."
Since the Star Falcon Clan had already left, Luo Zheng had no need to stay here.
Tianshui City was already considered a city without a master, but Luo Zheng naturally couldn't occupy this city alone.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to return to Wanling City, three Nirvana creatures in Tianshui City stepped forward. These three Nirvana creatures each represented three major Nirvana Clans, and the power of these three clans in Tianshui City was second only to the Star Falcon Clan.
The purpose of these three clans was very clear. Since Luo Zheng had driven away the Star Falcon Clan, he was naturally the master of Tianshui City.
Luo Zheng naturally couldn't do it alone.
Naturally, the three great clans were the most suitable ones to manage such a large city on their behalf.
The actions of the three clans were exactly the same as the actions of the Baitou Clan and the Giant Clan in Wanling City, but the difference was that the Giant Clan and the Baitou Clan were more sincere. Before Luo Zheng gave the order, the Baitou Clan and the Giant Clan had already taken action in advance and destroyed the Black Bear Clan.
These three clans obviously dared to step forward after seeing the Star Falcon Clan escape, which was a level lower than the Baitou Clan and the Giant Clan.
"You're late," Luo Zheng glanced at them indifferently, then walked out of the city with Feng Ge and the others.
The clan leaders of the three clans looked at each other. They were extremely shrewd and instantly understood Luo Zheng's meaning. Luo Zheng already had a suitable candidate to manage Tianshui City!
"No matter who you choose, they're not as suitable as us. We're the real …" A clan leader chattered with Luo Zheng, hoping to fight for this opportunity.
Luo Zheng finally stopped and looked coldly at the clan leader and said, "You should be glad that the Star Falcon Clan took the initiative to leave Tianshui City. Otherwise, there would be rivers of blood here, and you wouldn't be able to escape."
The clan leader immediately chose to shut up and didn't dare to say anything more. He watched Luo Zheng leave.
Dozens of Sun Souls gathered in a few high-lying buildings in Tianshui City.
These Sun Souls came from the Golden Crow Clan, You Xiong Clan, the Divine Farmer Clan, and the Minyue Clan.

"It's over. Now, let alone Wanling City, we can't even stay in Tianshui City!"
"It's not just Tianshui City that can't stay. It should be the 14th Layer of Heaven!"
"How did things develop to this point?"

Chapter 3221
Before Luo Zheng left Wanling City, he had called back Chidian and Blood Roar.
These days, Blood Roar often carried that thick metal stick and paced back and forth in front of the City Guards Mansion.
Except for Chidian, the leader of the Weasel Tribe, no one else dared to approach. Blood Roar's full attack under its rage was enough to smash any creature into a meat patty.
A few days ago, when it was about to take down Tianshui City, it was forcibly called back by Luo Zheng!
Once it thought of this, it felt as if there was a thorn in its throat, making it very restless.
The key was that Blood Roar was still worried about the safety of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
The Dark Domain was much more dangerous than the Light Domain. Luo Zheng would be in danger if he rashly broke in.
These days, Blood Roar wanted to lead the Giant Tribe to attack Tianshui City again several times, but it was stopped by Chidian.
Chidian was not afraid of the remaining forces in Tianshui City, but it would not act on its own when Luo Zheng was not there.

Luo Zheng knew that Blood Roar's excitement was more about the gains and losses of the Giant Tribe and him, but Luo Zheng was still very touched that a Nirvana Realm creature was so concerned about his safety.
In the lobby of the City Guards Mansion.
The core figures of the Weasel Tribe and the Giant Tribe gathered together.
These Nirvana Realm creatures in Wanling City did not know that Luo Zheng had been to Tianshui City. Chidian even thought for a while before asking, "City Guards, when do you plan to attack Tianshui City?"
"I've been to Tianshui City," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
"You've been there?" Chidian's soybean-sized eyes showed surprise.
"The Star Falcon Tribe has escaped from Tianshui City, and no one dares to resist. You can take people to take over Tianshui City," Luo Zheng said.
No matter how the Star Falcon Tribe escaped, they were still in the Fourteenth Heaven.
Without Tianshui City as their foundation, and having been severely injured by Luo Zheng, it was impossible for this large clan to recover in a short time.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't have the intention to chase down the remaining Star Falcon Clan. When the time came, he could just let the great clans of Sky Water City and Myriad Spirit City chase after them.
Three days later, the Giant Tribe and the Ferret Tribe entered Tianshui City.
Sure enough, as Luo Zheng said, after entering the city, no one dared to stop them. Even several major races in Tianshui City rushed to seek refuge with the Ferret Tribe and the Giant Tribe.
Crimson Lightning and Blood Roar also knew that if these major races joined forces, they would still have the power to fight. The biggest reason why they were able to smoothly take over Sky Water City was because of Luo Zheng!

After that, Luo Zheng invited Li Shan to Wanling City.
