But Luo Zheng didn't want anyone to have any ideas about Luo Yan. In the end, he persuaded Li Shan's people to relocate Li Shan's stronghold to Wanling City.
… …
The entire Fourteenth Heaven now fell into the hands of Greater One Heavenly Palace, or rather, Luo Zheng.
This news once again made the Land of Sword Grief boil.
All the disciples of the Heavenly Palace could walk unhindered in the Fourteenth Heaven. Two months ago, this was simply an unimaginable thing!
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace on the first floor revered Luo Zheng as if he was a god.
A few days later, it was the beginning of the month again.
According to the rules of the Land of Sword Grief, all the disciples of the Land of Sword Grief automatically obtained the right to challenge all the disciples on the same floor and determine their position on the Ranking Stone.
Those disciples ranked in the top ten originally coveted Luo Zheng's first place and were thinking of pulling him down.
But last month, because Luo Zheng was in seclusion, no one on the first floor dared to disturb him.
Today was a new month. The disciples of the first floor chose to come out of seclusion and choose their opponents to challenge. The fighting stage behind the bronze building was surrounded by disciples of the Heavenly Palace.
After Luo Zheng came out of Room 1, he also came to the fighting stage.
As soon as he appeared, he attracted the attention of many people.
"Luo Zheng came out of seclusion!"
"This time he intends to use his right to challenge?"
"He won't challenge the Second floor, will he?"
"It's possible …"
Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to those discussions and focused on the fighting stage.
There were two people fighting on the fighting stage. One of them was Chou Xun, and the other was an old man of the Land of Sword Grief named Zhao Jiuxiang, ranked 600th.
As a newly promoted disciple, Chou Xun dared to challenge an old man ranked 600th on his second challenge. He was quite courageous.
In fact, the newcomers this time had performed well. For example, Feng Ge, Luo Yan, Mo Yijian, and a few others had all entered the top 1000 in the first month. In the past, this would have been very impressive.
But this time, a monster like Luo Zheng had appeared. The difference between the two was too great. The so-called "impressive" performances of Mo Yijian, Chou Xun, and the others did not attract the attention of others.
"Pa, pa, pa!"
On the fighting stage, Chou Xun circled around Zhao Jiuxiang like a gust of wind.
In a situation where they were unable to use the Nirvana Realm Token, the two of them were competing on strength, physical strength, and pure skills.
Zhao Jiuxiang had the absolute advantage in physical strength, but his movement technique was inferior to Chou Xun's. Every time Zhao Jiuxiang wanted to get close to Chou Xun, Chou Xun would always use ingenious methods to avoid him.
After dodging too many times, Zhao Jiuxiang became a little impatient.
He increased his speed and was about to force Chou Xun into a corner.
But Chou Xun seemed to have calculated it. With a slight twist of his shoulder, he dodged past Zhao Jiuxiang like an agile fish.
Zhao Jiuxiang did not expect this move. His back was already wide open.
How could Chou Xun let go of such an opportunity?
He came to a sudden stop. After flipping around, he compressed his strength to the limit before sending out three palms at Zhao Jiuxiang!
"Three Layered Waves!"
Chou Xun was particular about planning before taking action. The reason why he dared to challenge Zhao Jiuxiang who was ranked 600th was because he had fused with a new Nirvana token at the 14th Level Heaven.
The Nirvana token in the Desire Realm granted him a stronger Nirvana Energy. This was also the reason why Chou Xun was so confident.
The three palms he sent out with all his strength contained profound principles. Every palm contained close to 3,000 divine catties of strength. Furthermore, the three palms overlapped each other, increasing the power.
Zhao Jiuxiang had entered the Chaotic Origin Realm for a long time. After cultivating for a long time, the Chaotic Origin Spirit in his body had improved significantly. Even so, it was still hard for him to take on such attacks.
The powerful palm force entered his body through his back, causing the Chaotic Origin Spirit in his body to become fatigued. The remaining energy was still surging within his body, forcing him to cough out a mouthful of blood.
"Zhao Jiuxiang was actually defeated?"
"This Chou Xun cannot be underestimated. He has only been in the Land of Sword Grief for half a month yet he has defeated Zhao Jiuxiang!"
"He might be powerful, but he is still incomparable to Luo Zheng …"
When Chou Xun heard those words, he revealed a helpless smile on his face.
This new Nirvana token he fused with was given to him by the Ear Mouse Clan. And the reason why the Ear Mice gave him the Nirvana token was because of Luo Zheng's reputation.
After all, the entire Myriad Spirit City was trying to curry favor with Luo Zheng.
As for comparing him with Luo Zheng?

Chapter 3222
Chou Xinghe came down from the stage and saw Luo Zheng below the stage. He smiled and said, "Brother Luo Zheng, you've also come out of seclusion?"
When Luo Zheng came to the front of the stage, only a small number of people noticed him. Most of the disciples on the first floor had their attention on the stage.
When Chou Xinghe said that, all the disciples on the first floor focused their attention on Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng turned the City Guard Mansion of Wanling City into the stronghold of the Heavenly Palace, Luo Zheng was either running around outside or busy communicating with the Nirvana Realm races in Wanling City. It was not easy to see Luo Zheng in the Nirvana Realm.
But after returning to the Land of Sword Grief, Luo Zheng was still their "Junior Brother". The difference in status was not that big, so it was naturally easier for them to ask.
"It's Brother Luo Zheng! Brother Luo Zheng, I remember the Heavenly Cloud Clan gave me a rare golden turtle. Can you give me that golden turtle? "
"Brother Luo Zheng, when will you distribute the soul crystals again?"
"Junior Brother Luo, I received 200,000 soul pills from the Scaled Tiger Clan. Can I not hand them over …"
Taiyi Heavenly Palace was not rich in the Nirvana Realm. Even in the Desire Realm, soul crystals were still scarce.
The disciples in the Land of Sword Grief found soul crystals with great difficulty or exchanged them for some soul pills, but they always had to pay a part of it to the stronghold.
The stronghold also took care of the many disciples and distributed them according to their needs. The problem was that several races in Wanling City often "extorted" Taiyi Heavenly Palace. The annual tribute was not small, which put a heavy burden on the stronghold.
But after Luo Zheng came, not only did they not have to pay tribute, but the entire Wanling City also paid tribute to Taiyi Heavenly Palace. Now Tianshui City also joined in.
It could be said that the resources of the entire Fourteenth Heaven were in the hands of Luo Zheng alone. How could the disciples in the Land of Sword Grief not be jealous?
Luo Zheng had already given Chou Xinghe and Ling Shuang once.
Almost all of the disciples on the first floor benefited equally from the rain and dew, obtaining great benefits.
Human desires were endless. In the past, the Heavenly Palace's stronghold had been too poor. It was good enough that everyone took care of themselves. They would take as much as the Heavenly Palace could give them.
Right now, Luo Zheng was guarding a mountain of gold!
Among the disciples of the first floor, not only did the disciples of the 15th, 16th, and 17th Heavens come down, even the disciples of higher levels, such as Chun Hao and the other top existences of the first floor, planned to return to the 14th Heavens.
Although Chun Hao and Luo Zheng had a grudge against each other, in front of the Soul Crystal and other benefits, this grudge was nothing.
Many disciples from the first floor surrounded Luo Zheng. In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng was surrounded by three layers of disciples.
"This …"
Chou Xue saw that his greeting had actually attracted such a reaction, which was completely unexpected.
Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry. He didn't expect the disciples on the first floor to have such a reaction.
Seeing that more and more people were gathering around, he could only wave his hand and say, "Everyone, please deal with the matters in the other side of the world!"
How could these people not know this rule?
But it was not so easy to find Luo Zheng in the Wanling City. They had to go through the Weasel Tribe and the Giant Tribe. Now that they were facing Luo Zheng in person, no one was willing to let go of the opportunity to ask for help.
"It's the same to talk about the matters in the other side of the world here!"
"You and I are both disciples of the Heavenly Palace. Why bother …"
"Brother Luo Zheng, I …"
"Shut up, all of you!"
Just as everyone was discussing amongst themselves, a cold and stern voice rang out.
Everyone looked over and saw Ling Shuang.
That day, Luo Zheng carried Ling Shuang all the way from the 13th Heavens to the 14th Heavens. The disciples of the Heavenly Palace also saw the relationship between Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang. This matter was also spread in the Land of Sword Grief.
What's more, her status was relatively high. Under such a cold rebuke, no one said anything more.
"If you need anything or do anything, just find Chou Xue and record it. If the request is reasonable, there will be no problem. Although Luo Zheng occupies the Wanling City, the resources are not unlimited. What's more … he doesn't owe you anything!" Ling Shuang continued.
After occupying the Wanling City, Luo Zheng handed the collected resources to Chou Xue for distribution. Luo Zheng did not give any restrictions. As long as it was a request of the disciples of the Heavenly Palace, he would grant it.
It was human nature to be greedy. This also caused the disciples of the first floor to eagerly wait in Wanling City. The resources that they collected, except for the portion that was left for Luo Zheng, were instantly divided among the other disciples!
According to this trend, it would not be long before all the disciples of the first floor would hide in the Wanling City and wait for Luo Zheng to feed them.
This was not only unfavorable to Luo Zheng, but also to the disciples of the entire Land of Sword Grief.
"Princess Ling Shuang is right. Brother Luo Zheng asked me to manage it. If you have any requests, just come to me," Chou Xue also smiled at the crowd.
However, the requests put forward by these people were more or less excessive. It was basically impossible to get past Chou Xue. Coupled with Ling Shuang's words, the disciples of the first floor were suddenly unhappy.
"Humph, what's the use of looking for you?"
"I heard that the Soul Crystals handed in by the Weasel Tribe and the Giant Tribe every month are as many as ten thousand fingers, but only one finger is given to us …"
"Chou Xue, last time I asked you to help me ask for a Nirvana Realm token from Chidian. It's not too difficult to ask, but you flatly refused …"
These disciples, who were still fawning over Chou Xue just now, had changed their expressions at this moment. They even turned around and blamed Chou Xue.
In fact, the implication was that even Luo Zheng was blamed.
But after all, they did not dare to offend Luo Zheng. Most of them were targeting Chou Xue.
Looking at the ugly faces of the disciples of the first floor, Luo Zheng also frowned.
After occupying the Thousands Spirit City, Luo Zheng also realized that he would obtain a huge amount of resources.
Luo Zheng alone could not consume all the resources in the Fourteenth Heaven, and Luo Zheng, as a disciple of the Heavenly Palace, should indeed give back to the Heavenly Palace. Luo Zheng was also willing to share a part of it with the Land of Sword Grief. He never thought that it would lead to such a reaction from the disciples of the Heavenly Palace.
At this moment, Feng Ge strolled out from the back door of the bronze building, and at the same time said in a cold voice, "Sure enough, you were bullied in the Thousands Spirit City in the past, and did not even dare to fart. Now that Luo Zheng and I have occupied the city, you are quite confident in front of us?"
For the Greater One Heavenly Palace to have such a situation in the Fourteenth Heaven, Feng Ge also had half the credit. Her words immediately suppressed the disciples of the first floor.
But in the end, there were still some people who were not convinced. Fang Wei, who was standing in the corner, suddenly said, "According to the rules of the Land of Sword Grief, the disciples should pay half of the resources they excavate, and share it with each disciple of the Land of Sword Grief. In the past, we have followed this rule, so there is nothing wrong with everyone's request …"
Before Fang Wei finished speaking, a golden scroll appeared in Feng Ge's hand. She smiled coldly and opened the scroll, saying at the same time, "The Eastern Emperor's Imperial Decree, the resources obtained by me and Luo Zheng belong to me and Luo Zheng. There is no need to share it with the Land of Sword Grief. We can distribute it however we like, you can open your dog eyes wide and watch!"
Feng Ge would not be polite to these guys who did not know what was good for them!

Chapter 3223
After Feng Ge finished speaking, she stretched out her hand and gently threw the scroll behind her.
The golden scroll was nailed to the door like an arrow. The scroll hung down, revealing the contents of the edict.
Donghuang had personally issued an edict. Of course, it would not be as straightforward as Feng Ge's words, but that was the general meaning.
These days, Feng Ge had been in Wanling City. Compared to Luo Zheng, who had been busy cultivating, Feng Ge had more contact with the disciples of the Sword of Grief and could see more clearly.
Feng Ge had long expected that the issue of resource allocation would develop to this point.
Even if the Fourteenth Heaven was at the bottom of the Desire Realm, it was still very shocking when added together. There must be rules to endorse, otherwise it would definitely be chaotic!
Therefore, Feng Ge quietly returned to the Taiyi Palace for this purpose. She directly found Donghuang and issued an edict!
As soon as this edict was issued, the disciples on the first floor were completely speechless.
They had hoped to get more, but they never thought that Feng Ge would do such a thing. Moreover, this was Donghuang's edict, which could not be disobeyed!
The crowd, who had been noisy, suddenly fell silent.
If it was really like what Feng Ge said, the resources that everyone wanted to get would probably be in vain.
"Donghuang said that you will distribute it. How do you plan to distribute it …" Someone asked in a low voice.
Feng Ge shrugged her shoulders, turned her gaze to Luo Zheng, and nodded to him.
Luo Zheng glanced at these people indifferently.
He had previously asked Chou Xue to try his best to satisfy the demands of these people, but the result was more greedy demands. This was human nature. He sneered and said, "Everything will be as usual. Brother Chou Xue will handle it for me."
The edict that Feng Ge had taken out earlier was a big stick. After these guys who did not know what was good for them suffered the stick, Luo Zheng threw out the "carrot" appropriately. The same conditions were exchanged for endless gratitude.
"If you have any requests, you can register with me. Come one by one and line up properly!" Chou Xue said with a smile.
The issue of resource allocation had come to an end. Under Chou Xue's supervision, there would naturally be no problems.
Following that, another person jumped onto the stage to compete for their own ranking.
The new disciples challenged each other, the new disciples challenged each other, the old disciples challenged each other, and the old disciples challenged each other on the first floor. However, no one dared to challenge Luo Zheng …
On one hand, it was because of Luo Zheng's high status in the Nirvana Realm. On the other hand, the scene of Luo Zheng defeating Chun Hao was still vivid in their minds.
Luo Zheng was not in a hurry. He silently observed from below the fighting ring.
After more than ten fights, Luo Zheng finally stepped onto the stage.
Others did not dare to challenge Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng could take the initiative to challenge others!
After Luo Zheng stood on the fighting stage, many people had expectations in their eyes.
"This time, Luo Zheng's target should be the Second Floor, right?"
"The first time he entered the Land of Sword of Grief, he took first place. The second time he challenged it, he ascended to the Second Floor?"
"I think it's too exaggerated, but Luo Zheng might be able to do it …"
Whether it was the Land of Sword of Grief or the Nirvana Realm, Luo Zheng's performance was obvious to all.
When Luo Zheng was about to exercise his right to challenge, many people on the Second Floor cast their gazes toward the fighting ring.
There were a total of 203 people on the Second Floor.
Compared to the disciples on the First Floor, the disciples on the Second Floor had been reborn. Their strength was one level higher than the disciples on the First Floor.
Even Long Yi, who ranked last on the Second Floor, was stronger than Chun Hao.
In order to climb to the Second Floor, Chun Hao had challenged Long Yi several times. However, he had failed miserably every time.
There was only one rank between Chun Hao and Long Yi. However, this one rank represented the huge gap between the First Floor and the Second Floor.
"Long Yi, I think Luo Zheng might challenge you," the fair-skinned scholar said with a smile.
Gu Lie nodded by the side of the scholar, "If he defeats you, he will be able to stand firmly on the Second Floor. This should be his first step!"
A dark-skinned hunk stood by the side of the two. This dark-skinned hunk was the "Long Yi" mentioned by the scholar. He was also one of the last ranked disciples on the Second Floor.
"He does not have the strength to stand on the Second Floor yet." Long Yi proudly said.
"That might not be the case." Gu Lie shook his head, "Don't forget that he has occupied the 14th Level Heaven. He is truly a mysterious fellow!"
"He is indeed mysterious. But it is far from enough to challenge me." Long Yi shook his head, "No matter what, he has just reached the Chaotic Origin Realm not long ago. Perhaps he might have some methods to strengthen himself. But those are ultimately unorthodox methods. I'll just break through them!"
Luo Zheng suddenly waved his finger on the fighting ring. This finger happened to point at Long Yi.
"He is indeed challenging me?" A strand of coldness appeared on Long Yi's face. Being ranked last was a huge humiliation. This was also why Long Yi cared about his ranking. Luo Zheng's decision to challenge him unintentionally made Long Yi feel even more displeased, 'Then I'll let you understand what the Second Floor is …'
Just as this thought appeared in Long Yi's mind, Luo Zheng pointed at the Second Floor and said, "I want to challenge Zheng Shuangqi of the Second Floor!"
The audience was silent for a moment. Suddenly, an uproar broke out.
"Luo Zheng wants to challenge Zheng Shuangqi!"
"Heavens!"
"The first time he challenged the first ranked of the First Floor, Chun Hao. The second time, he wants to challenge the first ranked of the Second Floor, Zheng Shuangqi. Doesn't that mean Luo Zheng is going to challenge the first ranked of the Sword of Grief, Li Bianxue?"
"In my opinion, it is only a matter of time before he challenges Li Bianxue. But Luo Zheng has only entered the Sword of Grief for two months. Just two months …"
Who was Zheng Shuangqi?
Seven years ago, Zheng Shuangqi issued his last challenge to the Third Floor. After his defeat, he no longer had any plans to challenge the Third Floor. The disciples of the Second Floor also crazily challenged Zheng Shuangqi, but no one had ever pulled him down from the first place of the Second Floor.
There were two hundred and two people on the Second Floor, and Zheng Shuangqi was on a different level all by himself!
The expressions on the faces of the fair scholar, Gu Lie, Long Yi, and the other disciples of the Second Floor were extremely interesting. The fair scholar opened his folding fan and said to Long Yi, "It seems that we all guessed wrong. He doesn't like you at all …"
"He doesn't like you either," Long Yi retorted.
"He also doesn't like me," Gu Lie said with a self-deprecating smile.
They really didn't expect Luo Zheng to directly challenge that freak Zheng Shuangqi …
Even Lian Chou, Mo Yijian, Ling Shuang, and the others were dumbfounded. Luo Zheng planned to activate the World Inheritance here? If he didn't activate the World Inheritance, challenging Zheng Shuangqi was no different from courting death. After all, the gap between them was too big.
"Zheng Shuangqi is in seclusion. Wait a moment, I'll go get him!"
There was no lack of busybodies on the Second Floor. After saying this, that person really went to find Zheng Shuangqi.

Chapter 3224
Room 1 on the far right of the Second Floor was Zheng Shuangqi's secret cultivation room.
It was a taboo to disturb someone's seclusion, but there was an exception in the Land of Sword Grief, which was when the right to challenge was exercised at the beginning of every month.
Unless there was a special reason to stay on the other side, under normal circumstances, one would have to accept the challenge.
Zheng Shuangqi had just returned from the other side a few days ago. He found a clue about the temple, and following this clue, he might find an undiscovered temple.
After Zheng Shuangqi sorted out these clues, he prepared to go out. There were three Sanskrit words that needed to be deciphered.
Other than Donghuang himself, there were a few people in the Tai Yi Heaven Palace who were good at deciphering Sanskrit, but to get them to decipher it would cost a lot, and there was no guarantee of time.
But Zheng Shuangqi didn't have many choices. There were some people in Ming Hu who could decipher Sanskrit, but their standards were even worse.
"Dong dong dong …"
At this moment, someone knocked on the door of the secret cultivation room.
Hearing the knocking, Zheng Shuangqi frowned slightly.
Today was the first day of the month, and also the day of the challenge in the Land of Sword Grief.
All the disciples of the Second Floor wanted to pull him down from the first place. He had been challenged countless times over the years, but he was still the first place of the Second Floor, as stable as a mountain.
Four months ago, Zheng Shuangqi was tired of the endless challenges and severely defeated a disciple of the Second Floor. The disciples of the Second Floor realized the gap between them and Zheng Shuangqi and finally stopped for a period of time.
Zheng Shuangqi thought that he would be able to stop for at least a year and a half, but after four months, someone had the idea of challenging him?
It seemed that he wasn't ruthless enough last time!
After opening the door, Zheng Shuangqi stared outside. Standing at the door was Song Fei, ranked 168 on the Second Floor.
"You want to challenge me?" Zheng Shuangqi stared at Song Fei and asked.
Song Fei's face revealed a mocking smile, "How would I dare?"
There weren't many people in the Second Floor who dared to challenge Zheng Shuangqi, and Song Fei was obviously not one of them.
"Who is that?" Zheng Shuangqi asked again.
"That person is called Luo Zheng, and he's already standing on the stage," Song Fei replied.
"Luo Zheng? I've never heard of this person. "Zheng Shuangqi was stunned again.
During this period of time, Zheng Shuangqi had been cultivating in the secret chamber, so he wasn't aware of what had happened in the Land of Sword Grief.
The name Luo Zheng was unfamiliar. He didn't seem to be from the Second Floor.
Zheng Shuangqi's strange expression was within Song Fei's expectations. Song Fei laughed and said, "That kid is first place on the first floor. He hasn't entered the second floor yet."
The champion of the first floor didn't even come up to the second floor, and he directly challenged the champion of the second floor?
A strange look appeared on Zheng Shuangqi's face.
Let alone the champion of the first floor, even the top ten rankers of the second floor had to be careful when challenging Zheng Shuangqi. They had to be prepared to be seriously injured or even die!
Zheng Shuangqi walked out of the secret cultivation room and asked, "Is this person not clear about the situation?"
He stood by the window on the second floor, and his gaze had already descended onto the arena. On the arena stood a young man in black clothes. This young man stood with his hands behind his back as he looked at the second floor. His eyes were spirited, and his face was suffused with boundless confidence.
The black-clothed young man was naturally the waiting Luo Zheng.
When Zheng Shuangqi stood by the window, the disciples of the first floor below the arena immediately burst into an uproar.
"The second floor's Zheng Shuangqi has come out!"
"It seems that Zheng Shuangqi intends to accept the challenge!"
"I'd like to see if Luo Zheng can still create a miracle this time …"
Actually, at this point, many people really hoped that Luo Zheng could defeat Zheng Shuangqi. However, almost everyone knew that this was simply impossible.
Once was enough for a miracle to happen to a person. The possibility of consecutive miracles happening to a person was almost negligible.
On the second floor, Gu Lie, the pale-faced scholar, Long Yi, and the others were also staring at Zheng Shuangqi. They wanted to see how Zheng Shuangqi would respond.
They knew that Zheng Shuangqi wasn't willing to accept the challenge, but Luo Zheng had exercised his right to challenge Zheng Shuangqi. In addition, he was the champion of the first floor. This was in accordance with the rules. Zheng Shuangqi had no right to refuse!
"I heard that you wanted to challenge me? Who gave you the courage? "
Zheng Shuangqi looked down coldly at Luo Zheng, who was on the arena.
That aloof look made it seem as though he was looking down at an ant.
He didn't know about Luo Zheng's recent series of valiant battle results and his deeds in the Nirvana Realm. If he knew, Zheng Shuangqi might have been more polite.
He only thought that Luo Zheng was a new disciple of the first floor. After climbing to the first floor's position, he was flushed with success and actually had the wishful thinking of challenging him, the champion of the second floor!
Luo Zheng raised his head and stared at Zheng Shuangqi. When he heard what Zheng Shuangqi said, Luo Zheng's expression didn't change at all. He just said calmly, "The Land of Sword Grief grants every disciple the right to challenge."
"Is that so?"
Zheng Shuangqi walked out of the window without saying a word.
Every time he took a step, a fist-sized spatial grid would suddenly appear under his feet. These spatial grids combined to form a staircase.
When he took another step, the spatial grid would condense into another staircase. That was the supernatural power of the True Meaning of Void and Vanishing.
He walked down from the second floor directly and stepped on the arena. At the same time, he said, "I don't have time to play with you. Although you do have the right to challenge me, you should give up. You shouldn't be interested in me, and you shouldn't choose me to challenge. There are many people there who are more suitable for you."
As Zheng Shuangqi spoke, he pointed to the second floor.
Many disciples on the second floor had left the secret cultivation room and were standing by the window to watch the show.
Zheng Shuangqi felt that he had been very patient in these few months of closed-door cultivation.
If it was four months ago, he wouldn't have warned Luo Zheng that since he dared to challenge him, he should be prepared to get injured or even die.
At this point, this kid called Luo Zheng should choose to give up, right?
However, Luo Zheng still looked at Zheng Shuangqi with indifferent eyes and said, "I have no interest in you. The reason I challenged you is because you occupy the first place of the second floor. If I win, I can challenge the third floor. Therefore, you are naturally the most suitable opponent."
The disciples on the first floor and some of the disciples on the second floor knew about Luo Zheng's previous performance. They didn't find Luo Zheng's words strange.
After all, Luo Zheng's performance had far surpassed Li Bianxue's in the past. It was only a matter of time before he stood on the third floor.
However, this was the first time some of the disciples on the second floor had heard of Luo Zheng. When Luo Zheng said these words, their eyes suddenly became straight.

Chapter 3225
In the eyes of these Second floor disciples who were unaware of the situation, Zheng Shuangqi had already given Luo Zheng a way out.
But Luo Zheng was unwilling to come down and still insisted on challenging Zheng Shuangqi. It seemed that he didn't know what was good for him.
"He came out of nowhere," Gu Lie explained with a smile, "He is a new disciple of this batch and has only been in the Sword Grief Land for two months."
"No wonder, it's only been two months, he probably hasn't figured out the Sword Grief Land yet … Wait!"
The Second floor disciple suddenly realized that something was wrong. If this kid was first on the First floor, didn't that mean he became first in the Sword Grief Land in two months?
"Brother Gu Lie, you mean this new disciple became first on the First floor in two months?" He asked with a strange expression.
These disciples who had climbed to the second floor had all fought their way up from the first floor. They knew how difficult it was to become first in the first floor. The disciples of the second floor found it hard to believe their ears that they had become first in just two months.
"Not two months, he defeated Chun Hao and became first on the first day he entered the Sword Grief Land," Gu Lie said with a shrug.
Even after two months, Gu Lie and the other Second floor disciples still found it unbelievable. When the Second floor disciple heard Gu Lie's words, an extremely shocked expression naturally appeared on his face. For a moment, he didn't know how to describe his feelings.
"It's fine if he became first on the first day, but now he wants to challenge Zheng Shuangqi after two months," Gu Lie narrowed his eyes.
A monster like Luo Zheng was rarely seen even in Great One Heavenly Palace. Since he dared to challenge Zheng Shuangqi, it was impossible for him to act recklessly.
He must have a certain degree of confidence.
What was this kid's confidence?
Zheng Shuangqi's thoughts were the same as Gu Lie's. What was Luo Zheng's confidence? What was it that supported his confidence?
"Brother Shuangqi! Don't look down on this guy, he became first on the First floor on the first day he entered the Sword Grief Land! "A Second floor disciple reminded in front of the window.
After Zheng Shuangqi heard this, a strange light flashed in his eyes, and the contempt in his eyes instantly disappeared.
He instantly understood where Luo Zheng's confidence came from!
"Dominating the first floor on the first day of entering the Land of Sword Grief, how capable!" Zheng Shuangqi pondered for a moment before continuing, "As far as I know, there has never been such an extraordinary person in Greater One Heavenly Palace. Even Li Bianxue can't do it. Unfortunately, you're still ten years too early to challenge me!"
After Zheng Shuangqi finished speaking, he waved his hands and the Nirvana Force gushed out like a flood, flowing wantonly through his entire body.
A dense aura spread out, and the entire fighting stage seemed to be heavily pressed down!
In the depths of Zheng Shuangqi's eyes, a trace of killing intent flashed.
Zheng Shuangqi had been at the top of the Second Floor for far too long …
In the past, he had tried countless times to reach the third floor, but he had failed countless times. In the early days, there were people who stepped on his shoulders and stood on the third floor. A genius like Luo Zheng was destined to stand on the third floor one day.
Zheng Shuangqi harbored deep jealousy towards the geniuses who could stand on the third floor.
Before, he had only intended to teach Luo Zheng a lesson and let him understand the price of speaking arrogantly. But now, there was a trace of impulse in his heart to strangle this genius.
After feeling the power surging out of Zheng Shuangqi's body, the faces of the people below the fighting stage changed slightly.
Although everyone knew that Zheng Shuangqi was the top of the Second Floor, they didn't expect that Zheng Shuangqi's power had reached such a level. The thick and heavy power was highly condensed, as if it was real. It was as if he could casually crush people into pieces with a raise of his hand.
"It's a pity that I can't use the power of the Nirvana Token on the fighting stage," Zheng Shuangqi said with a deep gaze after activating the Nirvana Force. "Otherwise, my power can be increased by three times …"
After Luo Zheng felt Zheng Shuangqi's power, there was a trace of graveness in his eyes. This guy could casually extract more than five thousand Jun of power. Who knew where his limit was.
And Zheng Shuangqi's other Nirvana Token could actually increase his power by three times. This kind of Nirvana Token was indeed very terrifying. However, he didn't have to worry about the fact that he couldn't use the power of the Nirvana Token on the fighting stage.
Just as Zheng Shuangqi finished speaking, he had already jumped up and pounced towards Luo Zheng. As he raised his hand, the Nirvana Force had already flocked towards his right hand.
If one looked carefully, they would be able to see that the Nirvana Force in his right hand was gathering in a spiral shape.
"Swish!"
This incomparably powerful punch had already smashed towards Luo Zheng's head.
Facing Zheng Shuangqi's first punch, Luo Zheng didn't take it head on. Instead, he took a slight step and dodged to the side.
"Boom!"
This punch smashed onto the fighting stage, causing the entire fighting stage to shake violently. The formation on the surface of the fighting stage shone brightly. It was obviously not easy to withstand Zheng Shuangqi's attack.
"Since you took the initiative to challenge me, you should face me head on. What kind of man are you to dodge?"
Zheng Shuangqi sneered. Yet another punch was sent towards Luo Zheng.
The fighting stage of the Ground of Sword Grief was a competition of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin and the Nirvana Force. There weren't many techniques involved in these two battles. Thus, there wasn't much room for tricks.
When Luo Zheng heard those words, he really didn't dodge anymore. He squinted his eyes and spread his hands apart. He actually wanted to parry Zheng Shuangqi's fist!
'Idiot …'
When Zheng Shuangqi saw Luo Zheng's action, he sneered in his heart.
He had merely said a few words, yet this brat really didn't dodge anymore. Instead, he wanted to face him head on. With his explosive strength and speed, how could Luo Zheng possibly parry his attack?
Could such a foolish person really become the number one of the First Floor in a single day?
Zheng Shuangqi suspected that those fellows from the Second Floor were lying to him …
Whatever.
He would just settle this brat with a single punch.
He was still in a rush to hand over those Sanskrit books. He didn't have the time to play around with those fellows from the First Floor. As for whether this brat would die or not, it would depend on his own fortune.
"Peng!"
Just like what Zheng Shuangqi had predicted, this punch of his broke free from Luo Zheng's hands and landed on Luo Zheng's chest.
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng was like a sandbag that was thrown out. He was sent flying towards the edge of the stage. Ultimately, he didn't fall off the stage. Instead, he stopped at the edge of the stage.
If it were any other ordinary cultivator, he would be quite satisfied with the outcome of his punch.
But Zheng Shuangqi didn't seem happy at all. Instead, he became more solemn.
This punch didn't penetrate through Luo Zheng's chest. It didn't even cause Luo Zheng's chest to cave in. That also meant that he didn't exhaust Luo Zheng's Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
This number one of the First Floor had cultivated his Spirit of Chaotic Origin to the Middle Stage?
The disciples of the Supreme Yi Heaven Palace would be kept into the Ground of Sword Grief the moment they reached the Chaotic Origin Realm. Did that mean this brat had just reached the Chaotic Origin Realm?

Chapter 3226
The three levels in the Sword Grief Ground corresponded to the three stages of the Chaotic Origin Realm.
Even though Zheng Shuangqi did not use his full strength in that strike, the strength he unleashed was not something that someone who had just entered the Chaotic Origin Realm could withstand!
They said that Luo Zheng had entered the Sword Grief Ground for two months. Did that mean he had just entered the Chaotic Origin Realm not too long ago?
That was impossible …
To be able to receive a punch from him without the Chaotic Origin Spirit becoming fatigued meant that Luo Zheng was at least at the Middle Stage Chaotic Origin Realm. Reaching the Middle Stage Chaotic Origin Realm in just a few short months?
There was only one possibility. Luo Zheng had already stepped into the Chaotic Origin Realm many years ago. It was just that he was accepted into the Sword Grief Ground recently!
Zheng Shuangqi had only entered the Sword Grief Ground after reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm for a long time. Even though such situations were rare, it was still possible.
"Entered the Chaotic Origin Realm?" Luo Zheng did not expect that Zheng Shuangqi would ask such a question. He lowered his head and pondered for a moment before answering: "About three months ago."
Zheng Shuangqi's expression sank when he heard that, "Do you take me for a fool? Reaching the Middle Stage Chaotic Origin Realm in just three months? That is preposterous! "
Zheng Shuangqi did not believe it at all!
"It is really just three months. Luo Zheng is in the same batch as us!"
"We entered the Chaotic Origin Great World together. How can there be a mistake?"
"Right, to be precise, it is three months and nine days!"
The new disciples below the fighting ring spoke one after another. Each of them had a solemn expression on their faces.
Zheng Shuangqi was completely bewildered when he saw the expression on the faces of these new disciples.
At this moment, Gu Lie shouted from the Second Floor: "Brother Shuangqi, this Little Brother Luo Zheng is a disciple of our Heart Flow Sword Sect. He is indeed a new Chaotic Origin Realm disciple."
Zheng Shuangqi was familiar with Gu Lie. He knew that Gu Lie would not spout nonsense.
After confirming this matter, Zheng Shuangqi became even more bewildered. He stared at Luo Zheng and asked: "Then how did you do it?"
Zheng Shuangqi was not the only one who was bewildered. Actually, some knowledgeable people had already guessed that Luo Zheng had improved at a godly speed after obtaining the Chaotic Origin Spirit. There must be some secret behind it!
However, there was a limit to the "godly speed" that everyone believed at first. Luo Zheng's speed of progress was only one or two times that of others.
Currently, Zheng Shuangqi was certain that Luo Zheng had reached the Middle Stage Chaotic Origin Realm. How was this a godly improvement?
This was like a thousand miles in a day!
Luo Zheng indifferently glanced at Zheng Shuang Qi. He revealed a meaningful look, "Your Excellency has also stepped into the Chaotic Origin Realm. Why are you asking such a childish question?"
Many Nirvana Realm powerhouses would do anything to become stronger, and everyone more or less had their own secrets, so how could they tell others about it?
Regarding the secrets of the [True Comprehension Chapter], Luo Zheng could contribute it to the entire Greater One Heavenly Palace and contribute to the orthodoxy of the sword path.
But how could he tell the secrets of his fleshly body and Dao Insight to others?
"Childish?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Zheng Shuangqi's eyes flashed with coldness. He said, "Since you don't want to tell me, I'll have to investigate it myself!"
After saying that, Zheng Shuangqi lightly stomped on the ground.
A peculiar ripple appeared on the surface of the entire arena. This seemingly light stomp actually contained a huge force. At this moment, Zheng Shuangqi disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
Even the disciples on the lower level of the arena could not catch sight of Zheng Shuangqi's figure. Only a portion of the disciples on the second level subconsciously raised their heads and stared at Zheng Shuangqi's figure that flew up.
Luo Zheng hesitated for less than half a breath after seeing Zheng Shuangqi disappear. At the next moment, circular ripples appeared on the arena again, and Luo Zheng was right at the center of these ripples!
"He's above!"
Luo Zheng subconsciously raised his head. He frowned.
"It's too late." Gu Lie shook his head.
"That brat can't avoid this stomp." The pale-faced scholar said with certainty.
Long Yi also agreed. "Zheng Shuangqi is serious this time. I'm afraid his Nirvana Force has reached 7,000 Jun. I'm afraid that brat will be crippled by this stomp!"
No matter how talented Luo Zheng was, the gap between him and Zheng Shuangqi was still very large. Unless Luo Zheng could continuously dodge and consume Zheng Shuangqi's strength, he would have a slim chance of winning. However, this was almost impossible.
Luo Zheng had no room to dodge this stomp. The consequences of taking it head-on were not difficult to imagine.
Seeing this scene, the disciples on the first level had different thoughts.
Those who were jealous, such as Fang Yi and the others, had a gloating expression, while Feng Ge and the others were full of worry.
Zheng Shuangqi was the strongest person on the second level. It was too difficult for Luo Zheng to challenge him now.
However, Luo Zheng had no intention of dodging this time!
He slightly tilted his body, and the Nirvana Force released by the Weapon of Civilization was majestic.
Even though the Green Jade Spirit's Weapon of Civilization was limited to the 13th Level World, it could still unleash a force of 8,000 Jun!
Previously, due to the limitations of his fleshy body, he could only unleash such a powerful force in the form of a World Vessel.
But after Luo Zheng stepped into the Middle Stage Chaotic Origin Realm, his Chaotic Origin Spirit could withstand a force of 4,000 to 5,000 Jun. And because of the True Meaning of Dao in his fleshy body, it greatly increased the amount of energy the Chaotic Origin Spirit could contain!
"Hua!"
The Nirvana Force contained within the Weapon of Civilization surged out. The Chaotic Origin Spirit within Luo Zheng's body was almost squeezed to the limit. Many Chaotic Origin Spirit light spots swelled up and protruded on the surface of Luo Zheng's body!
The Chaotic Origin Spirit was on the verge of exhaustion. But at the moment when the Chaotic Origin Spirit fell into exhaustion, the True Meaning of Dao would be imbued into it, maintaining the state of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
"Eight thousand Godly Jun!"
This fist of Luo Zheng struck out towards the bottom of Zheng Shuangqi's feet!
Zheng Shuangqi did not place this fist of Luo Zheng in his eyes. He was thinking of crippling Luo Zheng before finding out the secret of how to improve so quickly from Luo Zheng. If he could truly grasp this secret, he would have a chance of obtaining a seat in the Third Level World. He might even defeat the number one expert of the Third Level World, Li Bianxue who had never been seen before.
But at the moment he stomped down, he felt a vast energy being transmitted from his right foot. This energy was even stronger than his!
"How could this be?" Zheng Shuangqi revealed a look of shock.
"Da da da …"
Those Chaotic Origin Spirits which were originally emitting light within Zheng Shuangqi's body were rapidly extinguished. In the blink of an eye, it spread from his right leg to his entire body!
"Ka ka ka cha!"
The remaining energy that the Chaotic Origin Spirits had yet to dissipate started invading his body.
This energy traveled up along Zheng Shuangqi's right leg. His right leg, right arm, and half of his body were fractured, emitting a series of popping sounds.
Without any room for resistance, he fell outside the stage. It was a tragic sight.

Chapter 3227

At the beginning of each month, the battle would last for several days. The ranking on the ranking stone would also change.

Luo Zheng reflexively thought of the True Comprehension Chapter.

Chapter 3228

Shua shua shua …

Chapter 3229

Luo Zheng and Ning Yudie had been together all these years. He would not be able to get past this.

During this period of time, Luo Zheng was busy running around in the Paramita Realm. He did not have time to return to the Heart Flow Sword Sect to take care of Ning Yudie and the others. A few days ago, Feng Ge said that she would bring Ning Yudie to the Taiyi Palace.

The Raging Inferno was a cultivation method in the Qian Palace. Concubine Yan should not be lying.
But with Concubine Yan's means, she could naturally arrange everything logically.

Chapter 3230

Qian Refining Palace, Chenghuo Hall.
Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin, Xun, Mu Ning, and Han Liusu were all sitting cross-legged. Above their heads hung a red-hot chain that was emitting waves of heat. This was the unique cultivation method of the Heavenly Refining Palace, the Raging Inferno.
After a period of cultivation, Xun, Mu Ning, and Han Liusu also made great progress in their cultivation.
Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin had only been here for about a month.

"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
After Luo Zheng merged according to the order, an accident also occurred. More than 30 types of Taoist Connotation of Divine Paths split up on their own.
Luo Zheng had observed this accident once and was prepared for it. He stretched out his hand and flicked it repeatedly. The more than 30 types of Divine Paths merged into a ball again and converged into the energy of Dao Fusion.
A strand of violet light blossomed …
Luo Zheng stood with the sword in his hand. He then said: "I don't have much time to cultivate the Fusion Deity Sword Scripture. However, this Fusion Deity Sword Scripture is similar to the rhombus energy you created. I can directly borrow your experience of fusing that rhombus energy!"
One had to admit that the talent of Evil Deities in this aspect made Luo Zheng jealous.
Evil Deities had relied on their own instincts to create their own rhombus energy. Even though the rhombus energy created by them did not have any moves, it was far more stable and easier to learn than the < Fusion Deity Sword Scripture > created by Donghuang Tai Yi.
One had to know that Donghuang Tai Yi had spent a lot of time and effort to create a supreme sword scripture like the < Fusion Deity Sword Scripture >. And how long had it been since Evil Deities cultivated the Fusion Deity Sword Scripture and released it into the Chaotic Origin Great World?
There were times when Luo Zheng had the impulse to devour Evil Deities. But when he recalled the warning of the Spirit of Green Jade, Luo Zheng suppressed it.
"Hua!"
The three violet sword lights fused together. This sword light was condensed according to the < Fusion Deity Sword Scripture >. But during the condensation process, Luo Zheng made some changes. He turned the sword light into a rhombus shape. And during the fusion process, Luo Zheng used the Evil Deities' technique to stack them together!
After retrieving Evil Deities into his inner world, Luo Zheng did not just obtain the Paramita Token. He also obtained everything about him, including his memories, experiences, experiences …
But out of respect, Luo Zheng did not take the initiative to pry into the memories of Evil Deities. He only obtained the other party's experience of condensing the rhombus energy.
Tenth level …
Fiftieth level …
Hundredth level …
Ninetieth level …
The more he stacked, the more terrifying the Fusion Deity energy on Luo Zheng's sword became.
Soon, the Fusion Deity energy on the sword was so strong that even Evil Deities were surprised. He could not help but say, "This energy seems to be even sharper when released with the Sword Principle …"
"East Emperor is a supreme expert in the Mother World. He is comparable to the captain of the Kurofune. The < Fusion Deity Sword Scripture > he wrote has its merits. If you want to learn it, I can get it for you," Luo Zheng said.
As a supreme sword scripture, the < Fusion Deity Sword Scripture > could not be passed to outsiders after it was borrowed. But Luo Zheng could borrow it for Evil Deities once, so the divine crystals were not a problem.
After saying that, Luo Zheng suddenly raised his hand.
That sword had already slashed out into the distance. The purple sword light contained the power of nearly 20,000 Jun, and when the sword light flew out, the surrounding space had already been shattered beyond recognition …
Just before the sword light exploded, Luo Zheng made a huge black crack bloom in front of the sword light. It swallowed the sword light completely.
By letting this sword light explode in his inner world, it could easily destroy his inner world.
"In any case, I can't leave this place. What's the point of learning it?" Evil Deities lowered his head and said unhappily.
Luo Zheng looked at Evil Deities indifferently. He then said, "Trapping you here is indeed a waste …"
Hearing this, Evil Deities abruptly raised his head and looked at Luo Zheng with bewilderment.
Luo Zheng flipped his hand and passed over a handful of Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals. At the same time, he said, "200 Chaotic Origin Spirits are enough for you to step into the Chaotic Origin Realm."
Since the Kurofune personally sent Evil Deities to the Chaotic Origin Great World, Evil Deities naturally understood how important the Chaotic Origin Spirits were. After Evil Deities failed, he no longer harbored any hope of stepping into the Chaotic Origin Realm. Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would give him one? This was something that Evil Deities had never thought of.
If Luo Zheng gave Evil Deities other things, Evil Deities would definitely reject it. But the Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals …

Chapter 3235
In the future, the Chaotic Origin Great World would no longer exist. There would be no other way to obtain the Chaotic Origin Spirit. This would most likely be the last chance for him to step into the Chaotic Origin Realm.

After placing the Chaotic Origin Spirit into the fleshy body, it required an array to refine it.

However, the final result was the opposite …

After returning to the Greater One Heavenly Palace, Luo Zheng also took some time to understand the Dao Refinement Clones. It was not a complicated ability, because it could directly control the clones, so it was more practical than the Spirit Dwelling Land.
Refining Evil Deities into an ordinary doppelganger would not be of much use to Luo Zheng. Purely in terms of fleshy body, the Evil Deities were far inferior to him. However, the Evil Deities had some talents that were stronger than him. If he could utilize them properly, it would be a huge help.
After finishing his words, Luo Zheng continued asking, "Are you willing?"
"I don't have a choice." Evil Deities chuckled.
Deep in his heart, the Evil Deities was willing. No matter what, it was better than being trapped in his inner world. Furthermore, the Dao Refinement Clones might not necessarily …
When the thoughts of the Evil Deities reached this point, he stopped. Luo Zheng could pry into his thoughts. Some thoughts were taboo to the current him.
Luo Zheng extended his hand and waved towards that golden fence. Immediately, the entire fence broke apart, and the mountain collapsed.
When the mortals in the nations below saw the mountain collapsing, they revealed expressions of fear and trepidation.
"Lord, I …" Xiuzhu floated in the air and looked at Luo Zheng with a pleading expression.
The living beings in his inner world did not know that there was a heaven beyond heaven. Naturally, they did not have many thoughts.
But once they knew that there was a world beyond their inner world, how could those with lofty aspirations be willing to confine themselves to this world?
Xiuzhu was the woman of the Evil Deities. She had known for a long time that there was a great world outside. However, compared to seeing that great world, she was more willing to follow the Evil Deities.

Luo Zheng stood up and turned around. When he faced an empty space, he waved his hands in the air. Xiuzhu, dressed in white, appeared in the courtyard.
It was Xiuzhu's first time in this world. Her beautiful face was filled with fear, but at the same time, she was filled with curiosity about this world.
"My Lord, you live in a place like this?" Xiuzhu asked.
In Xiuzhu's eyes, Luo Zheng was omnipotent. He was an invincible god. Although this independent courtyard was exquisite, it was still very ordinary. It was not worthy of her Lord's identity. Xiuzhu could not understand this …

Xi Youqin, who was as obedient as a lamb a moment ago, had a thunderous expression on her face. She looked like she was about to explode.

Although Luo Zheng was not afraid that the Evil Deities would escape, before releasing the Evil Deities, he had to make thorough preparations to ensure that nothing would go wrong.

Chapter 3236

The arrays on the Seven Mountains of the Center God Province were activated. All the passes, teleportation arrays, and Realm Walls were strengthened. Those Tai Yiwei experts who were originally staying in the Dragon City were all sent out. It was as though they were facing a great enemy.
Luo Zheng stayed in the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Naturally, he did not notice these changes. In reality, Qiu Yinhe was also rushing around. He was busy with the defense of the Greater One Heavenly Palace.

Chapter 3237

Chapter 3238
The elders of the Land of Sword Grief looked at the arena with a mocking expression.
Not everyone was as monstrous as Luo Zheng, who dared to challenge Chun Hao as soon as he entered the Land of Sword Grief.

As for the new disciples, they carefully stared at Evil Deities on the arena.

As soon as he finished speaking, the Nirvana Realm Token in Fang Wei's body began to move restlessly. The turbulent Nirvana Power began to gather in his body.
Fang Wei did not intend to use his full strength to teach a latecomer a lesson. His full strength was used to charge into the top ten, and this guy in front of him was not qualified to see it!
"You should rejoice that this is the Land of Sword Mourning, and that we're fellow disciples of the Heaven Palace. I won't cripple you. However, if I teach you a lesson, then …."
As Fang Wei spoke, he charged toward Evil Deities.

However, the scene that unfolded before their eyes was different from what they had imagined.
Before Fang Wei could finish speaking, he had already charged in front of Evil Deities. Everyone saw a ball of energy suddenly appear in Evil Deities' hand.
"Taoist Connotation?" A sharp-eyed person immediately recognized it.
"It seems to be a fusion of several types of Shinto, but the power of the fusion is very ordinary …." Someone was puzzled.
"But this ball of Taoist Connotation …."
Just before everyone came to a conclusion, the ball of energy had already exploded in front of Fang Wei.
"Boom!"
A blazing sun bloomed on the battle stage.
Within the blazing sun, there were also bunches of purple energy arrows. The power contained within these energy arrows was also astonishing!
Fang Wei originally wanted to say something, but the exploding energy had forcefully cut off those words. At this moment, Fang Wei had fallen into a state of confusion.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit within his body became fatigued at that moment. His entire fleshy body was sent flying. And during this process, the energy continued corroding his body. His blood, flesh, and bones were all undergoing a huge test at this moment …
"Kuang!"
Eventually, Fang Wei smashed into the side of the bronze building. His entire body was badly mutilated. His injuries were extremely serious.
It was extremely quiet beneath the battle stage.
Even though fists and kicks had no eyes, the gap in strength between the disciples on the First Level was not too big. In addition, everyone had the protection of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. Even if they were injured, they would not be too heavily injured.
Because this was the first time everyone saw someone being injured so severely during a spar.
"Hmph, just like an ant."
The Evil God snorted coldly and walked off the fighting ring without saying a word.
The disciples from the Land of Sword Grief had their mouths agape. They watched in a daze as Evil Deities left. After a long while, someone exclaimed: "Hurry, save him!"
A few old men from the Land of Sword of Grief rushed over and practically pulled Fang Wei down from the wall. Someone used an ability to protect his life force and hurriedly transported Fang Wei away.
The new disciples had a worried look. Evil Deities was ultimately that Evil Deities. But from the looks of it, he should be under the control of Luo Zheng. Hence, his performance was relatively restrained.
If not for that, Fang Wei would not be the only unlucky one today. Everyone present here would be unlucky …
Evil Deities walked to the front of Luo Zheng. He said: "Since you did not stop me, I will personally act to teach him a lesson."
Evil Deities was an intelligent person.
He knew that Luo Zheng was not a bloodthirsty person. Naturally, he did not like to see him casually kill people.
But Fang Wei was a noisy fellow. Making Evil Deities endure was not something he could do. Thus, Evil Deities did his best to teach Fang Wei a lesson without taking his life. He had a very precise grasp of the situation.
"Well done."
Luo Zheng gave his evaluation.
He had long since disliked Fang Wei. Especially after he brought such great benefits to the Land of Sword Grief, this fellow still did not like him.
But back then, on the First Floor, he had set his sights on Chun Hao. This Fang Wei really did not have the qualifications to be Luo Zheng's target.
Today, he had borrowed the hands of Evil Deities to teach this fellow an unforgettable lesson.
Because he had defeated Fang Wei, Evil Deities's name on the Ranking Stone skyrocketed. He had risen from the top 1000 to the top 500.
But everyone who had seen Evil Deities act understood that this fellow was probably on the same level as Luo Zheng. He was completely qualified to crush the First Floor, or even … the Second Floor.
Moreover, this person's attacks were even more ferocious. Everyone was silently vigilant. If by any chance they were chosen as the target of this person's challenge, they had to be careful.
After Evil Deities opened the secret cultivation room, he directly chose to go into seclusion.

Chapter 3239
Luo Zheng did not interfere too much with how Evil Deities cultivated.
After hearing Chou Xue's words, Luo Zheng felt a strand of danger rising in his heart. He needed to improve himself as soon as possible.

Within the Thirteen Heavens …
A Sun Soul was condensing continuously, rapidly turning into a human figure.

It was not easy for him to enter the Paramita Realm. He had more important things to do.
Otherwise, he would have devoured all these living beings.

The green sand stained the body of Evil Deities. Evil Deities could not escape from the Paramita Realm.

A rope wrapped around Evil Deities, firmly binding the Sun Soul of Evil Deities.
Evil Deities' expression didn't change at all. He asked coldly, "What is the meaning of this?"
The leader was overjoyed when he saw how easy it was to catch a big fish. He walked over and said, "With such a powerful Sun Soul, you can refine quite a few Soul Crystals!"
"Refining Soul Crystals?" Evil Deities narrowed their eyes.
According to what the Evil Deities knew, Soul Crystals could not be refined because the memories in the Yang Soul were very difficult to erase. They could only be refined over a long period of time to form a pure Soul Crystal.
He didn't know what these small clans were up to, but they claimed that they could refine soul crystals …
"Yes, you don't have to think too much about it." The creature chuckled. "Everyone, let's carry him back!"
After saying that, these creatures rushed forward and reached out their hands, carrying the Evil Deities on their heads.
Just as they were about to set off, these creatures suddenly felt a sharp pain in their arms. It was as if they had been splashed with sulfuric acid. Their arms were instantly corroded!
Under the stimulation of the intense pain, these creatures subconsciously wanted to throw the Evil Deities down. However, their hands had already melted into the Evil Deities' body. They couldn't throw them away!
"Hua!"
The Evil Deities' body swallowed their arms from top to bottom. They quickly absorbed their heads into their bodies, then their bodies, their legs, their feet …
In just a few breaths' time, the creatures carrying the Evil Deities were completely swallowed up.
Only the outermost creature still had half of his body left. However, he clearly couldn't pull himself out of the Evil Deities' Sun Soul. That creature stammered, "You, you are the legendary Soul Sucking Demon …"
"Congratulations, you got it right." The Evil Deities struggled slightly, and the ropes binding him broke inch by inch. Then, he reached out and grabbed the creature's half of his body. In the blink of an eye, he had completely absorbed it.
In the Mother World, Luo Zheng controlled his every move. The Evil Deities had no chance. They had no choice but to obey.
However, it was different in the Nirvana World. As long as Luo Zheng didn't put the Evil Deities back into his Inner World, he wouldn't be able to access his memories of the Nirvana World!
The Nirvana World could be said to be the Evil Deities' only chance.
After the Evil Deities identified the direction, they ran wildly.
To the west of the Thirteenth Heaven, there were many strongholds of large and small clans. They were densely packed.
The Evil Deities concealed his presence and shuttled through these strongholds. Soon, he found a low building.
At the entrance of this building, there were two humanoid creatures standing guard.
"Who are you?"
One of the humanoid creatures scolded sternly when he saw the Evil Deities.
"The Ship Travels Without Boundaries." The Evil Deities replied.
When the two humanoid creatures heard this Dark Domain, their hearts trembled. They immediately retreated.
Within that low building, there were several people standing guard.
These were the strongest living beings of the 'Black Mountain Sect'. They were considered talented disciples even among the superpowers.
Of course, these living beings were not qualified to join the Black Ship. In reality, those who could be chosen by the Black Ship were one in ten thousand existences.
"Let's see who is here?"
"Tsk tsk tsk, I've heard that you were taken in by your master in the Chaotic Origin Great World?"
"Even the invincible Evil Deities have a hard bone to chew. Hahaha …"
Those few living beings sneered.
These living beings had suffered at the hands of the Evil Deities in the Black Mountain Sect. Two of them had even nearly died in the hands of the Evil Deities. But they did not feel any true resentment in their hearts. The infamous Black Mountain Sect had always been this cruel.

One of the tall and sturdy living beings shook his head, "When you were in the Black Mountain Sect, the Black Ship came more frequently. Previously, after you went to the Chaotic Origin Great World, the Black Ship did not descend even once."
Unquestionably, these living beings were extremely jealous of the Evil Deities.

"You also know that it is when there is a calamity." The tall and sturdy living being said.
"I am currently facing a calamity." The Evil Deities said solemnly.
"You represent the Black Mountain Sect?" The tall and sturdy living being chuckled.
The Evil Deities stood stiffly on the spot for several breaths of time. It was not realistic to expect these living beings to help him. It was already good enough if they did not hit him when he was down.

The Evil Deities did not need the Soul Crystal. He only needed to devour the Sun Souls.

The word 'unity' did not exist in the dictionary of the living beings of the Black Mountain Sect. Activating the communication array was not a difficult matter. The other living beings immediately chose to compete with that tall and sturdy living being.

After saying that, he drilled out of this stronghold.

Chapter 3240
In the Spiritwind Province.
A large black ship streaked across the sky in the blink of an eye.
Following behind the black ship were two gigantic golden Buddha statues. And these two golden Buddha statues were actually carried on the shoulders of a bald giant that was over 10,000 feet tall!
The bald giant's eyes glowed with green light, and a string of huge Buddha beads hung in front of his chest. His upper body was naked, and his muscles bulged like mountains.
"Since you've met me, don't even think about running away! Hand over those things, and I'll let bygones be bygones! "
The bald giant roared.
The voice came out of his mouth, forming a powerful sonic boom.
It just so happened that below the bald giant was a medium-sized country, where several kinds of creatures lived. Most of them were mortals, and their lifespans were no more than a hundred years.
When the sonic boom bloomed from the sky, it was like the end of the world for these ordinary creatures.
Many of them didn't even have the chance to react before they were turned into blood mist and disappeared into thin air.
All the tall buildings, walls, and streets in the country were turned into dust and turned into a desert.
There were many mortals in the mother world, and they lived like ants. It was common for them to die in an instant in a battle between powerful beings.
The thing the bald giant was referring to was the Ring of Descent that the black ship took from Lei Meng.
Since the black ship took the Ring of Descent, Shennong and the You Xiong clan had joined forces to besiege the black ship in the mother world many times.
However, the black ship came and went without a trace. So far, they hadn't been able to destroy a single ship, nor capture a single crew member.
A small sampan was hanging from the stern of the black ship. The sampan was hung with two golden locks. When the black ship moved forward at high speed, the sampan was like a swing in the air.
The one sitting on this constantly swaying sampan was none other than Shen Tu.
After hearing the bald giant's roar, she smiled faintly and said, "What is Senior Fa talking about? I'm afraid this little girl doesn't understand."
Her voice wasn't as explosive as Senior Fa's, but it spread far and wide. Almost half of the creatures in the Spiritwind Province heard this sentence.
Those creatures looked around, trying to find the source of the voice, but where could they find it?

A murderous look flashed across Lord Fa's face. His shoulders shook, and the two Buddha statues on his shoulders flew into the air.

Lord Fa's hands chopped towards the black ship, and the two Buddha statues did the same.
In an instant, countless huge palm prints swept across the sky. There seemed to be tens of millions of them.
These palm prints were chaotic at first, but in the midst of the chaos, they completely overlapped, forming a solid and incomparably powerful palm print.
Looking at the palm print that seemed slow but was actually fast, it slapped down towards the black ship.
Shen Tu's pale face suddenly became excited, as if she wanted to verify something.
"Hahaha …"
The palm print covered with a terrifying pressure descended, and the seemingly old black ship actually made creaking sounds, as if it was about to disintegrate at any moment.
But when the palm print approached the black ship, a circle of silver light suddenly appeared on the surface of the black ship. After the palm print covered the silver light, a palm print sank into the light.
