Fa Daren's broad chest suddenly caved in, and a huge palm print appeared immediately.
He had just slapped out such a terrifying palm print, and it had hit him directly.
Caught off guard, Fa Daren was slapped down from the sky by himself and fell down.
"Honglong …"
His huge body smashed into the ground, creating a huge crater hundreds of thousands of feet wide. Thousands of giant mountains in the surrounding area collapsed one by one, and countless creatures living in this area were killed or injured.
Fa Daren's bronze-colored body was extremely tough. Even if he was hit by this palm, his aura only stagnated for a moment. His huge fist slammed into the ground, and his whole body bounced into the air again.
However, as he looked around, his surroundings were already empty. Where was the figure of the black ship?
…
Shen Tu got up from the small sampan and walked towards the cabin.
Inside the cabin, the 'bamboo pole' complained, "Since we got the Ring of Descent, we haven't stopped. Every few days, we're being hunted down!"
A silver sphere floated above Bamboo Pole's head. This was the latest Paramita Token the black ship obtained, the 'Inch Strike Stone'.
Even though this was the first time he used it, the effect it displayed was outstanding. Shen Tu was very satisfied with it.
"It's not like we haven't experienced such a life before." Shen Tu consoled.
"But we can't use that Ring of Descent! The three Void Devourers who descended have all died. This plaything is flawed. "Bamboo Pole said with dissatisfaction.
"The problem lies with those Golden Chaotic Origin Spirits. Of all things, they were taken away by that brat." Shen Tu sighed.
The black ship had naturally investigated Luo Zheng's identity. Shen Tu even suggested to the captain to go to the Great Yi Heavenly Palace and kidnap Luo Zheng.
But on one hand, entering the lair of the Great Yi Heavenly Palace to kidnap someone wasn't a simple matter. On the other hand, the captain seemed to be afraid of some other things. They could only wait …
But in the process of waiting, they were being chased all the way. Bamboo Pole naturally felt unhappy.
Just as Shen Tu was consoling Bamboo Pole, an inconspicuous communication talisman lit up.
Shen Tu glanced at that communication talisman before revealing a strange expression, "The communication array of the Black Mountain Sect has been activated. Is someone planning to act against the Black Mountain Sect?"
"Who cares if they die?" Bamboo Pole said with dissatisfaction.
The Black Mountain Sect and the black ship did not have a direct relationship. The black ship did not have a need to protect the Black Mountain Sect.
Shen Tu's eyes blinked twice. The figure of the Evil Deities appeared in her mind.
Driven by her intuition, she returned to her room and took out a small bottle. Inside the bottle, there was still a vague figure of the Evil Deities.
Ever since the Evil Deities were absorbed into Luo Zheng's inner world, this figure had not moved for many days. This only proved that the Evil Deities were still alive. But she could not see the current state of the Evil Deities.
Even though Shen Tu had chosen to give up, she would still take out this bottle from time to time to take a look.
"Yi?"
Shen Tu's eyes were filled with bewilderment. The vague figure of the Evil Deities was sitting cross-legged in the bottle. It seemed to be cultivating.
Had he regained his freedom?
No …
Shen Tu immediately rejected this conjecture. If the Evil Deities had regained their freedom, they would have found a way to contact her.
Had he regained his freedom but was controlled by Luo Zheng?
That should be the case …

Chapter 3241
The Fourteenth Layer of Heaven.
In the inner hall of the City Guard Mansion, there was a row of transparent jars, each filled with spirit crystals.
Luo Zheng sat in the form of a Sun Soul, continuously absorbing the spirit crystals.
The Fourteenth Layer of Heaven had completely stabilized.
The two big cities were in the hands of the Weasel Tribe and the Giant Tribe, and these two tribes were completely subservient to Luo Zheng.
It could be said that Luo Zheng did nothing, and the resources of the entire Fourteenth Layer of Heaven would be sent to him.
But Luo Zheng couldn't stay in the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven forever. He needed to climb to a higher level, and strengthening his Sun Soul naturally became an inevitable choice.
Traces of spirit crystals were absorbed by Luo Zheng's soul, and these spirit crystals directly became a part of Luo Zheng's soul. After Luo Zheng's Sun Soul entered the indigo state, it looked deep purple from afar.
As Luo Zheng absorbed more and more spirit crystals, the purple light on the surface of his Sun Soul became more and more intense.
After the row of spirit crystals in the transparent jars were completely absorbed, Luo Zheng got up and asked a Giant to send in another spirit crystal.
Several Giants carried the jars into the inner hall, neatly placed them there, and then respectfully retreated.
Luo Zheng reached out and pointed, and the spirit crystals in the jars turned into a thin line again, and continuously merged into his body.
After Luo Zheng absorbed three jars, he suddenly felt a sense of rejection. The purple light on the surface of his body seemed to have flashed to the extreme, and the purple light was in a saturated state.
Luo Zheng frowned and tried to continue absorbing spirit crystals, but a sense of rejection rose from the bottom of his heart.
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng asked.
Spirit crystals only needed to be continuously absorbed, and as long as enough spirit crystals were obtained, the cultivation of the soul could be rapidly improved.
In the First Layer of Heaven, Luo Zheng had seen spirit crystals for the first time, Ling Shuang had told him so.
But now he couldn't absorb them?
While Luo Zheng was still puzzled, 9527's voice came out, "Your soul has been cultivated to the level of Indigo Soul, and naturally can't be improved."
"Indigo Soul?"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and looked at the purple brilliance on the surface of his palm.
"Yes, the Spirit Crystal does not increase your Sun Soul indefinitely. After absorbing it to a certain extent, it will reach its limit. This limit is called the Indigo Soul Limit," 9527 explained.
Luo Zheng pondered for a moment and asked, "If the Sun Soul is cultivated to the limit here, then wouldn't the souls of Donghuang, Senior Qiu, and Concubine Yan all be at the extreme Indigo Soul?"
Luo Zheng couldn't believe what 9527 said.
Whether it was facing Phoenix or Qiu Yinhe, Luo Zheng felt a lot of pressure. The aura emitted by their souls was much stronger than his, and this couldn't be faked. "In theory, that is true," 9527 replied. It naturally understood Luo Zheng's doubts, and then said, "But after reaching the Indigo Soul Realm, there are other ways to strengthen yourself. When you reach the 29th level of Chang Rong Heaven, you have to enter the Soul City, transform into a soul body, and become a colorless consciousness.
My body has reached a new level. "
"Colorless consciousness?" Luo Zheng heard of it for the first time.
"Yes," 9527 nodded and said, "At that level, you have already broken away from the ordinary level of the soul. The colorless consciousness is much stronger than the soul, and at this level, it is enough to fight against the physical body."
The Sun Soul had always been very oppressed in the Nirvana Realm. No matter which level, any Nirvana Realm creature could do anything to it, and it was almost impossible to resist.
But after reaching the 29th level of Chang Rong Heaven and becoming a colorless consciousness, the situation changed.
Although the physical body was still strong, the soul had transformed into a new form, and it was not without the power to fight back. Some of the more outstanding existences even had the power to kill the Nirvana Realm creatures. "Also, to correct one of your thoughts," 9527 continued, "Concubine Yan, Phoenix, and Qiu Yinhe have all entered the Soul City and cultivated the colorless consciousness. But Donghuang is different. He has entered the 33rd level of Sanqing Heaven, and his soul level is different.
At the same time, his colorless consciousness is stronger. "
"There is another level in the colorless consciousness?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a touch of surprise.
"There is more than one level," 9527 said with a faint smile.
"From what you said, there are many more levels?" Luo Zheng was surprised. "Hey, the 33rd level of Sanqing Heaven is the core of the Nirvana Realm. Every time you walk a level, you will have your own opportunities. Whether it is the physical body or the soul, they will be tortured and become stronger. Those who step into it all have the ability to transcend the world.
No one has entered into Sanqing Heaven, but there is no answer to what level the soul can cultivate and how strong the physical body can be in Sanqing Heaven. You can ask the Spirit of Green Jade, "9527 said.
"Brother 9527 has already explained it in detail," the voice of the Spirit of Green Jade rang out, "Brother 9527 should have entered Sanqing Heaven before."
9527 hesitated for a moment, and then said, "I don't have any memories of Sanqing Heaven, but as far as I know, I should have been to Taiqing Realm."
The 33rd level of Sanqing Heaven was divided into three realms. The outermost realm was Taiqing Realm, the inner realm was Qing Realm, and the innermost realm was Yuqing Realm. The Spirit of Green Jade suddenly sighed faintly. This conversation seemed to evoke an ancient memory. It said, "Back then, we went into Sanqing Heaven hand in hand with Yi King, but we were within three million miles of Taiqing Realm. It is said that we could see the edge of Qing Realm a million miles ahead.
But in the end, we didn't have the fortune to step in … "
"Sanqing Heaven is so dangerous? Can't even go through with the physical body? "Luo Zheng asked.
"Physical body?" The Spirit of Green Jade shook its head and smiled, "In Qing Yuming's era, the Nirvana Realm didn't prohibit the True Meaning of Holy Body. Yi King's physical body was so strong at that time, hehe … he still couldn't move an inch in Sanqing Heaven!"
All the creatures in Qing Yuming had extremely strong physical bodies, not to mention Yi King.
"As far as I know, before our era, no one could enter Qing Realm, let alone the core Yuqing Realm," the Spirit of Green Jade continued.
In other words, Lun Ming had been wandering in the Nirvana Realm for so many chaos eras, but he had only been wandering around the periphery of Sanqing Heaven.
After hearing the Spirit of Green Jade, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Obviously, he was interested in the highest level of the Nirvana Realm.
Since he had swallowed enough soul crystals and reached the extreme of Indigo Soul, it was indeed time to attack a higher level.

Chapter 3242
After making his decision, Luo Zheng once again ordered his subordinates to take away the soul crystals.
A portion of the collected soul crystals were distributed to the disciples on the First Floor of Sword Grief. Even the number one Chun Hao received some. Almost all the disciples on the First Floor had returned to the Fourteenth Heaven.
But Luo Zheng kept a considerable amount of soul crystals for himself. Luo Zheng gave these soul crystals to Luo Yan, Ling Shuang, and Feng Ge. Next, he gave them to the few people from the Nine Li Tribe.
After the soul crystals were taken away, the giants placed stacks of Cutting Body Astral Jades over. And Luo Zheng took this opportunity to leave the Paramita before entering it with his fleshy body.
Pieces of Cutting Body Astral Jades were crushed, absorbed, and refined by Luo Zheng.
Currently, Luo Zheng's fleshy body was divided into several parts. Firstly, it was the strength of his fleshy body. Before reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm, Luo Zheng's fleshy body could be said to be the strongest.
Secondly, it was the Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
Through the True Meaning of Dao born from the Body of Weapon, it allowed Luo Zheng's Spirit of Chaotic Origin to grow rapidly. Currently, Luo Zheng was about to reach the Late Stage Chaotic Origin Realm. The limits of his fleshy body had reached a whole new level!
Thirdly, his fleshy body was reconstructed by the Ancient God, causing his body structure to change. And those bone pieces could greatly strengthen his fleshy body. It was considered another form of improvement. Finally, it was the Sealing Stone Solution. It's a pity that the Sealing Stone Solution was too rare. Luo Zheng had already asked the Eared Mouse Clan to help Luo Zheng search for it. As long as they could find a supreme treasure made from the Sealing Stone Solution, they would help Luo Zheng purchase it regardless of the price. This method of strengthening the fleshy body.
The method was similarly different.
In reality, other than the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, Luo Zheng had rarely seen other methods of strengthening his body.
Hence, using the Cutting Body Astral Jades to strengthen his body should be the most normal method. It's just that currently, only Luo Zheng and Feng Ge could use it.
After absorbing several pieces of Cutting Body Astral Jades, Luo Zheng placed one in his hand. He then asked: "The Sun Soul has the limit of Indigo Soul. Don't tell me the fleshy body has the same limit …"
9527 was silent for a moment, then laughed heartily, "Physical body, hehehehe, of course I have it..."
When the Aragami entered the Nirvana Realm, it would absorb the Body Qi Jade and become stronger. In ancient times, it was known as the Valve Body Realm, which corresponded to the Yang Soul entering Indigo. But after the True Meaning of Strength Crusade Sacred Body was banned, no one could cultivate this Dao anymore. Even the number of living beings who knew about the 'Valve Body Realm' decreased. In the end, it was replaced by the 'Chaotic Origin Realm'. Because the Chaotic Origin Realm was theoretically a way to strengthen oneself.
It was a realm.
However, the laws of the entire Primal Chaos were as such, and there was nothing they could do.
"Don't tell me there's a place like the Soul City in the Nirvana Stratum where we need to strengthen our bodies?" Luo Zheng guessed again.
"You guessed it right again. However, the strengthening of the body is not in the 29th layer of heaven, but in the 30th layer of Yu Sheng-tian. It is called the Land of Retribution," 9527 replied.
"The Land of Bones of Tribulation …" Luo Zheng remembered this name.
No matter what, Luo Zheng would touch on these places sooner or later. Currently, Luo Zheng had to strengthen his fleshy body first.
Pieces of Cutting Body Astral Jades were sent in, and Luo Zheng accepted them all.
The giants who were in charge of using the Body Piercing Jade also revealed envious expressions.
Regardless of whether it was the Soul Crystal or the Cutting Body Astral Jades, they were good things to the Nirvana Stratum beings. But there was a limit to how much they could strengthen themselves.
Restricted by this layer of heaven, they could not grow any further after reaching a certain stage.
The Cutting Body Astral Jades Luo Zheng absorbed were enough to strengthen a hundred giants. And seeing how Luo Zheng did not stop, no one knew how much he would absorb.
"Again!"
After Luo Zheng finished absorbing the Cutting Body Astral Jades in the inner hall, he ordered the giants.
He could feel that he had absorbed quite a lot of Cutting Body Astral Jades, but his fleshy body did not seem to have reached its limit.
One of the giants saluted Luo Zheng respectfully and said, "Lord City Guard, there are not many Cutting Body Astral Jades left in Wanling City …"
"No more?" Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of disappointment.
"Our Giant Tribe and the Luring Weasel Tribe still have some left. If Lord City Guard needs them, I will go to the Blood Roar Tribe Leader …" the giant said again.
The Giant Tribe was able to turn the tables in Wanling City because of Luo Zheng. These giants were cruel, but they were also very honest and loyal. They could give everything to Luo Zheng without holding back.
"No need." Luo Zheng shook his head. He knew that the Giant Tribe would take out all the Cutting Body Astral Jades, but he did not need to do so. "What about Tianshui City?"
"A batch of Cutting Body Astral Jades from Tianshui City has been sent here. Lord City Guard and Lord Feng Ge have refined them. It is estimated that it will be difficult to …" the giant replied.
Compared to the crystallization of souls, Cutting Body Astral Jades were more popular. After all, ordinary Nirvana Realm creatures would not climb to a higher level. They needed Cutting Body Astral Jades to strengthen themselves, so there were not many left.
"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded.
After he got up, he went to another inner hall.
In this inner hall, Feng Ge was also absorbing Cutting Body Astral Jades.
After Feng Ge absorbed Cutting Body Astral Jades, he could only strengthen the fleshy body of the Pure One in the Nirvana Realm. Feng Ge in the mother world could not benefit, but it was also beneficial to improve the fleshy body of the Pure One. Feng Ge would not slack off.
She noticed Luo Zheng coming in and crushing a piece of Cutting Body Astral Jade. After absorbing it, she smiled and said, "Luo Zheng, the Cutting Body Astral Jades you asked me to refine are all gone!"
Feng Ge felt that the fleshy body of the Pure One had become stronger. She also hoped to refine more Cutting Body Astral Jades.
After Luo Zheng rejected her in Taiyi Heaven Palace, Feng Ge's mind was calm. After all, she knew Luo Zheng's thoughts and understood that this matter could not be rushed. In turn, Feng Ge comforted her father …

"Then we have to leave the Fourteenth Heaven?" Feng Ge's eyes flashed.
It was unrealistic to stay in the Fourteenth Heaven. She hoped to go to a higher level with Luo Zheng.
"Before we leave, there are two more things," Luo Zheng said again.
The Blackfly Bug seemed to have a strong attraction to the Pure One. Luo Zheng judged that swallowing the Blackfly Bug was originally a means to enhance the ability of the Pure One.

As for how to elegantly swallow these bloody Blackfly Bugs, it was not a big problem. The attraction of the Blackfly Bug came from the essence and blood in its body. After the bug's body was peeled off, the essence and blood were absorbed.

Chapter 3243

。

The Heaven Lord of the Fourteenth Heaven had stayed in the Hidden Domain for so many years and had devoured a considerable number of Darkflybugs, but he had not grown to the point where he could not match him. This meant that devouring one or two Darkflybugs would not have much of an effect.
Feng Ge stuck out her tongue. Although the Darkblight Bugs were very attractive, she still felt a sense of disgust when she thought about swallowing a lot of bugs and drinking their blood.
The Luring Weasel Tribe had already arranged the Nirvana Realm tokens they had collected during this period of time.

The Ear Mice were geniuses who were good at trading. They were also good at identifying Nirvana Realm tokens.

Feng Ge stood beside Luo Zheng and looked at the statue with interest. He asked, "What ability does this Nirvana Realm token have?"
"After this Nirvana Realm token has been integrated, it cannot be activated. It cannot be used normally either. It can only be activated after death," the Ear Mouse answered.

Luo Zheng had seen similar abilities before. Some of the Hermit's supernatural powers could only be activated after death. But what was the use of activating it when the person was already dead?
Feng Ge shrugged. It could only be activated after death. No matter how powerful this Nirvana Realm token was, it did not have much meaning.
The Ear Mouse noticed Feng Ge's expression and added, "Don't underestimate the ability of the Three-Eyed Dragon Life Sparrow. After death, it will enter a state of anger after being activated. It will kill and devour the person who killed you. It is extremely powerful!"

"Yes. Not to mention the body being reduced to pieces, even the treasures, weapons, and Sumeru Ring that came with the body can be restored after being reduced to pieces. So strictly speaking, the ability of the Three-Eyed Dragon Life Sparrow is not resurrection, but complete recovery!" the Ear Mouse said.
The ability of the Nirvana Realm token was originally extremely powerful.
For example, the Golden Abacus of Martyrdom. Not to mention the "Mountain" whose cultivation base was still low, even if it encountered the strongest in the Mother World, the Golden Abacus of Martyrdom could still seal the Nirvana Realm token no matter how powerful it was.
These unique abilities were often unreasonable.
"If I fuse with this Nirvana Realm token, won't I become immortal?" Feng Ge asked. Obviously, he was interested in this Nirvana Realm token.
"Of course not. Squeak squeak. Every time I die, the eye on the Three-Eyed Dragon Life Sparrow will be blinded once. After three times, this Nirvana Realm token will be completely destroyed and can no longer be used," the Ear Mouse said. "But even so, this Nirvana Realm token is very powerful.
However, there are no weak Nirvana Realm tokens. "
"If it can restore the body infinitely, I'm afraid it has completely surpassed the level of Nirvana Realm, right?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Even the Accomplice's Heart, which was a powerful Nirvana Realm token, had a great limitation. One had to sacrifice one's life, and it was a one-time use.
After fusing with the Three-Eyed Dragon Life Sparrow, it would become immortal and could even devour the opponent. It was so powerful that Luo Zheng found it hard to believe.

Chapter 3244

In the outside world, the Evil Deities had become Luo Zheng's Tao Refinement Avatar. Luo Zheng could interfere at any time. However, it was different in the Nirvana Realm. Luo Zheng could not control the movements of the Evil Deities and could only let them roam freely.
In the Color Realm, each layer of heaven was connected by a star ring. Even each clan had an independent star ring. The situation in the Desire Realm was similar.
In the north of the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven, the star rings were neatly arranged.

Chapter 3245

They were surrounded by low barren mountains that were three to five hundred feet high. There was nothing special about the surrounding scenery.
As they walked, Luo Zheng also checked the map, but it still didn't match the map in his hand.
Under such a check, the two of them slowed down their speed.
After a day and a night, they only walked 50 miles in a straight line to the south. There was still a long way to go, not to mention the Seventeenth Heaven was vast.
It would take a long time to finish walking step by step and check the map.

Hearing Feng Ge's words, Luo Zheng was silent for a few breaths. He looked helpless. It turned out that Feng Ge had already calculated that he wouldn't be able to find it …
Speaking of which, whether it was the Sky Sunflower Temple or the Sky Temple, Luo Zheng didn't rely on the map to find it. This was Luo Zheng's first time looking for a temple, so he didn't have much experience.
According to Feng Ge, after walking south for about a hundred miles, the continuous low mountains disappeared. A large grassland appeared, and on both sides of the grassland stood a huge fence.
"How can these fences be built so high? And the grass inside is also …" Luo Zheng looked up, and his eyes were slightly surprised.
The fence at the edge of the grassland was thousands of feet high. Compared to these fences, the low mountains in front of them were simply low mounds of earth.
As for the grassland, how was it grassland? Every blade of grass was dozens of feet high, swaying like a big tree. In comparison, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were as small as ants.
Feng Ge shook his head. Although he had done his homework, there were too many things in the Paramita Realm, including almost everything. It was already good enough that he knew that the Elephant-Men were in the south.
"Let's go in and take a look," Luo Zheng said.
After crossing the fence, the two entered the grassland. After walking for about ten miles in this huge grassland, they suddenly heard a long bugle horn.
"Wu …"
As soon as the bugle horn sounded, the ground trembled as if there was an earthquake.
Feeling this violent tremor, Luo Zheng frowned and looked back at Feng Ge. With a light pull of his hand, the lightning wings on his back flashed out and circled up.
When Luo Zheng reached a height of thousands of feet, he finally saw the situation in the distance.
Black bulls that were twenty to thirty thousand feet tall were running wildly on this grassland. Behind the black bulls was a group of humanoid creatures that were thousands of feet tall.
These humanoid creatures stood on two feet, but they had a head that was no different from an elephant's. Their skin was also ash-green. They were clearly the Elephant-Men that Feng Ge had mentioned.
The Elephant-Men chased after the black bulls, causing the earth to tremble.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge floated in the air. To the Elephant-Men, they were really like two mosquitoes.
Under the pursuit of the Elephant-Men, the black bulls were already charging towards the fence. Although the fence was tall, it shouldn't be able to stop the charge of the black bulls.
But just as the black bulls were about to reach the fence, one of the Elephant-Men raised the bugle horn in his hand.
"Wu wu wu …"
When the bugle horn sounded, lightning-like patterns appeared on the surface of the seemingly ordinary fence.
"Bzzt bzzt bzzt …"
Thick bolts of lightning gathered on the surface of the fence. These bolts of lightning formed lightning chains that wrapped around the black bulls.
When the black bulls were struck by the lightning, they were immediately unable to move and lay on the ground.
"What a powerful lightning power." Luo Zheng looked at the lightning escaping from the fence and clicked his tongue in wonder.
"It's Thunderstruck Wood. It's also a relatively rare Nirvana token." Feng Ge said, "I didn't expect there to be so many Thunderstruck Wood in the Elephant-Men's territory …"

Chapter 3246
Standing behind Luo Zheng and Feng Ge was a huge elephant-man.
The elephant-man seemed to be furious at the arrival of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge. As soon as he finished speaking, a huge hand slapped down on Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's heads.
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng flipped over and protected Feng Ge, but the slap caused the two of them to fall to the ground.

"Dong dong dong dong dong …"

After he stomped more than ten times, another elephant-man finally persuaded, "Okay, Hu Fu, that spy has been stomped into meat paste by you!"
The elephant-man called Hu Fu snorted coldly and walked towards the black bulls.

Using the lightning contained in the Thunderstruck Wood, they could easily take down these black bulls. These black bulls were half of their tribe's food.
Just as Hu Fu turned his head, he suddenly heard a thunderous sound. It was not loud, but it was clear.
"Crackle …"

"Friend …" Luo Zheng said.
He did not come to the elephant-men's territory to cause trouble, but to understand the terrain of the Seventeenth Layer of Heaven. Since he was smashed by Hu Fu, Luo Zheng suppressed the anger in his heart.

The elephant-man called Hu Fu had no interest in communicating with Luo Zheng. He once again waved his huge fist and smashed it towards Luo Zheng's head.
Hu Fu's fist was seventy to eighty zhang wide. To Luo Zheng, it was like a flying hill.
Seeing the mountain-like fist flying towards him, Luo Zheng frowned and finally showed anger on his face.
If he did not teach them a lesson, they would not talk nicely.
When the huge fist smashed in front of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng also punched back. Luo Zheng's fist was three thousand times bigger than Hu Fu's!
It was like a human's fist hitting a flying mosquito!
Hu Fu's eyesight was very good. Even though Luo Zheng was very small in his field of vision, he could still see Luo Zheng's movements clearly.
A mosquito and an ant's fist? There was a mocking look in his eyes.
But when Hu Fu's fist smashed in front of Luo Zheng, he suddenly felt a dull pain coming from the front of his fist.
With a muffled sound, his fist was actually blocked by an almost inaudible mosquito!
"Impossible …"
Hu Fu's eyes were full of disbelief.
The elephant-men had large bodies and were arrogant. They were especially confident in their size and strength. How could he allow a mosquito to block his attack?
"Buzz buzz …"
The muscles on Hu Fu's right arm swelled and his strength continued to erupt, trying to suppress the mosquito in front of his fist.
But that inaudible mosquito was like a wall that could not be crossed.
The other elephant-men were originally rushing towards the black bulls, but when they heard the movement behind them, they all turned around. Seeing Hu Fu's actions, the elephant-men all had strange expressions on their faces.
"What's wrong, Hu Fu?" The elephant-man chief asked.
From their point of view, it was indeed difficult to see Luo Zheng who was as small as a mosquito.
Hu Fu's face turned red. He had used up all the strength in his body. He gritted his teeth and said, "This little thing is so strong …"
Hu Fu said as he pulled back his fist and once again smashed it towards Luo Zheng.
"Hu!"
"Boom!"
Once again, the fist smashed into an invisible wall. The air in all directions surged, and the green grass, which was hundreds of feet tall, fell down. However, Luo Zheng was still able to withstand the punch.
In order to receive Hu Fu's fist, Luo Zheng drew out the power of Shen Jun and spread it out into a fist that was over a hundred meters tall.
If he had only gathered his strength in his body to receive Hu Fu's fist, he was afraid that he would have been like a steel needle that drilled into Hu Fu's fist …
"I don't believe it!"
Hu Fu roared and his fists rained down on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng stood in the air and slightly raised one hand. The invisible power of Shen Jun spread out and slightly distorted the surrounding transparent space, forming a tall wall of strength that blocked all of Hu Fu's attacks.
Luo Zheng planned to wait until the elephant-man's strength was exhausted before communicating with him.
But at this moment, Feng Ge on the ground suddenly warned, "Luo Zheng, behind …"
Although he warned, Feng Ge wasn't worried about Luo Zheng's safety.
The elephant-man race had a height of over a thousand meters, which was indeed terrifying. But they were only creatures of the seventeenth light domain. Even if they were strong, they wouldn't be ridiculously strong. They shouldn't be able to do anything to Luo Zheng.
Just as Luo Zheng turned around, he saw a huge hand grabbing towards him. This huge hand covered Luo Zheng and pinched him. This elephant-man actually wanted to crush him …
The elephant-man kneaded and pinched him, feeling as if he was pinching a fine and hard stone. How could he crush him?
As this thought flashed through his mind, he gently threw Luo Zheng away. At the same time, he pulled out a stone spear from his back and swung it in a circle before accurately thrusting it towards Luo Zheng!
"Ka!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body suddenly expanded.
In an instant, his eight chi tall body had grown to over a hundred meters tall.
While blocking the stone spear, Luo Zheng's body was still expanding crazily. In the blink of an eye, he had grown from over a hundred meters tall to over a thousand meters tall. At the same time, he grabbed the tip of the stone spear with one hand and frowned at the two elephant-men in front of him.
The group of elephant-men surrounding the black bull looked on helplessly as Luo Zheng's body expanded from the size of a mosquito to about the same size as them. They were all dumbfounded.
Their huge protruding ears drooped on both sides and their eyes were filled with disbelief.
"Friends, if you insist on having your way and want to exchange pointers with me, I can only accompany you!"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he suddenly pulled the stone spear out of the elephant-man's hand. After swinging it in a circle in the air, he suddenly exerted strength with his arm!
"Ka ka ka …"
The thousand meter long stone spear was actually crushed by Luo Zheng's hand!
This was a stone spear made of solid white stone. It was extraordinarily sturdy, and there weren't many of them in the elephant-man race …
Seeing the strength Luo Zheng displayed, the elephant-men swallowed their saliva.

Chapter 3247
Luo Zheng turned his head and glanced at the Elephant-Man Chief. He said coldly, "Now you know it's a misunderstanding, but you wanted to kill me just now?"
Hearing this, the Elephant-Man Chief said, "Hu Fu, quickly apologize to this friend."
Hu Fu looked at Luo Zheng, who suddenly grew bigger, and still had a silly expression on his face. He had never experienced the era of the Aragami, so he naturally did not know about this kind of magical power. A mosquito could become as big as him in the blink of an eye …
However, Luo Zheng suddenly becoming a thousand feet tall was already shocking enough. Hu Fu was no longer dissatisfied. He rolled up the elephant trunk on his face and sincerely apologized.
Luo Zheng did not care too much. He bent down and gently cupped Feng Ge in his hand, then gently placed him on his right shoulder.
Although Feng Ge had heard of the Aragami, he had never seen Luo Zheng display it. When he saw Luo Zheng transform into a thousand feet tall giant, he was also dumbfounded.
Sitting on Luo Zheng's shoulder and looking at Luo Zheng's head that was like a tall building, he had a blank expression on his face for a long time.
Since the misunderstanding had been cleared, these Elephant-Men's attitude towards Luo Zheng was naturally much more polite.
The other Elephant-Men began to deal with the black bulls that were killed by the thunderstruck wood. The Elephant-Man Chief introduced himself to Luo Zheng. "I'm Lei Qiao, the Chief of the Elephant-Man Tribe. You shouldn't be from the Nirvana Realm, right? However, the woman on your shoulder seems to be from the human race … "
The Elephant-Man Tribe was a very closed race. They did not communicate much with the outside world. However, as the Chief, he had stayed in the Seventeenth Layer of Heaven for so long, so he knew much more than ordinary Elephant-Men.
He noticed the woman on Luo Zheng's shoulder and could clearly see her appearance. She was from the Nirvana Realm …
"That's right. She's from the Nirvana Realm."
Luo Zheng said while paying attention to the Elephant-Man Chief's expression.
He believed that the seventeenth layer of heaven had clues about the Nirvana Human Tribe, and the Nirvana beings in the seventeenth layer of heaven might know something, so he purposely called Feng Ge a Nirvana Human Tribe to see the elephant-man chief's reaction.
However, Lei Qiao's expression didn't change. He only responded with an 'oh' before asking, "May I know why Your Excellency has come to our territory?"
"I'm here to find a map of the Seventeenth Layer of Heaven. I wonder if the Elephant Human Race can provide me with a copy? I can use Soul Pills or Body Cleansing Jade to buy it," Luo Zheng said.
Lei Qiao was not stupid. When Luo Zheng mentioned the map, his face showed an expression of understanding, but he still asked, "Your Excellency, are you here for the Seventeenth Heaven Temple?"
The Paramita Key only marked the location of the temple on the map, and the map didn't even have any indication of the location. If there wasn't a big map to compare, he could only explore bit by bit.
After being guessed by Lei Qiao, Luo Zheng's expression didn't change.
"We don't have a map of the entire Seventeenth Layer of Heaven, but we do have a map of the south. I'll bring Your Excellency back to the tribe to get it," Lei Qiao said with a wave of his hand. "As for Your Excellency buying it from us, you're treating us as outsiders!"
The elephant-man chief was rather forthright.
After the elephant-men flipped the black bulls over, they pierced their necks with stone spears and strung the black bulls up. They formed groups of two and carried seven or eight black bulls forward.
Luo Zheng also followed this bumper harvest team and traveled southward along the central part of the grassland.
The grassland in the southern part of the Nirvana Realm stretched as far as the eye could see. After entering the hinterland of the grassland, one could only see that the surroundings were lush green, as if there was no end to it.
Feng Ge sat on Luo Zheng's shoulder, as if she was sitting on top of a mountain. This feeling was strange and novel.
She knew that her father, Fuxi, and the others were able to become very large because they had once cultivated using the Ancient God Body Tempering Technique. After that, there was basically no one who cultivated like this. Feng Ge even suspected that Luo Zheng didn't come from this era …
After traveling along the grassland for more than half a day, low houses appeared in front of them.
Of course, these houses were "low" in comparison to the enormous bodies of the elephant-men. Their absolute height was still more than ten thousand feet, but the appearance of these houses was very simple and crude.
Luo Zheng didn't have much contact with the elephant-men, but he could roughly make his own judgment. The elephant-men should be a native race born in the Nirvana Realm.
If the creatures in the Primal Chaos experienced a long period of development, even if they moved to the Nirvana Realm, they would still have a relatively developed civilization.
Native races like the human race didn't have much contact with the outside world and had always been isolated in a corner. Naturally, they were very backward. Therefore, their food, clothing, and weapons were very simple and crude. They only had strength and huge bodies.
Lei Qiao and the others returned to the tribe with their spoils of war. A large number of elephant-men came out of the low houses to welcome them. Most of the elephant-men who stayed in the tribe were old, young, sick, and weak.
They relied on the strong young men in the tribe to hunt for rations.
Many elephant-men were surprised by Luo Zheng's arrival. Some young elephant-men hid in the corners of the houses and cast curious and slightly uneasy gazes at Luo Zheng.
"Your Excellency, please follow me. I will bring you to get the map," Lei Qiao said.
Luo Zheng followed behind Lei Qiao and came to a house in the center of the tribe. Although this house was still simple and crude, it was several times more spacious than the other houses.
After entering the house, they saw an elephant-man sitting cross-legged in the middle of the house. The elephant-man was covered in bright-colored oil paint from head to toe. The oil paint was applied very evenly, and one couldn't even see the original skin of the elephant-man.
"This is our High Priest Lei Gu," Lei Qiao introduced.
The High Priest's position was obviously higher than Chief Lei Qiao's. After Lei Qiao entered, he bowed to the High Priest Lei Gu and said, "High Priest, this foreign friend would like to look at the map of the south."
The High Priest Lei Gu sized up Luo Zheng. He and Chief Lei Qiao had controlled the tribe together for many years and trusted Chief Lei Qiao very much.
The High Priest just nodded and didn't ask any more questions. He got up and took out a wooden board.
After flipping the wooden board over, a map of different heights was carved on the front of the board. Then, the High Priest Lei Gu retreated to the side and closed his eyes again.
"This is the south of the Seventeenth Heaven. We are here," Lei Qiao pointed at the bottom of the map and said. It was the hinterland of the grassland.
Luo Zheng looked around and realized that more than half of the grassland was surrounded by fences made of thunderstruck wood. It wasn't easy to build such a large fence.
He didn't know if it was built by the elephant-men or by other creatures.
Each Heaven had its own secrets. This involved the secrets of the elephant-men. Although Luo Zheng was curious, he didn't ask too much. His purpose was to find the location of the temple.

Chapter 3248
His eyes scanned from top to bottom, checking and comparing the areas one by one.
The terrain of the grassland area in the south wasn't complicated, so Luo Zheng skipped over it and focused on the four corners of the map.
After about half an hour, Luo Zheng finished the comparison. His brows, which were originally relaxed, were now furrowed. Luo Zheng's target wasn't on this map.
Chieftain Lei Qiao noticed the expression on Luo Zheng's face and asked, "Did you not find it?"
This map only recorded the southern area of the Fourteenth Heaven, so it was normal that he couldn't find it. Luo Zheng nodded. Since he couldn't find it here, he could only search elsewhere.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to leave, Feng Ge, who was on his shoulder, suddenly said, "Wait a minute, Luo Zheng, look at the rightmost part of the map."
As the difference in size between Feng Ge and Luo Zheng was too big, his voice was really like the buzzing of a mosquito. Feng Ge realized that Luo Zheng couldn't hear him clearly, so he used his strength to repeat loudly.
When Luo Zheng heard Feng Ge's size, he looked at the left side of the map.
On the leftmost part of the map, the grassland area came to an abrupt end and was replaced by a desert. Perhaps because of the desert, the map drawn on the wooden board wasn't very accurate. The carvings were blurry, and there was a hint of ambiguity.
But even so, at the intersection of the grassland and the desert, a gourd shape was outlined, and it was a three-bellied gourd.
"Look at the left side of the map in your hand. Isn't there a wave?" Feng Ge said again.
After Luo Zheng looked at the map formed by the Paramita Key in his hand, his eyes flashed slightly. This wave was exactly the same as the half gourd shape below!
He even pressed his palm down and compared it to that direction. After careful comparison, the more he looked, the more similar it was!
"Feng Ge is still the most attentive," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"That's right," Feng Ge said with a sweet smile.
When Luo Zheng noticed this direction, Chief Lei Qiao, who had been standing next to him, suddenly had an unnatural expression on his face.
Luo Zheng pointed in that direction and asked, "Chief, this direction should be out of your Elephantmen's territory. Do you know where we are?"
"This place …" Lei Qiao looked hesitant.
Luo Zheng looked at Lei Qiao strangely. Was there something special about this place?
The High Priest suddenly opened his eyes and said, "Foreign race, I advise you to give up on going there. That's not a territory you can set foot in …"
"Why?"
Of course, Luo Zheng wouldn't back down just because of the High Priest's words.
"That place is very dangerous, very dangerous. Even the most powerful Heavenly Lord of our Seventeenth Heaven has fallen there!" The High Priest said.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was slightly shocked. Feng Ge also frowned.
With the strength of the Pure Witches, it was easy for them to walk in the Nirvana Realm. This place seemed normal, but even three Pure Witches had died?
"Even the Heavenly Lord had fallen there? Moreover, three of them had fallen? How is this possible … "Luo Zheng couldn't believe it.
"Hehe, the young people naturally don't know about this part of history, but an old man like me still remembers it clearly. Back then, they fought fiercely in the Seventeenth Heaven for so many years to fight for the position of Heavenly Lord. No matter which side won, they would go there immediately!"
As the High Priest spoke, he pointed to the rightmost side of the map. "They were all there for that place. That place was too dangerous, so they wanted to borrow the strength of the Heavenly Lord. Unfortunately … the Heavenly Lord didn't have the ability to enter it!"
"Who are they?" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and asked.
"I don't know." The High Priest shook his head. "Back then, I was only a child. At that time, our Elephantmen often suffered unexpected disasters. It was only when they took turns to fail three times and finally chose to give up that our tribe settled down …"
Speaking of that memory, there was a bitter taste in the High Priest's mouth.
"Based on what you said, there was more than one force?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, there were at least three forces," the High Priest replied.
To be able to directly compete for the position of Heavenly Lord, such a force was probably the most powerful civilization in the Nirvana Realm!
Luo Zheng thought of the One-Eyed Clan and the black monkey. Apart from that, the Three-eyed Boy of the Thirteenth Heaven seemed to be from another force. Could it be those three forces?
"So you'd better not go. There's no chance of survival there!" the High Priest warned.
Although he hadn't seen Luo Zheng's skills, the High Priest understood that Luo Zheng was no ordinary person. But so what if he was powerful? How powerful could he be compared to those Heavenly Lords?
"Thank you for the reminder. If it's too dangerous, it's not worth the risk." Luo Zheng cupped his hands toward the High Priest.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Feng Ge, who was on his shoulder, had a strange expression on his face.
Under such circumstances, Luo Zheng couldn't possibly back down …
After that, Luo Zheng didn't stay in the Elephantmen Tribe for long. Although Chief Lei Qiao urged them to stay, Luo Zheng still left with Feng Ge.
Not long after leaving the Elephantmen Tribe, Luo Zheng's figure quickly shrank. At the same time, he grabbed Feng Ge and landed on a blade of grass.
The weight of the two was like a drop of dew, causing the grass to bend.
"Are you really planning to leave?" Feng Ge looked doubtful.
"Of course not." Luo Zheng shook his head, his eyes revealing a deep look. "But the Elephantmen Tribe doesn't seem to want us to go in."
"They don't want us to? Do they have the right to stop us? "Feng Ge said calmly.
Not to mention Luo Zheng, even if Feng Ge was only a thousandth the size of the Elephantmen, he could still kill them.
"Of course they can't stop us, but I suspect that the Elephantmen Tribe is related to those large civilizations. Or rather, the Elephantmen were left here by those civilizations for the purpose of being an eye," Luo Zheng said.
Previously, Luo Zheng had suspected that the fences were not built by the Elephantmen. Now, it seemed that the purpose of these fences was most likely to demarcate their territory and enclose the Elephantmen Tribe in the grassland.
"That's just your inference," Feng Ge said.
"Regardless of whether this is possible or not, we'll go around this tribe before going in. It's much safer this way," Luo Zheng replied.
"That's true …"
After this brief planning, Luo Zheng brought Feng Ge across the northern grassland and went straight to the area marked on the map.
In the Elephantmen Tribe.
Not long after Luo Zheng left, the originally calm High Priest suddenly said, "Lei Qiao, quick, quickly take the Celestial Spirit Pearl!"
Hearing this request, Lei Qiao's body trembled slightly.
The Celestial Spirit Pearl was a sacred object of the Elephantmen Tribe. It had always been placed on top of the tribe's totem pole and was worshipped by the Elephantmen Tribe's descendants.
The pearl had been placed on the totem pole for countless years. The Elephantmen Tribe was able to grow to their current size because of the Celestial Spirit Pearl.
Now, the High Priest wanted to take the pearl down?

Chapter 3249
Other than the high priest, no one in the Elephantman tribe knew the origin of the Heavenly Spirit Pearl.
As for the true use of the Heavenly Spirit Pearl, no one knew either.
However, since the high priest had given such an order, Lei Qiao did not refuse.
After it left the door, it went straight to the totem pole and began to climb to the top of the totem pole.
"Chief …"
"You!"
"That's our sacred object. Chief, how can you …"
The other Elephantmen wanted to stop Lei Qiao. Even the chief could not touch the Heavenly Spirit Pearl as he wished.
"Taking down the sacred object is our mission." The high priest's voice suddenly sounded.
In the Elephantman tribe, the high priest's orders were the golden rule. However, the high priest rarely managed the tribe. Most of the time, it was the chief who did it on his behalf.
"Why?" an Elephantman asked, puzzled.
"Because the sacred object was left here to wait for some living beings to come. Now … they have come," the high priest said seriously.
"High priest, are you referring to that human?" an Elephantman asked.
The high priest nodded.
He knew very well that the Elephantman tribe was an insignificant existence. Before their mission was completed, perhaps they still had a need to exist.
Once this mission was completed, they would be dispensable. The final outcome was obvious. However, no matter what the outcome was, they could not defy it.
After taking down the Heavenly Spirit Pearl, the high priest placed it on the ground.
The other Elephantmen stared at the Heavenly Spirit Pearl with shining eyes. Only the high priest could use the pearl's power. They wondered what kind of spell the high priest would use.
Just as everyone was looking forward to it, the high priest suddenly reached out and pulled out the stone spear from Lei Qiao's back.
"Whoosh!"
The stone spear was swung in a circle and heavily smashed toward the Heavenly Spirit Pearl.
"Ah!"
"High priest!"
"You can't …"
At this moment, even Lei Qiao was stunned. However, it was too late to stop it. He could only watch as the stone spear flew toward him.
"Clang!"
The stone spear hit the Heavenly Spirit Pearl head-on. With a crash, the Heavenly Spirit Pearl was smashed into pieces by the stone spear, turning into translucent white fragments …
All the Elephantmen looked at the High Priest in horror. They didn't know why the High Priest would go crazy and smash the Elephantmen's holy item.
Just as the entire tribe was in a state of panic, the white particles on the ground suddenly changed. They trembled slightly, as if tiny ants were beginning to move.
These white shards might be very small for the Elephant-Men, but the Celestial Spirit Pearl itself had a diameter of seventy to eighty feet. Each of the translucent white shards was the size of an ordinary person's fist.
The white fragments continued to rearrange themselves, quickly forming a large circular array. Then, all the white fragments released a powerful energy, and a unique array was activated!
"Whoosh —"
A white ray of light shot up into the sky from the grassland!
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were traversing across the grassland. Seeing the beam of light in the distance, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly stopped.
"It's the Elephantman Tribe's territory," Feng Ge said seriously.
"It doesn't look like someone has ascended," Luo Zheng pursed his lips.
The white ray of light that shot up was similar to a star ring. Just as Luo Zheng left, a beam of light shot up. This couldn't be a coincidence …
"It looks like the problem is most likely due to the Paramita Key in your hands," Feng Ge said.
According to the Elephantmen, the dangerous area at the edge of the grassland wasn't very secretive. At least the major forces knew of its existence, but they couldn't do anything about it.
"That's most likely the case." Luo Zheng looked at the white light expressionlessly. The light gradually dissipated and disappeared from his sight. "We have to speed up …"
"Crack!"
Accompanied by the electric light released by the lightning wings, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge turned into lightning and flew along the grassland that looked like a green ocean.
The white light kept going up and quickly entered the Eighteenth Layer of Heaven.
There was a small formation hidden at the top of a tall mountain in the Eighteenth Layer of Heaven. The moment the white light entered the Eighteenth Layer of Heaven, this formation also released a white light towards the Nineteenth Layer of Heaven.
In a valley in the Nineteenth Layer of Heaven, a small formation activated …
The Twentieth Layer of Heaven …
The Twenty-First Layer of Heaven …
The Twenty-Second Layer of Heaven …
…
The Thirtieth Layer of Heaven …
The Thirty-First Layer of Heaven …
The Thirty-Second Layer of Heaven!
Inside a huge palace, there was a huge metal formation.
This metal formation was constantly rotating. All kinds of patterns intersected within it, and terrifying energy surged within it.
"Crack …"
When the white light entered the Thirtieth Layer of Heaven, the metal formation suddenly shook.
"Hmm?"
A small ball of light made a strange sound.
Inside this ball of light, there was blue water light rippling. The water light condensed into slender ice crystals.
It was a member of the Elephantmen, and also the weakest of the Elephantmen, which was why it was sent here to guard this metal formation.
"The Seventeenth Layer of Heaven's formation activated? What could there be in the Seventeenth Layer of Heaven … "This water spirit pondered.
Generally speaking, the Elephantmen would only focus on the Twenty-Ninth Layer of Heaven and above. There really wasn't anything below the Twenty-Ninth Layer of Heaven that the Elephantmen would pay attention to.
A few days ago, they had received news that two Pure Witches had appeared in the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven. Moreover, these two Pure Witches belonged to the human race.
Even so, the Elephantmen had only issued a decree and ordered those lowly black monkeys to take action. The Elephantmen didn't take any further action because there was no need to.
But it didn't expect that the Seventeenth Layer of Heaven's formation would also activate not long after, so the water spirit was naturally very puzzled.
The Seventeenth Layer of Heaven …
Could it be that danger zone?
Blue light suddenly spread out from the body of this "water spirit".
The light on the surface of its body spread out in a circle, and then began to flash to its companions.
The Elephantmen of the Elephantmen were all individuals, and at the same time, they could rely on and integrate with each other. The instant it called out to its companions, it had already adjusted its voice to the highest level.
Therefore, in an instant, all the Elephantmen who could respond to the "water spirit" immediately gathered within its body.
Within the body of this "water spirit", specks of dazzling golden light and balls of blazing flames floated.
"What's wrong?"
"This looks like news from the lowest level …"
"Hmph, it's just news from the Seventeenth Layer of Heaven. Do you need to call everyone here?"
The Elephantmen within this "water spirit" all expressed their dissatisfaction. They felt that this water spirit was making a mountain out of a molehill.
The water spirit didn't say anything.
As the metal formation continued to rotate, a blurry image appeared in front of everyone. The image was precisely that of the Elephantmen High Priest "Lei Gu", who had been painted with various colors.
The transmission of information in the Paramita Realm was very complicated. Even the Elephantmen who had a thorough understanding of the Paramita Realm could only transfer information from one Layer of Heaven to another.
It was already very impressive that the image of the Elephantmen could still be displayed after a dozen or so transfers.

Chapter 3250
High Priest Lei Gu couldn't see what was happening on the other side. There was only a blurry white ball of light floating above the formation.
Lei Gu knew very well that this white ball of light represented that powerful race.
It was also thanks to this powerful race that the elephantmen were able to survive until now in the Seventeenth Heaven.
In theory, this ball of light was indeed the god that saved the elephantmen tribe.
High Priest Lei Gu made a hand gesture, indicating for all the elephantmen to kneel.
Including Lei Qiao, all the elephantmen knelt on the ground, worshipping the white ball of light with pious expressions.
In the Thirty-Second Heaven, the "water spirit", or rather, the amalgamation of all the elephantmen, showed a hint of impatience when it saw this scene.
The elephantmen were the purest living beings. After all, they were formed from the most basic elements.
In their eyes, all living beings with flesh and blood were impure and dirty!
The elephantmen were the same …
"What is it?" the water spirit asked.
Although it had gathered its companions, the water spirit was still the main body. Out of courtesy, it should speak first.
"I see that paramita key! It's the one here! " High Priest Lei Gu raised the wooden board and pointed at the gourd-shaped area on the right side of the board.
As soon as Lei Gu finished speaking, the consciousness in the water spirit's body started to fluctuate.
"It's that temple of the past?"
"That temple key has finally appeared?"
"Back then, in order to enter it, we did everything we could, but those bastards fought for so many years, and in the end, no one succeeded!"
"It's really a heaven-sent opportunity!"
The elephantmen communicated through their consciousness very quickly. In almost a breath's time, these elephantmen had already discussed the past of the Seventeenth Heaven.
At that time, the three factions that had worked together with the elephantmen had all obtained the position of Heaven Lord of the Seventeenth Heaven. At that time, the elephantmen had thought that they were the only ones who could excavate the dangerous land, and in the end, they inevitably failed.
However, the failure of the other two factions gave the elephantmen a bit of comfort. Thus, the dangerous land of the Seventeenth Heaven was delayed just like that …
The Meta-Spirit Race had only casually supported the Seventeenth Heaven's elephantmen to stand guard. If the Paramita Key were to appear one day, they would naturally be the first to know.
In fact, the Meta-Spirit Race didn't have much hope. For a long time, they had almost forgotten about the Elephantman Race, until the Elephantman High Priest Lei Gu smashed the Heavenly Spirit Pearl.
"What should we do? Let those monkeys think of a way? "A fire spirit asked.
"Hmph, those monkeys can only hide in the dark. What use are they? Not long ago, the matter of the Fourteenth Heaven was a complete mess. You can't do anything right! "Jin Ling said coldly.
"The Light Domain isn't a problem, the key is that the monkeys aren't strong enough, and the Pure Witch of the 17th Heavenly Layer isn't under our control, I'm afraid we can only send down Moon Fire Slaves …" Shui Ling suggested.
There was no doubt about the strength of the Moon Fire Slave. However, descending from the 32nd Layer of Heaven was not a small price to pay. Moreover, the strength of the Moon Fire Slave would be greatly suppressed.
Under normal circumstances, Moon Fire Slaves wouldn't be sent down below the 30th Layer of Heaven. However, such an opportunity was hard to come by, so it was worthy of the Meta-Spirit Race's attention.
While the other Meta-Spirit Race members were hesitating, a fire spirit asked a very crucial question, "Which race is the creature that holds the Paramita Key?"
High Priest Lei Gu was waiting for the light orb's decision in the metal formation. When he suddenly heard this question from the light orb, he answered truthfully, "Humans. There are two humans. One of them has an appearance that I can't see clearly, but when the Rage God's body is activated, he can be as tall as us …"
The news of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge in the Fourteenth Heaven had already caused a stir. The Meta-Spirit Race also knew a little about it, but it didn't attract much attention.
When Lei Gu's voice fell, these Meta-Spirits already understood.
The human who occupied the Fourteenth Heaven had already stepped into the Seventeenth Heaven.
Moreover, this human just so happened to have the Paramita Key in his possession. This was no coincidence!
"Send down the Moon Fire Slaves!"
"Agreed!"
"Agreed!"
"…"
Not long after, the hall's door opened from the middle.
Three huge Moon Fire Slaves walked in, and the entire hall trembled slightly with their footsteps.
Green flames escaped from their necks, waists, and ribs. Their ferocious and tyrannical auras were clearly visible.
When the three Moon Fire Slaves stood in the center of the metal formation, the metal formation started to spin again.
Whoosh —
As the metal formation spun wildly, it turned into a streak of white light and shot toward the Thirty-First Heaven. However, this white light contained traces of dark green flames.
Some powerful beings that lived in the Thirty-First Heaven and the Thirty-Second Heaven also knew what the Meta-Spirit Race was doing after seeing the dark green flames. They once again sent down karmic fire and calamity to the lower heavens …
The Thirty-First Heaven …
The Thirteenth Heaven …
The Twenty-Ninth Heaven …
…
The Eighteenth Heaven …
The Seventeenth Heaven!
The elephant people still knelt and didn't get up.
To the ignorant and backward elephant people, that ball of white light was a miracle. As long as the miracle didn't disappear, they wouldn't dare to leave without permission.
Most of the elephant people looked extremely pious, and they were even longing in their hearts. Since the miracle had appeared, a blessing should descend next, right?
Just as they were longing, a white and green light fell from the sky.
Boom, boom, boom!
When that white and green light fell, it directly smashed the entire formation into smithereens. A pit with a radius of one thousand five hundred feet appeared in the center of the formation.
In terms of normal size, this pit was already incomparably huge.
However, the elephant people were thousands of feet tall, and this huge pit was only about the size of the water tank they used.
Three huge Moon Fire Slaves slowly crawled out of the pit.
When they noticed the even larger Elephantmen around them, the three Moon Fire Slaves were all stunned.
Although the Moon Fire Slaves were about two hundred to three hundred feet tall, they were insignificant compared to the elephant people that were thousands of feet tall …
"Oh, the suppression of the Seventeenth Heaven hasn't descended yet," one of the Moon Fire Slaves said.
"Then what do we do with these elephant people?"
"What do we do with them? They should be dead by now … "
When the Seventeenth Heaven didn't suppress the Moon Fire Slaves, the Moon Fire Slaves had the strength of the Thirty-Second Heaven. Just the residual power of the flames emitted from their bodies was enough to burn everything in the Seventeenth Heaven.
Chief Lei Qiao, High Priest Lei Gu, and the elephant people closest to the Moon Fire Slaves didn't understand what had happened. They only felt a wave of heat hit them in the face before they lost consciousness. Their bodies were instantly turned into charcoal.

Chapter 3251
The elephantmen who were further away from the Moon Fire Slaves were shocked by this sudden scene.
A scorching hot aura was transmitted to them, forcing them to retreat.
"Chief …"
"High Priest …"
The elephantmen repeated the name over and over again as they retreated.
They couldn't understand why these things that had descended would kill the High Priest.
These things should have sensed the High Priest's devotion, which was why they had appeared. But why would they kill a devout believer?
"Number 12, what should we do with these elephantmen?" One of the Moon Fire Slaves asked.
The Moon Fire Slaves didn't have their own names.
In fact, it wasn't just the Moon Fire Slaves. Even most of the Meta-Spirit Race didn't have names.
In the Meta-Spirit Race, a name was a symbol of nobility. It represented a distinct individual. Ordinary creatures didn't have the right to have a name.
The name of a Moon Fire Slave was a string of numbers. The closer the number, the stronger they were.
These three Moon Fire Slaves were Number 12, 13, and 14. They were very powerful beings among the Moon Fire Slaves. Number 12 was the strongest, so he was naturally the leader of the three.
"They're useless. Get rid of them," Number 12 replied indifferently.
Number 13 Moon Fire Slave took a step forward. A ball of green flames spread from its feet. Then, wisps of green flames quickly spread throughout the entire village.
Some of the elephantmen realized that something was wrong. They ran out of the village.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
Chaotic and heavy footsteps spread throughout the entire village. However, no matter how fast the elephantmen were, they couldn't outrun the spreading green flames.
When the green flames in the village reached a certain level, all the elephantmen running around were turned into huge flaming men. The low buildings, bushes, totems, and miscellaneous items in the village were all ignited in an instant. They turned into green flames and were burned to ashes …
As the elephantmen tribe burned, three small balls appeared above the heads of the three Moon Fire Slaves. The moment the balls appeared, they drilled into the bodies of the Moon Fire Slaves.
When the three balls drilled into the bodies of the Moon Fire Slaves, their powerful strength was quickly suppressed. This suppression came from the seventeenth layer of heaven, so they couldn't resist at all.
The suppression of their strength brought about extreme pain. The three Moon Fire Slaves struggled painfully for a while before they managed to adapt.
"I heard that those two are Pure Ones. Their strength shouldn't be underestimated," Number 14 Moon Fire Slave said worriedly.
When one's strength was suppressed, one's self-confidence would often be greatly reduced.
"If the other party is really a Pure Witch who can cross over to the Nirvana Realm, it is indeed very tricky," Number 13 agreed.
"If it was in the past, it would indeed be very troublesome. But now, it might not be so troublesome," Number Twelve replied coldly.
Number 13 and Number 14 remained silent after hearing this.
The suppression of the Otherworld Heavenly King was unsurpassable. That was the rule in the past.
However, the Meta-Spirit Tribe had found a way to circumvent this, and they would only have to pay the price of their lives.
Although Number 12 was only slightly stronger than the other two, he was the leader of the three of them here. If they had to sacrifice their lives to escape the suppression of the Seventeenth Heaven, it would definitely be Number 13 and Number 14 …
"Let's go quickly. I hope we can be the first to sense the danger." Number Twelve did not care what they thought. He only needed to complete the task given by the Lord Elemental Spirits …
Accompanied by a gust of wind, the several hundred feet tall grass on the grassland bent over, setting off waves of green waves.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge did not know what was happening in the Elephant-Man Tribe. They were still moving through the northern part of the grassland.
If it were not for this gigantic grassland, Luo Zheng's momentum would be extremely terrifying. However, on this gigantic grassland, the lightning he transformed into was as thin as a needle and thread …
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Suddenly, a loud buzzing sound came from behind Luo Zheng. Several giant bees the size of houses flapped their transparent wings and quickly chased after Luo Zheng.
"Another three …"
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly. At the same time, he said to Feng Ge, "Get ready!"
Then, he suddenly threw Feng Ge upwards. At the same time, Luo Zheng's body began to expand rapidly, and in the blink of an eye, he became thousands of feet tall.

Along the way, they had lost count of how many times they had encountered these giant bees. Although Feng Ge and Luo Zheng could kill these giant bees, if they did not transform into their gigantic Aragami, it would still take some effort to catch and kill these giant bees.
After being chased by the giant bees twice, Luo Zheng suddenly came up with the simplest way to kill them, which was to swat them like flies.
After a few rounds, Luo Zheng was already very familiar with it. He did not hesitate at all.
After half a day, fences made of giant trees appeared in front of them again. These fences were the edge of the grassland.
"We've reached the edge of the southern grassland," Feng Ge said.

Luo Zheng and Feng Ge went down for more than two hundred miles and finally saw the end of the gobi. From here, they could see the intersection of the desert and the grassland in the distance. The two different areas converged and formed a circle.

Luo Zheng opened his palm and stared at the map of the Paramita Key. "This circle should be the bottom of this gourd-shaped cave!"

Chapter 3252

A normal map of the Nirvana Key would have the temple as the center.

Chapter 3253

Chapter 3255

Chapter 3260

That Spirit of Void was so familiar with this area. It would not be difficult to pass through this Killing Fog Array. If it chased after them, it would be troublesome.

Just now, Luo Zheng had judged that there was a giant in the Gobi Desert. But judging from the frequency of the giant foot's appearance, there were probably thousands of invisible giants lining up to step on him …
Seeing that his altitude was constantly being pressed down by the giant foot, he was afraid that if he continued to fly forward, he would die. He could only clench his teeth and quickly retreat.

Chapter 3264
Although Luo Zheng had only met Chiyou once, he had seen his statues many times.

Whether it was Donghuang, Chiyou, Nuwa, or Fuxi, none of them would erect a statue of themselves inside this temple.

Why was Nuwa born as Nuwa?
Was Fuxi born as Fuxi?
Did that mean that the Yellow Emperor of Dihong Clan, Shennong of Lie Shan Clan, Donghuang, and Chiyou of Dihong Clan were also born for them?
Seeing these statues in the hall, Luo Zheng's confusion was instantly cleared.
Regardless of whether it was Nuwa or Fuxi, they had probably existed since the 77th Human Civilization. They were the top powerhouses of that era. They had entered the Nirvana Realm before they could complete the End and became the Nirvana Human Tribe.
When they spread the seeds of humanity into a new cycle of Chaos, they would naturally prioritize spreading their own substitutes.
Those substitutes inherited their powerful bloodline. Thus, Goddess Nuwa created Li Mountain, Yellow Emperor Xuanyuan created You Xiong Clan, and East Emperor Taiyi used a sword to win over all the clansmen in the world, forming the Great Yi Heavenly Palace!
"I'm afraid this Emperor Dong is the real Emperor Dong, and Feng Ge, your Father Emperor should be a substitute he spread," Luo Zheng continued to explain. He was very sure of his own judgment.
"How is this possible …"

In the center of the hall, there was an independent statue. This statue was exactly the same as the one outside the divine temple. This statue was calmly looking down at a square stone platform in front of it. On the platform, there was a blue book floating quietly.

No.12 let out a strange laugh.
Actually, No.12 didn't realize the value of this divine temple. It was only Genryū's slave.

Just as Moon Fire Slave reached out its hand towards the book, the statue that was reading with its head lowered suddenly raised its head.

No.12's large hand slapped towards the statue. With this slap, a mere stone statue would naturally be smashed to pieces.
However, No.12 didn't understand. This person's statue was also standing outside the divine temple.
If his statue was so easily destroyed, with Wu Kong, Yuan Ling, and the other races' disgust towards him, they would have smashed it to pieces long ago. However, up until now, the statue outside the divine temple was still standing strong and hadn't been damaged at all!
When No.12 slapped over, a smile appeared on the statue's face. He suddenly reached out his hand and lightly tapped the air with his finger.
"Pop!"
A cracking sound suddenly came from the hall.
Number 12's tall and sturdy mountain-like body had already completely stiffened, and he was frozen in place.

Moon Fire Slaves were very hard to kill!
Normal methods couldn't destroy the spirit core at all!
No.14 had already "died" twice, but as long as it had stardust, it would immediately be lively again.

That statue didn't stop after killing No.12. He muttered again, "Otherwise, the other races will step in here …"
"Whoosh —"

Another two invisible finger lights pierced through.

Chapter 3265

The most powerful civilization in the Nirvana Realm was the Elemental Spirit Clan. They stood on the opposite side of the human race and many other civilizations. Even if some of the civilizations disappeared and some went into hiding like the Green Jade Civilization, the Elemental Spirit Civilization would not be able to monopolize the Nirvana Realm.
According to the information given by the Green Jade Spirit, the ten universal civilizations were all extraordinary.

。”
Luo Zheng killing the 14th Moon Fire Slave in one hit made the human female even more interested.

The human female from the Wu Kong Clan looked at the starry sky pattern not far away. Her face showed hesitation.

Chapter 3266
The human woman only hesitated for a moment before making a decision.

When the several people around the bonfire saw Luo Zheng, they revealed a look of respect. They bowed respectfully towards Luo Zheng as if Luo Zheng was an amazing character.
Seeing the attitude of these people towards him, Luo Zheng felt his heart relax slightly. He extended his hand and pointed towards those pieces of meat.
Among the seven emotions and six pleasures, appetite was the most ferocious. Ever since Luo Zheng had become inedible, he rarely ate heartily except when he enjoyed special delicacies in the Divine Realm. Now that he had become an ordinary mortal, once his appetite was aroused, it was actually this ferocious.
"You can't eat this!"
"You'll die if you eat it!"

Those people warned Luo Zheng, their faces full of horror.
Luo Zheng glanced at them with a puzzled look. He didn't forget that he was in the illusion to pass the test of the statue.
But Luo Zheng still didn't believe that he would die if he ate the meat.
Luo Zheng looked at the meat pieces that emitted a rich fragrance. After swallowing his saliva, he was ready to go forward and eat.
Seeing this, one of them took the meat pieces and opened his mouth to chew.
After eating a few mouthfuls, this person suddenly rolled his eyes, spat out foam, and fell to the ground!
"It's poisonous!"
"You can't eat it!"
"…"

Right now, Luo Zheng was able to endure it!
After all, in the Dark Domain, Luo Zheng was strongly attracted to the Dark Flies. But Luo Zheng was still able to suppress it. It was just a few pieces of roasted meat.
Those people were still shouting like idiots that the meat was poisonous and couldn't be eaten, jumping up and down.

"Is this a test for me?"
Luo Zheng threw out several questions in a row, but those people didn't answer as if they didn't have ears.
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and left without saying a word.
The wilderness was boundless. If he kept walking like this, there would be no end.
This illusion gave Luo Zheng too few hints, so he could only keep walking …
Two hours later, Luo Zheng's physical strength was seriously exhausted. There seemed to be a ball of fire lingering in his throat, and even his consciousness was somewhat blurred.
He was really too thirsty and hungry …
The moment this thought popped up, a wisp of wine fragrance suddenly floated in the distance.

He, who was originally a little confused, quivered and saw a wall of brilliant lights in front of him.

Chapter 3267

When he saw the lanterns in front of him again, Luo Zheng no longer had any expectations.

Under the lanterns, there were all kinds of delicious food and wine gathered around, and each one was more tempting than the last. All of them touched Luo Zheng's soul, making it difficult for him to restrain himself.

Eight hours ….

Twenty hours ….

Luo Zheng walked forward like a walking corpse, thinking.

After an unknown amount of time, Luo Zheng gradually regained consciousness.
When he woke up, the first thing he did was to look around.
If he had failed, he should have returned to the temple.
He was no longer in the wilderness, nor was he in the temple. Instead, he was in a gilded hall. He was actually sitting on the dragon throne, wearing a dragon robe, looking down at the civil and military officials in front of him!

If Luo Zheng already knew that everything was an illusion, the so-called power and wealth were nothing but fleeting clouds. How could this desire for power test him? What's more, Luo Zheng didn't have much desire for glory and wealth.
This stage shouldn't be too difficult.
However, Luo Zheng still thought too simply.
With limited time, this kind of test was indeed meaningless to Luo Zheng.
However, this was an illusory realm. Dreaming for a hundred years or even a thousand years was a piece of cake.

His true memories of himself seemed to have become extremely distant. He seemed to have accepted his identity as a mortal emperor in this environment.
He was entangled with his subjects, his concubines, and everything else. He had completely integrated into them …
…
Inside the shen temple.
The statue in the center was using a calm gaze to look at a patch of starry sky.

The statue was a puppet. His thoughts were normal, but he was restricted in some areas.
His mission was to wait for the shen temple to open and choose a qualified candidate.
In his heart, from the beginning, he had rejected the woman and Feng Ge who had sided with the Wu Kong Clan. If he had the authority, he probably would have stopped the two women from entering the starry sky to receive the test.
"The desire for power is stopped at the first test?" the statue said.
In comparison, Feng Ge and the human woman's performance was much better than Luo Zheng's.
Of the six tests, the woman from the Wu Kong Clan had already passed three tests. What was even more strange to the statue was that Feng Ge had passed four tests in a row. It was as though she didn't have the six desires and couldn't be trapped.
"Speaking of which, the more you experience the test of the six desires, the more you think. The more complicated it is, the easier it is to be trapped," the statue said with a sigh.
Putting aside the woman from the Wu Kong Clan, although Feng Ge's past experience was tragic, it was far from being complicated. Before she met Luo Zheng, most of her life could be described as "boring." This kind of simple thinking had a strong resistance to the temptation of the six desires … …
In the dreamscape in the starry sky.
Luo Zheng had "reigned" for a hundred years.
He seemed to have completely forgotten who he used to be. He only knew that he was the Emperor who controlled the world.
There were often praises in his ears. One word was the law of the world.
Two hundred years … …
Three hundred years … …
Five hundred years … …

The real time Luo Zheng had been trapped was only the time for two incense sticks to burn … …
After Luo Zheng had passed this test, the whole world was turned upside down again. What appeared in front of Luo Zheng were beautiful women. Some women were flirtatious, and some were full of affection.
"?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.

Chapter 3268
Luo Zheng's body shuttled back and forth among these stars.

Three Sheath Stars. Three million people lived on these three stars. It was truly a huge number …
Looking ahead, there were Charm Star, Arisaema Star, Tiger Star …
Perhaps because he had stayed in the illusion for too long, Luo Zheng's reaction was somewhat sluggish. At this moment, he suddenly realized that this was the human race's Chaos Era, the 77th Chaos Era!

However, whether it was the Mind's Eye or the Green Jade Spirit through the Tool of Civilization, they were far from comparable to the scene in front of him.
