If he didn't know that he had fallen into an illusion, he would have treated this place as another 'mother world'.

The speed of the stars increased rapidly, constantly approaching Luo Zheng and rapidly distancing themselves from Luo Zheng.

The star was completely red, and its surface was like a sea of purgatory flames. On the surface of the sea of flames, fiery dragons spiraled and spat flames into the sky.

As he approached, Luo Zheng also felt the heat of this star.
However, Luo Zheng's body had already returned to its normal state. Although the heat was astonishing, he was still able to withstand it.

Even those 'fire spirits' would find it difficult to survive in the surroundings of the palace. This flaming palace was the core of the entire planet.
When Luo Zheng landed in front of the palace, he saw a person in front of him.
This person had red eyebrows and red hair. He was completely naked and sat cross-legged on a huge fire lotus. When he saw Luo Zheng, he pointed at him and said, "Please sit!"

"I am the master of the Fire Domain, Fire God Zhurong," the red-browed, red-haired man said.
There were two faint flames in Zhurong's eyes, as if they could burn everything.
"Fire God." Luo Zheng's eyebrows raised slightly.
Being able to erect his statue in the temple on the other side and being one of the 39 Heroes, he was probably a top master in the Human Race.
"My test is very simple. Please sit next to me for half an hour." Zhurong pointed at the fire lotus beside him.
Luo Zheng blinked twice. The corners of his mouth curled up slightly, and he jumped onto the fire lotus.
The moment he sat down, the eyes of Zhurong's statue in the temple flashed with flames.
The statue looking at the bamboo slips noticed Zhurong's action and immediately laughed, "Hey, this kid just passed the Six Desires Trials in one go. Brother Zhurong is going to test him …"
"I will burn him to ashes," the statue Zhurong responded faintly.
As soon as the statues finished speaking, the fire lotus under Luo Zheng in the starry sky illusion turned into a blazing white in an instant. The white light rushed upward and enveloped Luo Zheng's body.
"Buzz …"
Zhurong looked at Luo Zheng in the fire lotus with a trace of indifference in his eyes.
He had a bad temper and was never soft-hearted.
Even though the statue only contained a wisp of his soul, it was still very strict. It could only be said that this kid was unlucky to encounter him in the first trial.
However, the indifference on Zhurong's face only lasted for a very short time. Soon, a strange look appeared in his eyes, and this strange look quickly turned into shock and disbelief!
The golden vortexes on the surface of Luo Zheng's body absorbed all the heat in the fire lotus. The fire lotus almost didn't cause much trouble for Luo Zheng.
At this rate, even if it took an hour, a day, or a month, Luo Zheng would be safe and sound, not to mention the time it took to burn a stick of incense.
"Buzz …"
As Zhurong's thoughts turned, the fire lotus bloomed even more intensely.
The light from the fire lotus turned from blazing white to brilliant blue. This blue light reflected a strange color on Luo Zheng's body.
However, Luo Zheng still sat there indifferently.
"Phew …"
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, the fire lotus gradually stopped. Luo Zheng sat up straight, cupped his hands towards Zhurong and said, "Elder Zhurong, can I be considered to have passed?"
Zhurong looked at Luo Zheng carefully. This wisp of his soul had a lot of questions to ask.
However, when Zhurong planted this wisp of his soul, he had given this wisp of soul a certain level of authority, so naturally, he couldn't ask any questions.
"You can leave now," Zhurong answered simply.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands towards Zhurong again, then jumped down from the fire lotus. When he left the palace, his body once again drifted away at an abnormal speed, quickly leaving this unimaginably huge star.
As Zhurong said, this area was the Fire Realm, which used to be under Zhurong's control.
After leaving the Fire Realm, many ice-blue stars drifted towards Luo Zheng. Even though these stars were terrifyingly huge, their bodies were still sparkling and translucent, as if the entire star was pure.
Soon …
A pure-white star flew straight towards Luo Zheng.
On the outermost side of the star, a strong airflow rolled up a snowstorm, which seemed to be able to tear everything that approached it into pieces.
After entering the star, Luo Zheng found that the inside of the star was spotlessly white and strangely peaceful.
A huge ice palace appeared in front of Luo Zheng. In the palace, a skinny adult man was still sitting alone. The surface of this man's body was translucent blue, with ripples of water flowing along the surface of his body.
Before Luo Zheng said anything, the adult man had already answered, "I am Gong Gong, the God of Water. Please sit down."

Chapter 3269
Just as Luo Zheng was muttering in his heart, water started flowing out slowly from the surroundings of the palace.
The temperature in front of him was already extremely low, but the slowly flowing water was actually not frozen.
But when the water touched Luo Zheng, a chill seeped into the depths of his soul. In the blink of an eye, he could no longer think!
'This water is so cold …' Luo Zheng was inwardly startled.
Gong Gong was also gradually submerged by the water. Before he was submerged, Gong Gong said, "If you can endure for the time it takes for an incense stick to burn, you will pass … gululu …"
Soon, this completely transparent water had submerged Luo Zheng and Gong Gong.
The heat in Luo Zheng's body was rapidly draining away. His entire body was stiff, and he could not even move!
'So cold …'
Even though Luo Zheng's body was not afraid of being burned by raging fire and had easily passed the trial of Fire God Zhu Rong, when facing Gong Gong, this extremely low temperature finally gave Luo Zheng a hard time.
"Pi pa!"
Under the rapid freezing, cracks started appearing on the surface of Luo Zheng's fleshy body.
What made Luo Zheng even more gloomy was that his body was cracking, yet he could not feel it at all.
'Because the temperature is too low, I can't even use my strength?' Luo Zheng's mind was spinning rapidly.
When his fleshy body started cracking, even the Chaotic Origin Spirit could not be activated. This was the first time Luo Zheng experienced such a low temperature.
'Strength …'
The Godly Jun Energy was a type of energy, and the activity of energy would naturally bring about heat.
In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng began drawing out the Godly Jun Energy crazily!
As the power of Shen Jun began to move around in his body, his originally stiff body gradually began to recover. After a trace of heat gained the upper hand, it quickly gathered together, and then spread outward!
Gong Gong looked at Luo Zheng indifferently. He was slightly disappointed with Luo Zheng's performance.
A smart participant should not have stopped when he came into contact with the freezing water. But now, it was too late …
Luo Zheng, who was already frozen, could no longer move even if he wanted to.
Luo Zheng just stood there without moving. He could not even blink his eyes.
From the looks of it, Luo Zheng's wife had no way to break free.
However, in Luo Zheng's body, the Divine Jun Force was revolving around his body, and the speed of its rotation was getting faster and faster.
The faster it rotated, the faster the heat rose!
When Gong Gong felt that Luo Zheng was doomed to fail, Luo Zheng's fingers suddenly trembled slightly. Next, his arms began moving as well, then his shoulders, then his body and legs!
"This guy …" Gong Gong paused his eyes.
As Luo Zheng's body continued to "defrost", he also began to dance rapidly. As his body danced, the frozen water in the Ice Palace also began to revolve around him.
And because the frozen water was spinning faster and faster, the temperature of the water was gradually rising.
Within the time for an incense stick to burn, Gong Gong slightly waved his hand in the frozen water. Following his move, the rising frozen water dropped quickly, and soon flowed out completely.
On Luo Zheng's back, mist rose like dense smoke. That mist was formed by the remaining frozen water on Luo Zheng's body, which was evaporated by Luo Zheng's body heat.
"Elder, can I leave now?" Luo Zheng bowed to Gong Gong and said.
Gong Gong waved his hand to Luo Zheng, signaling Luo Zheng to leave.
This area was under Gong Gong's control, which was the Water Area, and took up about ten percent of this area. The same was true for Zhu Rong's fire area.
"This Water Area and the fire area should be at the same level as a superpower in my mother world. Gong Gong and Zhu Rong should be at the same level as East Emperor Taiyi and Goddess Nvywa back then. But they have been in the Nirvana World for countless years. I wonder how powerful they are now …"
Luo Zheng pondered while flying across the area.
Judging by the current situation, he might have guessed it right. He was really going to take the test of the 39 heroes.
But these 39 heroes were all the strongest beings in the human race. It would be an honor to meet them.
But thinking of this, Luo Zheng realized another problem.
Wouldn't he have to face the real "Goddess Nvywa", or even the real "East Emperor Taiyi", or the real "Chi You" and so on …
Goddess Nvywa in his mother world could be considered as another "Goddess Nvywa" created. Even though Luo Zheng understood this point, he still felt strange when facing her.
"Whoosh …"
While Luo Zheng was moving at high speed, the surrounding area changed again.
What greeted him were numerous bright golden stars!
When one of the huge golden stars shrouded him, Luo Zheng landed on the ground. What he saw was golden Buddha statues everywhere. Every golden Buddha statue had a different appearance. Some were standing, some were lying down, and some were standing, showing a myriad of postures.
Those golden Buddha statues were lined up in a row. It was impossible to see all of them. In any case, these golden Buddhas were everywhere on this golden planet!
"This is the Buddhist Domain …" Luo Zheng said with a serious gaze.
In his father's universe, there were some Buddhists that had been passed down. But in the Divine Domain, they had disappeared without a trace. In his mother world, they were nowhere to be seen.
This was not difficult to understand.
The civilization in the 77th chaos shouldn't have appeared in this round of civilizations.
Whether it was Goddess Nvywa or the East Emperor, they were just seeds that had been spread out …
The influence of Buddhism might have also been spread, but it hadn't developed.
Even the Fire God Zhurong that Luo Zheng had just come into contact with was something that Luo Zheng had looked up when he was flipping through the ancient records in his mother world. It was a human race that had once fought with the Sky-Splitting Hou, but this race hadn't developed and had even disappeared from his mother world.
"Dong …"
A long and melodious sound of a bell rang out in front of Luo Zheng.
As a golden light bloomed in front of Luo Zheng, a huge Buddha appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
This Buddha made a "Dharma Seal" with one hand and looked at Luo Zheng with kind eyes.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to ask, the Buddha suddenly closed his eyes.
As he closed his eyes, Luo Zheng felt his chest become unusually heavy. His eyes were also closed. He couldn't see, hear, hear, or even think about it.
But Luo Zheng, with his six senses sealed, didn't fall into darkness …
In the depths of his heart, a golden light bloomed again. Luo Zheng's consciousness faced the Buddha again. The Buddha still faced Luo Zheng with the "Dharma Seal". Even this "Luo Zheng" in his heart was sealed by the Buddha again and fell into his inner heart.
But just as Luo Zheng's heart bloomed with golden light, he faced the Buddha again and fell into the third level of mind flow …
As he closed his mind layer by layer, Luo Zheng didn't know which level he had reached.

Chapter 3270
When darkness and light no longer alternated, the seal finally stopped.
"Is this a test of the state of mind?" Luo Zheng stared at the Buddha and asked.
The Buddha nodded. "Yes."
"Do I also need half an hour to break this seal?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"As you wish, benefactor," the Buddha replied.
"That's good!"
After taking a deep breath, Luo Zheng's mind began to move rapidly.
The current world was not even an illusion. It was a state that the Buddha trapped Luo Zheng in his heart.
Since it was Luo Zheng's own heart, Luo Zheng naturally did as he pleased.
"Sword of Mind! Break! "
As Luo Zheng's mind fluctuated, long swords suddenly grew out of the whole world.
"Crack, crack, crack, crack …"
The Buddhas all over the sky were torn to pieces by the long swords, and this side of the world collapsed.
This layer of the world was like a huge curtain. After being torn apart by Luo Zheng, it immediately retreated. What greeted Luo Zheng was a brand-new layer of the world.
"The swordsmanship that focuses on the state of mind is the inheritance of the Stellar Sword Realm," the Buddha said calmly.
"Stellar Sword Realm?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows and thought of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace of the Eastern Emperor Taiyi. Luo Zheng asked, "Is the master of the Stellar Sword Realm the Eastern Emperor Taiyi?"
"Yes," the Buddha said. "But I'm afraid it's difficult to break the 'prison of my heart' with this Taoist method alone. Your state of mind is sharp enough, but your strength is not enough."
Luo Zheng smiled slightly and said, "Is that so? But it's not difficult for me to break this world … "
"Boom!"
Countless long swords spread out from all directions of this world and tore the whole world into pieces again. A new world appeared in front of Luo Zheng again.
"Boom …"
"Boom …"
"Boom …"
With Luo Zheng's constant bombardment, 13 layers of the world that surrounded Luo Zheng's heart had been shattered.
At this rate, he would be able to pass the Buddha's test in less than the time it takes for an incense stick to burn.
But when he reached the fourteenth floor, Luo Zheng's mind transformed into a long sword again. But this time, the long sword that appeared was very small. The world in front of Luo Zheng only showed a few cracks, but it was not completely shattered.
"Sword of Mind, break!" Luo Zheng frowned.
A tranquil smile appeared on Buddha's face. "You're tired …"
Luo Zheng's state of mind had always been extremely sharp, but something that was too sharp, the consumption of mental energy was enormous, and in the end, it was difficult to maintain it for long.
Luo Zheng was indeed feeling tired. His face revealed a trace of helplessness as he said, "When it comes to mental strength, there's no standard. I'm afraid I can't train it."
The path of one's mental state had always been mysterious, and it all depended on one's own comprehension.
The Buddha smiled faintly and said, "It's just that you don't know how to do it. If you don't believe me, I have a Great Buddhist Abhijna that I can impart to you."
"Great Dharma Divine Ability?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
No matter what, these 39 heroes were the strongest existences of the Origin Era back then.
Although he didn't know this Buddha's real name, he was a powerful figure of the Buddhist Realm. The Abhijna he imparted probably couldn't be underestimated.
"But isn't this a test? Why would you impart the Abhijna … "Luo Zheng asked the question that puzzled him.
The Buddha smiled faintly and said, "It's up to you whether you want to be tested or not. I have good karma with you, so there's no need to test you."
Although the Buddha had explained it this way, Luo Zheng was still somewhat puzzled. The process of this test was completely different from what he had imagined.
Just as Luo Zheng was feeling puzzled, the Buddha suddenly opened his mouth.
A series of strange words entered Luo Zheng's ears. Of course, Luo Zheng was originally trapped in his own mind, so in reality, he didn't have a voice. And Luo Zheng didn't use his ears to listen.
"These words …"
When the words were transmitted, Luo Zheng felt a heavy pressure in his heart.
Each word alone was meaningless, but when they were connected together, they could produce such an effect. This situation was very similar to Sanskrit.
When the Buddha finished reciting, the pressure in Luo Zheng's heart had reached a peak. Then, he suddenly relaxed. Luo Zheng felt a warm feeling in his heart. His whole body was extremely relaxed, and the fatigue just now was swept away!
"Sword of Mind!"
"Boom …"
"Sword of Mind …"
"Boom!"
Layers of the world were broken again by Luo Zheng. When he was tired, he would use the mental cultivation method passed by the Buddha.
Soon after, Luo Zheng finally broke through the layers of the sealed world.
He had successfully passed the Buddha's test and learned a mental cultivation method. Luo Zheng was naturally happy. He asked, "What's the name of this mental cultivation method? How could it have such a magical effect?"
"This is the Heart King Technique. If you use it in reverse, it will be my Heart Prison. It can trap people in the Heart Prison," the Buddha answered.
"Many thanks, but may I ask the name of the Buddha?" Luo Zheng asked again with cupped hands.
The Buddha calmly gave Luo Zheng a gentle push, and the entire Buddhist Realm began to go far away.
At the same time, the Buddha's voice was transmitted. "My name is Tathagata …"
"Tathagata Buddha …"
Luo Zheng remembered this name and flew into the void.
It seemed that the Buddhist Realm was an extremely powerful force in the 77th chaos era ….
Luo Zheng thought that since the 77th civilization was dominated by humans, it should have had a much better time than the current chaos era.
In fact, that chaos era was much more complicated than Luo Zheng thought.
Soon, a new great realm enveloped Luo Zheng.
When Luo Zheng landed on one of the stars, he saw a handsome man. This man's facial features were softer than a woman's, and he had a unique temperament.
"Senior is …?" Luo Zheng asked as usual.
"I'm Di Jun," the handsome man answered.
"Di Jun …" Luo Zheng's eyes widened.
The handsome man waved at Luo Zheng and said, "You can go. I don't have a test here. You pass."
"No test …."
Luo Zheng wasn't too surprised by his experience with Buddha Gautama. It was as though this test was to let these human heroes confirm their identities.
However, Luo Zheng did not retreat immediately. He did not care whether Di Jun was a human or a Golden Crow. He just stared at Di Jun and asked, "Senior Di Jun, did you have the same goal?"
"What goal?" Di Jun asked.
"To break the destiny of the chaos and complete the Way of the End," Luo Zheng said.
Di Jun smiled seductively and nodded. "Of course we have the same goal."
Luo Zheng curled his lips and said, "It's a pity that the 'Di Jun' of this chaos is on the opposite side."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Di Jun's expression became somewhat complicated.
Although that 'Di Jun' had inherited everything from him, including his talent, personality, bloodline, and even Divine Sense, he was an independent individual after all. Even the real Di Jun himself could not control him.
"I see."

Chapter 3271
Star regions covered Luo Zheng one after another. Luo Zheng also saw the powerful beings of the human race one by one.
There was Earth, the mother of the earth, who controlled Yin and Yang and nurtured all living things.
There was also Hou Yi, who could shoot stars and suns with his bow …
And …
Strictly speaking, these people all looked like humans in front of Luo Zheng, but quite a number of them weren't pure human beings.
For example, Emperor Jun had monster blood flowing in his body, but his body was still that of a Gold Crow.
For example, Goddess Nuwa was half human and half snake, so she wasn't completely human.
Another example was that monkey named Sun Wukong, who had extraordinary fighting power …
However, these people were all born in the 77th civilization of the human race, so they quite approved of the 77th civilization of the human race.
When Luo Zheng saw Goddess Nuwa again, he felt even more strange.
The two "Goddess Nuwa" were definitely not the same person, but they were almost exactly the same. The light of wisdom flashing in their eyes could see through people's hearts.
"I thought it was me who got the Paramita Key to open the temple. Why did it become you?" Goddess Nuwa stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
The "me" she was talking about was naturally Goddess Nuwa in the mother world.
"Is this question a test for me?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
"Sort of." Goddess Nuwa shrugged.
In fact, except for Zhurong and Gonggong, the other powerful beings didn't give Luo Zheng any serious tests.
Luo Zheng didn't hide it, because it wasn't worth hiding.
He then recounted how he had obtained the last six-stripe Blood Fixing Mysterious Seed and how he had discovered the Paramita Key.
After Luo Zheng finished, Goddess Nuwa sighed softly. "I didn't think I'd be impatient after all. I got the last six-vein Blood Fixing Seed. This is a freak combination of factors, but it's fate. You …"
Nuwa looked like she wanted to say something, but she hesitated.
After leaving Goddess Nuwa's star region, Luo Zheng entered a new star region. This time, Luo Zheng met Fuxi.
The Fuxi in front of him had the same personality as the Fuxi in the mother world …
"Hey, kid, was I powerful in the mother world?" Fuxi asked.
"Great, great …" Luo Zheng answered.
In terms of strength, almost all the experts in the mother world were wary of Fuxi. No one was willing to face Fuxi head-on.
"Then … how did the woman in the neighboring star region treat me?" Fuxi asked carefully.
"What?"
Luo Zheng stared blankly at Fuxi.
Under Fuxi's coercion, Luo Zheng bragged about his position in Mount Li before Fuxi let Luo Zheng leave.
However, when Luo Zheng left, Fuxi's face revealed a rare complex expression. Among the 39 heroes, Fuxi was the one who was the most unwilling to accept his current fate. His mentality was surprisingly the same as his own in his mother world …
Next, Luo Zheng met Shennong, who was also Lie Shan, as well as Ji Xuanyuan and Di Hong.
After the two of them learned about their clones' choices in the Mother World, their expressions became extremely grim.
They knew their own personalities, and they also knew the personalities of their clones. After deciding on their own path, they would probably walk down it without hesitation. However, they were trapped on the other side. Other than sighing, there was nothing else they could do.
Star regions flashed in front of Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng saw many powerful figures.
"Thirty-eighth …"
As another star region enveloped him, he appeared at the top of a green mountain. Across from him stood a burly middle-aged man.
Although the middle-aged man looked very different from the Chiyou aura Luo Zheng had seen before, Luo Zheng still recognized him at a glance.
The Chiyou in the Divine Realm was already at the end of his rope. Naturally, he was not as powerful as the Chiyou in front of him!
"My blood flows in your veins. Are you my descendant?" Chiyou stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Luo Zheng nodded and said, "You are … my grandfather."
A hint of joy appeared on Chiyou's face.
Putting the six-stripe Blood Fixing Mysterious Seed in Li Mountain was the result of many discussions.
According to the original calculation, the highest probability was that Nuwa would open the temple herself. In the end, it was his grandson. Chiyou was pleasantly surprised, "It seems that the Jiuli tribe is developing well. Heh …"
Jiuli Star Region was also a very powerful existence in the 77th civilization. Chiyou naturally had this confidence.
"Uh … grandfather, Jiuli has been destroyed. You in the Mother World were also killed by Dihong and Lieshan. Now only a part of your tribe is left, and I have joined Taiyi Heavenly Palace …" Luo Zheng said helplessly.
Chiyou's face stiffened for a moment. After a while, he showed a helpless smile, "I didn't expect it to be like this. But I am proud that you can open this temple!"
Chiyou was a born optimist. Even after hearing about the tragic experience of Jiuli, he was still very relieved. At the same time, he asked about it in detail.
Luo Zheng stayed with Chiyou for the longest time. He told Chiyou about his experience in detail.
Chiyou didn't expect the situation in the Mother World to become like this.
The human race's plan back then was indeed very successful. In the beginning, the mother world was flourishing, and many civilizations had interfered. Now, the human race in the 77th civilization had the upper hand. Unfortunately, You Xiong, Shinno, and the others had betrayed them. This was the biggest surprise …
Not long after, Luo Zheng left Jiuli Star Region again.
The star regions above his head circled, and the reddish-gold star region in the center shrouded Luo Zheng.
This time, Luo Zheng fell into the depths of the palace in the golden star. There were green scrolls floating everywhere, and the words in the scrolls were constantly flowing.
The statue in the center of the temple stood in the scrolls. Of course, he was not facing a statue now, but a living person.
"Young man, you can sit down." This person cupped his hands toward the chair in front of Luo Zheng.
"Senior, you're too polite." Luo Zheng sat down obediently. He realized that this person was the most important person among the 39 heroes.
This person smiled at Luo Zheng, placed a handful of jade scrolls in front of Luo Zheng, and immediately asked, "You're from the Human Race. Are you interested in learning about the history of the Human Race?"
"The 77th Chaos?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, the 77th Civilization." The person nodded.
"I'm interested." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
The person gently knocked the jade scrolls in his hand on the table. Wisps of green filaments floated out of the jade scrolls. These filaments constantly drilled into Luo Zheng's mind. The history of the Human Race was quickly engraved in Luo Zheng's mind …
In that era, the Human Race had also experienced many disputes and conflicts.
Especially in the early stage of the Chaos, the 77th Chaos had experienced three major tribulations. They were the War of the Ancestors of Sorcerers, the War of Deification, and the struggle with Taoists and Buddhas.

Chapter 3272
Among the 77 civilizations, there were three major sects in many star regions. They were the Ren Sect, the Jie Sect, and the Chan Sect.
The person in front of him was the Primeval Lord of Heaven, the leader of the Chan Sect.
"Senior, I have a general understanding of the past of the human race," Luo Zheng said.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven nodded, then said, "Then do you know why we are spreading the seeds of the human race in this round of chaos?"
Luo Zheng lowered his head and thought for a while, then answered, "It is said that this round of chaos is the last chance to complete the End."
"Yes, it seems that you have some understanding." The Primeval Lord of Heaven nodded again, "We have already failed once. Even if we failed and retreated to the other side, we have never feared anyone!"
"The human race was born in the 77th civilization. Before that, the 76 dominating civilizations had already established themselves in the other side. It was not easy for the human race to fight for a place, but our master led us all the way," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said proudly.
"Master?" Luo Zheng looked confused.
The memory in the jade slip did not record who the Primeval Lord of Heaven's master was, but the leaders of the three major sects, the Ren Sect, the Jie Sect, and the Chan Sect, seemed to have called each other brothers.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven's strength had probably surpassed the Wing King of the Green Jade Civilization. Then, wouldn't the master of the leaders be even more powerful?
"I erased my master's memory. His memory cannot appear in the minds of any living beings on the other side, so even I can't recall his appearance …" the Primeval Lord of Heaven said.
"No wonder! I was wondering why only nine people completed the End. Shouldn't there be ten people? "Luo Zheng said.
At the end of Luo Zheng's memory, he also watched the human race complete the Path of the End and fight against the needle.
Back then, King Ashoka had mentioned that the human race was especially well-prepared to complete the End. The Wing King of the Green Jade Civilization's method was only ranked eighth.
King Ashoka said that there were ten humans, but in the memory given by the Primeval Lord of Heaven, Luo Zheng clearly saw nine people.
Luo Zheng thought that Prince Ashoka had remembered wrongly, but now it seemed that there was more to it than meets the eye.
However, a wise man like the Heavenly Primogenitor was keenly aware of the details in Luo Zheng's words. He asked, "I only gave you nine people in my memory. You seem to be sure that there are ten people?"
"Well …" Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment before answering truthfully, "I heard from another place that there are ten people from the human race who plan to complete The End, and the first person is called the Ancestor of Humanity. It is said that the Nirvana Token used is the Heart of Limits and the Great Overdraft Stone …"
Luo Zheng had memorized every single word that Prince Ashoka had said, even though he had never seen why the Heart of Limits and the Great Overdraft Stone were Nirvana Tokens.
"Who is the other one?" Yuanshi Tianzun asked.
King Ashoka had originally gotten rid of Luo Zheng to search for the Nirvana Realm humans. Now that Luo Zheng had found them, there was no need to hide. He said, "That person is also a member of the 77th civilization, but he is not in the Nirvana Realm …"
"What? Impossible! "
Yuanshi Tianzun's eyes were filled with disbelief.
As a member of the 77th civilization, he naturally knew about the human race.
Other than the batch that entered the Nirvana Realm, the rest of the humans in the 77th civilization had all fallen and become part of the Chaos.
Other than the top masters, this batch of humans in the Nirvana Realm were either dead or injured. Most of them hid in the 33 Levels of Heaven to prolong their feeble existence.
As for humans like Luo Zheng, they were the seeds that Yuanshi Tianzun and the rest had quietly spread …
Other than these three places, there could not be any other exceptions!
Luo Zheng's heart was filled with admiration for this Yuanshi Tianzun. Seeing his shocked expression, he could not help but smile and said, "That person is indeed from the 77th civilization. However, he was able to escape the destruction of the Chaos because of an exception."
"Exception? What exception? "Yuanshi Tianzun asked in confusion.
"The Child of Truth of the 77th civilization banished him into a unique space," Luo Zheng said.
"That unique space is called the Abandoned Ash Land. The Child of Truth you mentioned is a member of the Red Cloud Beast Clan. This Red Cloud Beast is on the opposite side of us. It has banished many of its clansmen, including my son, Tung Wang Kung." Yuanshi Tianzun nodded. "However, the Abandoned Ash Land will be emptied every once in a while. Everything in it will be cleaned up!"
Tung Wang Kung was Emperor Dong Hua. He was thrown into the Abandoned Ash Land and was completely emptied. Naturally, he could not follow Yuanshi Tianzun to the Nirvana Realm.
"I heard that the space will indeed be emptied. However, that person not only avoided it but also destroyed it. Since the 77th civilization, everything that was thrown into the space has been preserved. That person even took away some of the rights of the space," Luo Zheng replied.
Yuanshi Tianzun was surprised. He smiled and asked, "A person who can be thrown into the Abandoned Ash Land by the Red Cloud Beast can't be a nobody. There really is such a strange existence in the Human Tribe. What is his name?"
"His name is Prince Ashoka," Luo Zheng replied.
"Prince Ashoka?"
Yuanshi Tianzun was stunned. He had an amazing memory. If there was such a person, it was impossible for him not to remember.
"When I was reading the memory given by the predecessor, I also wanted to look for Prince Ashoka. However, no one could match him." Luo Zheng nodded. "I was banished to the Abandoned Ash Land mentioned by the predecessor. There, I met Prince Ashoka. He asked me to find you …"
Luo Zheng also told him what happened in the space.
Strangely, Yuanshi Tianzun was not surprised by the "Mysterious World". Or rather, it seemed that he knew something about it.
After hearing Luo Zheng's story, Yuanshi Tianzun smiled. "He wants to create another Nirvana Realm. Hehe …"
"It's a pity that Prince Ashoka can't enter the Chaos or the Nirvana Realm because of the rules. However, I can still enter it. If the predecessor needs to pass on any message, I can help pass on the message," Luo Zheng said.
"There's no hurry." Yuanshi Tianzun waved his hand as he spoke.
All the illusions collapsed in an instant. The Star Field, the world, and so on all turned into nothingness. It was like a dream.
Luo Zheng stood in the center of the temple hall. Feng Ge and the human woman were nowhere to be seen.
"Young man, come here," the statue in the center said.
Luo Zheng naturally understood that this statue was the Yuanshi Tianzun he saw in the illusion. Logically speaking, these statues around him should have their own wisdom. He had seen them one by one.

Chapter 3273
When they first entered the Heaven-splitting Temple, the Moon Fire Slave wanted to snatch the scroll, but he was killed by the Heavenly Lord of Origin with one finger.
Luo Zheng was naturally aware that the significance of this scroll was probably extraordinary.
"You seem to have cultivated many kinds of True Meaning of Tao?" asked the Heavenly Lord of Origin.
"Yes, there are three kinds in total," Luo Zheng nodded.
Strictly speaking, Luo Zheng cultivated five kinds of True Meaning of Tao.
However, the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction and the True Meaning of Tao in his body could not bear the Nirvana Token.
Luo Zheng could already tell that the Heavenly Lord of Origin wanted Luo Zheng to fuse with the scroll.
The Heavenly Lord of Origin nodded and said, "Give up the position of the True Meaning of Tao. This scroll is the weapon of civilization of the human race. You can fuse with it."
"This …" Luo Zheng hesitated and said, "I'm afraid it's not appropriate."
"What's not appropriate? Do you know the meaning of a weapon of civilization?" The Heavenly Lord of Origin smiled.
There were not many races that could leave behind a weapon of civilization in the Nirvana Realm. The Heavenly Lord of Origin thought that Luo Zheng did not know the meaning of the weapon of civilization.
"I know," Luo Zheng nodded.
The Heavenly Lord of Origin was also a very senior figure. Whether it was the Nirvana Realm or the Chaos, he should be much better than the Green Jade Spirit and 9527.
Not to mention the use of the weapon of civilization of the human race, as long as he could be taught by the Heavenly Lord of Origin, it would be a blessing for Luo Zheng!
"Then why is it not appropriate?" asked the Heavenly Lord of Origin curiously.
"Because the True Meaning of Tao also bears another weapon of civilization," answered Luo Zheng.
The Heavenly Lord of Origin's lifelike statue raised his eyebrows slightly.
The test of the Heaven-splitting Temple was actually not very strict. Because only one Nirvana Token was given out, there was only one chance to choose the target.

Although Luo Zheng could not sense what Evil Deities was doing in the Nirvana Realm, Luo Zheng could still understand through his memories.
"Fairy Qiong Xiao, do you have a way to change memories?" Evil Deities asked.
Evil Deities could erase his memories.
The problem was that after erasing his memories, there would be a blank memory in the body of the Dao Refinement Clone. This would be a confession and would deepen Luo Zheng's suspicion.
Fairy Qiong Xiao looked at Evil Deities impatiently. She reached into the red ball with one of her jade-like arms and pulled gently. She took out a mini dipper. "This item is called the Primordial Chaos Golden Dipper. There is a world in the dipper. This world can change according to your thoughts. You can enter and create a world …"
Evil Deities' eyes flashed. Without hesitation, he jumped into the Primordial Chaos Golden Dipper.
Since the world of the Primordial Chaos Golden Dipper could change according to his thoughts, he thought of the memories of the Thirteenth Heaven, the memories of flipping through the energy vortex, and even the memories of exploring the Fourteenth Heaven Temple. With these memories, he could remove his memories about the Nirvana Realm and deceive Luo Zheng.
…
Evil Deities had a guilty conscience. He suspected that Luo Zheng was trying to forcefully awaken him.
However, at this moment, a sharp horn sounded in the entire Land of Sword Grief. The array in front of every secret cultivation room was activated.
When the array was activated, it also began to awaken the people in the secret cultivation rooms.
Whether they were cultivating in seclusion or walking on the Nirvana Realm, they were all summoned by the array.
Except for a few disciples who could not get out of the temple, whether they were on the first floor or the third floor, they all walked out of the secret cultivation rooms after receiving the order.
Luo Zheng had left the Nirvana Realm early and had been studying the artifacts of the human race's civilization.
After hearing the sound, he raised his eyebrows. He got up and pushed open the door of the secret cultivation room. He saw many disciples on the second floor gathered in the corridor, all of them worried.
"What happened?"
"Is he summoning everyone in the Land of Sword Grief?"
"I'm afraid it's not that simple …"
The disciples in the Land of Sword Grief were considered quite powerful in Tai Yi Heavenly Palace, so the environment in the Land of Sword Grief was very relaxed.
As long as they finished the challenge at the beginning of the month, they were basically free to come and go. It was rare to gather everyone like this.
In the secret cultivation rooms on the third floor, the doors were also opened.
There were only a dozen disciples on the third floor. Even if these people were placed outside the Heavenly Palace, they were basically independent existences.
Even they had strange expressions on their faces when they heard the horn.
"Click …"

Chapter 3278
The moment this woman went out, she attracted the attention of all the male disciples on the third floor.
There were a total of 13 people on the third floor, 12 of them were male and only one woman.
It was this woman who was indeed above all men.
And they were certain that no one in the past or the future would surpass her.
This woman was Li Bianxue, ranked number one in the entire Land of Sword Grief.
In terms of gender, she naturally did not have an advantage. In terms of age, she was also the youngest. But in terms of strength, all the men on the third floor submitted to her.
"Xue 'er, you're out?" A handsome man came over. This man was the eldest son of the Mountain Master of Great Brightness Mountain, Liu Hen, ranked number seven in the third floor.
"Bianxue, where did you go after such a long seclusion? I've been waiting for you in Soul City but you didn't appear, "a long-haired man said softly from the other side.
This person was called Ming Tingyue, the son of the Mountain Master of Great Brightness Mountain.
When the men on the third floor saw Li Bianxue, they could not help but fawn over her.
Li Bianxue just said coldly, "I've been in seclusion recently, I didn't go to Paramita …"
"Did sister Xue 'er's remarkable power have a breakthrough again?" Liu Hen asked.
Li Bianxue shook her head, pointed outside and said, "We're going down, Senior He Chi is here."
With that, she took a step forward.
Her figure flashed and she walked down from the third floor without anyone noticing. It was as if the distance between the two of them was only a single step!
"Li Bianxue greets Senior He Chi," Li Bianxue bowed.
"Xue 'er is too polite," A smile appeared on He Chi's face.
Li Bianxue was introduced to Great Brightness Heavenly Palace by He Chi. She was a gift from the heavens to Great Brightness Heavenly Palace.
Not long later, she would be someone who was on equal footing with He Chi.
But in Li Bianxue's heart, she had always treated He Chi as her master and was very respectful.
"Swish swish swish …"
The people on the third floor also jumped down from the windows and stood beside Li Bianxue, saluting He Chi one after another.
After the people on the third floor arrived, it was the turn of the disciples on the second floor.
Although Luo Zheng didn't know what had happened, he didn't hesitate. He flew up and landed behind the disciples of the Third Floor. At the same time, he cupped his hands and said to He Chi, "Greetings, Senior He Chi!"
"I've heard that you, Luo Zheng, are already ranked second on the Second Floor. You've indeed improved rapidly. You've truly lived up to everyone's expectations," He Chi said with a smile.
Heavenly festivals like He Chi and Qiu Yin River were usually tasked with searching for human geniuses.
The Dao Sword Palace founded by Qiu Yinhe also followed this convention and sent talents to the Great One Heavenly Palace. However, Qiu Yinhe lost a lot of disciples to the Great One Heavenly Palace, but only a few stood out.
He Chi had only brought in a few people, but with Li Bianxue, he was able to suppress the other Tian Jie Du.
Every time Li Bianxue's achievements were mentioned, even Qiu Yinhe would sigh. Li Bianxue was probably the most outstanding disciple since the establishment of Great Yi Heavenly Palace. If given enough time, she could even become a Mountain Lord!
Such a disciple was unprecedented.
When several Tian Jie Du gathered together, they also asserted that there would be no one like her in the future.
But not many years later, He Chi saw the newcomer. Naturally, it was Luo Zheng.
"This is the first on the Second Floor?"
"On the day he joined the Sword of Grief, he became the first on the First Floor. A month later, he became the first on the Second Floor..."
"It seems that he's even faster than Sister Xue 'er..."
The several disciples of the Third Floor had also heard of Luo Zheng, but they rarely came out of seclusion. Today was the first time they saw him.
Because Li Bianxue had been in seclusion for a long time, she was even more isolated from the news. She didn't know about Luo Zheng at all.
On the first day, he became the first on the First Floor. In the first month, he became the second on the Second Floor.
This speed was indeed far beyond Li Bianxue's.
Li Bianxue subconsciously turned her head and sized up Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng stood not far away. After noticing Li Bianxue's gaze, he nodded politely.
On the arena, Luo Zheng had heard of Li Bianxue's name more than once. But Luo Zheng wasn't too sure if the other party was male or female. Today, when he discovered that it was a petite girl, a strange glint flashed across Luo Zheng's eyes.
"You aren't part of this batch that entered the Chaotic Origin Great World?" Li Bianxue directly asked.
She believed that Luo Zheng wasn't part of this batch that just graduated from the Chaotic Origin Great World. Instead, he joined the Sword of Grief midway. Otherwise, it was impossible for him to achieve such results.
Before Luo Zheng could answer, someone had already helped by the side, "He is indeed part of this batch."
Hearing this answer, Li Bianxue's eyes flashed with a strange glint.
Her birth might not be good, but ever since she entered the Sword of Grief, she was treated like a princess.
Even the children of the Mountain Chieftains did not receive such treatment as she did!
Li Bianxue was naturally displeased by the sudden appearance of a person who broke her previous record and soared to the top.
But a genius was still a genius. She never thought that Luo Zheng would challenge her. Instead, she chuckled: "It's already the third month. Don't tell me you are planning to challenge me?"
Whether it was Li Bianxue or others from the Third Floor, it was impossible for Luo Zheng to do so.
Li Bianxue was not Zheng Shuangqi from the Second Floor.
Zheng Shuangqi might be stronger than others from the Second Floor, but the gap between him and the Third Floor was similarly huge.
That was also the reason why Zheng Shuangqi remained in the Second Floor for so long.
Who knew that Luo Zheng would nod and say: "I have the same intention."
Luo Zheng had just inherited the Human Race's book scroll. He was still trying to figure out how to use this Tool of Civilization.
According to what Heavenly Venerable Primordial Beginning said, he would bestow the book scroll with abilities according to Luo Zheng's needs.
One had to know that the book scroll contained the Qi Souls of the 39 Heroes of the Human Race!
Although these Qi Souls were not their true bodies, they had inherited their Divine Abilities. If the book scroll could unleash the power of these Qi Souls, wouldn't it be ever-changing?
Of course, the book scroll's abilities could not be tested in the Sword of Grief. But Luo Zheng had absorbed a large amount of Body Dipper Jades. Although he had not reached the limit of his physical body, he should not be far from the Desolate God Clan Physique Realm …
It would be good to check the enhancement of his physical body.
As for fighting for the first place in the Sword of Grief, that was not important to Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng's reply gave the disciples on the third floor a fright.
"This kid is really daring …"
"I admit that he is a genius, but we are not Zheng Shuangqi!"
"He wants to challenge Li Bianxue, haha …"
A few people from the Third Floor sneered at him.

Chapter 3279
Luo Zheng had just finished speaking when Ming Tingyue from Mount Taixiu snorted coldly. "Chun Hao is Chun Hao, Zheng Shuangqi is Zheng Shuangqi. How can these two good-for-nothings be compared to Beixue?"
When Ming Tingyue said this, Zheng Shuangqi also rushed over and Chun Hao was not far away.

In this short period of time, Luo Zheng's cultivation level should have improved again …

More than 90% of the disciples in the Land of Sword Grief had left the bronze building and gathered in front of He Chi.
It was definitely not a small matter to call all the disciples out.
The originally noisy environment suddenly quietened down and everyone stared at He Chi.

Hearing this, all the disciples' faces were filled with surprise.

Guanshan Prefecture itself was not important, but the entire prefecture was guarding the eastern and western gates. If Guanshan Prefecture was lost, the next few large prefectures would open their doors to Golden Crow Clan. The disciples of the Land of Sword Grief naturally understood the importance of Guanshan Prefecture.
Luo Zheng's face also revealed a surprised expression.
Not long after he entered Dragon City, he had been to Guanshan State and had a deep impression of the towering swords in the Mountain River Fortress. He did not expect it to fall so quickly.
"Yes, after Jin Wu took down Guanshan State, he drove in and took down several states in succession. Now, he has reached Qi Bei State," He Chi said with a solemn expression.

"So fast?"
"Oh my god!"
Not only the disciples from the first floor, but the disciples from the second floor and even the disciples from the third floor had a change in expression.
Qi Bei Prefecture was close to the Center God Province, which meant that the Jinwu Clan had already fought their way to the nest of Taiyi Heavenly Palace. They thought that the Jinwu Clan would move quickly, but they did not expect it to be so fast!
As He Chi spoke to here, his expression became more and more solemn, "As members of the Land of Sword Grief, you are also members of Greater One Heavenly Palace. Now that Greater One Heavenly Palace is in danger, I hope that everyone can contribute to the Heavenly Palace and protect the hundred million clansmen of the Center Divine Province!"
There were many prefectures under Great Yi Heavenly Palace, but ninety percent of them were concentrated in the Center God Province.
If the Golden Crows really invaded the Center God Province, the people in the Center God Province would probably be plunged into misery and suffering. The consequences would be unimaginable.
Both Dragon City and the sword sects of the Seven Mountains had begun to mobilize their disciples. The Land of Sword Grief was no exception.
Many disciples in the Land of Sword Grief maintained their silence at the start. It went without saying what a war between an ultra-powerful clan meant. Even Chaotic Origin Realm experts would most likely die in such a war!
Just as everyone was silent, Liu Hen's voice sounded, "Senior He Chi, you are treating us as outsiders. For us to be where we are today, it is naturally due to the grooming of the Heavenly Palace. It is natural for us to contribute to the Heavenly Palace."
"Brother Liu Hen is right. Those lousy birds will be wiped out by me sooner or later …" Another disciple from the third floor agreed.
"Mere Golden Crows, how dare they …"
Under the lead of the disciples from the third floor, the atmosphere of the entire Land of Sword Grief was rapidly mobilized.
They were part of the Human Race, and the majority of them had their roots in the Center God Province. If Great Yi Heavenly Palace were to be destroyed, they and their clansmen would end up in an incomparably miserable state. At the final step, they had no other choice but to fight to the death.
He Chi looked at these juniors. His mood did not improve at all.
The crisis in the Qi North Prefecture might be troublesome, but the greatest crisis in the Great Yi Heavenly Palace was not the Qi North Prefecture …
"The war in the Qi North Prefecture is imminent. Everyone, make some preparations before gathering at the Dragon City. By then, there will naturally be missions for everyone …" He Chi repeated before handing out golden swords.
The Great Yi Heavenly Palace used golden swords as tokens. Only a five-star Tai Yiwei could wield them.
When the golden swords were handed out, Luo Zheng received two. One of them was naturally given to Evil Deities.
The Land of Sword Grief summoned all those in closed-door cultivation. Evil Deities was naturally included. But until now, Evil Deities had yet to come out of closed-door cultivation.
'Unless they are trapped in the Deity Temple?' Luo Zheng guessed in his heart.
After He Chi left, Luo Zheng and the others returned to the bronze building.
As soon as they entered, they saw Evil Deities striding over. There seemed to be some panic on their faces. After seeing Luo Zheng, Evil Deities respectfully bowed. Luo Zheng asked, "Have you arrived at the Wanling City during your closed-door cultivation?"
"I have arrived at the Wanling City. I inadvertently explored a Deity Temple outside the city. I also gained a little, so I came out too late," Evil Deities replied.
Luo Zheng could not control what Evil Deities did in the other side.
Although Evil Deities had a clear conscience, Luo Zheng still had some doubts.
It was not easy to withdraw from the Deity Temple. If Evil Deities came out after a few days, it would be normal. But it was only a few incense sticks later. This was suspicious.
"Hum …"
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. The memory of Evil Deities in the other side appeared in Luo Zheng's mind.
The Thirteenth Heaven …
Crossing …
Receiving the Soul Crystal in the Wanling City …
Inadvertently obtaining a key to the other side and going to explore …
Evil Deities created a world that did not exist at all in the Primordial Dipper.
Everything Luo Zheng saw was a memory imagined by Evil Deities.
However, Evil Deities himself believed in this memory. After all, his real memory had been taken away.
This was the case with body-refining avatars. If he wanted to deceive his own master, he could only deceive himself.
But Evil Deities himself felt a little strange. It seemed that according to his character, he would not go through the experience in the other side step by step.
When checking his memory, Luo Zheng also found this strange feeling, but he could not tell what was really strange.
After handing the golden sword to Evil Deities, Luo Zheng asked him to prepare.
After returning to his own training room, Luo Zheng took out a piece of white paper and spread it out. After a little hesitation with his right hand holding a brush, he began to write on the white paper. What he wrote was the True Comprehension Chapter of the True Meaning of Dao.
Previously, Luo Zheng hoped that Greater One Heavenly Palace would help him fulfill his long-cherished wish of reviving, and the True Comprehension Chapter was a condition in exchange.

Chapter 3280
After he finished writing the True Comprehension Chapter, Luo Zheng carefully folded it.
He went to the first floor to find Feng Ge and stuffed it into her hands.
"What is this?" Feng Ge asked.
Luo Zheng's lips moved slightly, and he sent a message to Feng Ge, "It's the complete True Comprehension Chapter."
Hearing this, Feng Ge's eyes trembled. Luo Zheng really translated the entire chapter!
If it was in the past, Feng Ge would be even more surprised.
But after staying by Luo Zheng's side for so long, she felt that it was only natural …
"This True Comprehension Chapter originally belonged to Tai Yi Heavenly Palace. I only deciphered it. It should be passed down to all the swordsmen in the world through your father's mouth," Luo Zheng said.
Feng Ge pursed her lips and carefully put away the True Comprehension Chapter.
She was the daughter of the Eastern Sovereign. Now that Tai Yi Heavenly Palace was in trouble, she couldn't hide behind them. But before she went to Qi Bei Province, she had to return to Tai Yi Palace.
After Luo Zheng handed over the True Comprehension Chapter, he made some preparations and set off for Dragon City with Evil Deities.
For a long time, Dragon City had been the most lively place.
But recently, Dragon City seemed a bit deserted.
Whether it was inside or outside the city, it was like this.
Most Tai Yi Wei were concentrated in the Mountain River Fortress. When the Golden Crow attacked the Mountain River Fortress, countless Tai Yi Wei resisted with all their might, but in the end, they suffered heavy casualties.
The Banner Masters and Alliance Masters outside Dragon City originally wanted to become Tai Yi Wei, but they had to go through many tests.
But a few days ago, Dragon City suddenly changed the rules.
As long as they made a certain amount of contributions, they would automatically be promoted to Tai Yi Wei!
For the majority of people, this was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity. For a time, all the Alliance Masters and Banner Masters took their people to the battlefield.
Naturally, there were very few people who stayed outside Dragon City.
When Luo Zheng passed by Dragon City, he originally wanted to visit his former companions in Dao Sword Palace, but unexpectedly found that they had already left Dragon City.
Even the people under the banner of the Greater One Heavenly Palace, who were not very strong, were all conscripted away. This time the trouble that the Greater One Heavenly Palace had encountered was most likely not small.
After arriving at the Hidden Dragon Pavilion in Dragon City, Luo Zheng and Evil Deities showed their golden swords. Several Tai Yiwei stepped forward to verify the logo on the golden swords and then took them to the teleportation array. After passing through the teleportation array, they went straight to Qi Bei Province.
…
Although the Qi Bei Province was close to the Center God Province, the population there was much smaller.
In such a large province, there were only sixteen cities. The total population of fifteen of these cities was equivalent to that of a medium-sized city next to Lake Ming.
There was only one large city in the entire Qi Bei Region, and it was called Beixuan City.
Because of the war, the ordinary people in Beixuan City had already retreated to the Center God Province.
Now, Beixuan City had been taken over by Tai Yiwei Heavenly Palace as a stronghold.
When Luo Zheng came out from the spatial passage, he had already arrived at Beixuan City.
Many Tai Yiwei came and went in a hurry. Other than Tai Yiwei, there were many other Paramita Realm experts. From their auras, a portion of them were only at the second or third level of the Paramita Realm …
Everyone had an abnormally solemn and silent expression.
Even though there was no smell of blood in Beixuan City, the oppressive atmosphere of war still lingered in the hearts of everyone.
"Disciples of the Land of Sword Grief, please come this way." A five-star Tai Yiwei cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng and Evil Deities.
The Land of Sword Grief had a very high status in the Greater One Heavenly Palace. The weakest had reached the Desire Realm. Even the strongest five-star Tai Yiwei could not reach the Desire Realm — when a Tai Yiwei reached the Desire Realm, he would naturally be recommended to head to the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Hence, the Tai Yiwei were very respectful towards the people from the Land of Sword Grief.
Under the guidance of Tai Yiwei, Luo Zheng and the others passed through half the city and arrived before a spacious pavilion.
Inside the pavilion, some of the disciples from the Land of Sword of Grief had already gathered. There were about twenty of them, and Luo Zheng even saw a few familiar faces among them. After He Chi had mobilized them, many disciples had rushed here.
"Brother Luo Zheng!"
"Brother Luo Zheng!"
"…"
Several disciples from the First Floor greeted Luo Zheng. As for the disciples from the Second Floor, they looked at Luo Zheng indifferently. They did not express much.
After all, Luo Zheng's improvement was too fast. Even though he had defeated Zheng Shuangqi, it was not possible for him to convince everyone from the Second Floor.
As for the disciples from the First Floor, most of them had gone to the Fourteenth Heaven. In the City of Wanling, they had received the Soul Crystal that they were familiar with. Through a few Nirvana Realm races, they had also received many benefits. While they were shocked by Luo Zheng's means in the Nirvana Realm, they were also very grateful to Luo Zheng.
After the Tai Yiwei arranged for Luo Zheng, Evil Deities, and the others to stay here, they left.
Luo Zheng did not know how the battle in Qi Bei Province was going. Since he had already arrived at Beixuan City, he could just wait here.
However, what Luo Zheng did not expect was that he would wait for twelve to fourteen hours.
Occasionally, Tai Yiwei would send the disciples from the Land of Sword Grief who had rushed over. Those Tai Yiwei would then leave. If someone pulled those Tai Yiwei to ask about the arrangements, they would not know anything.
Luo Zheng and Evil Deities, on the other hand, were determined. They just sat cross-legged in their seats and did not say a word.
After more than twenty hours, it was already the next morning.
The disciples of the first floor and even a few of the second floor could no longer hold themselves back.
"We responded to Senior He Chi and came to the Qi Bei Province to kill the Golden Crow. What is the meaning of this?"
"They also said that the battle in the Qi Bei Province is urgent. I think they are very free and don't need us …"
"..."
A few disciples of the Land of Sword Grief started to clamor.
At this moment, a tall and sturdy man entered the pavilion with several five star Tai Yiwei.
The man had a crew cut, and his eyebrows were like arrows. Every move he made gave off a threatening murderous aura. This kind of murderous aura was cultivated through constant slaughter. Although the disciples of the Sword of Grief were powerful, they rarely killed, so they naturally couldn't cultivate such a rich murderous aura.
Luo Zheng had walked through a mountain of blades and a sea of flames in the past, so he was already used to this kind of killing intent. After sizing up the man, his expression returned to normal.
"Disciples of the Land of Sword Grief," the man with the crew cut cupped his hands towards the crowd after entering, "You are all outstanding figures of the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace, the pearls of the seven mountains, the hope of the Heavenly Palace in the future …"
The disciples of the Land of Sword Grief were originally very anxious, but after being praised by the man with the crew cut, a proud smile appeared on their faces. However, this smile only lasted for a moment.
The man with the crew cut suddenly changed the topic, "No matter how important you are, no matter how strong you are, in my eyes, you are no different from trash. If you want to live, then put away that ridiculous pride in your hearts."

Chapter 3281
The disciples from the Sword Grief Ground were all at the Chaotic Origin Realm. Even the disciples from the First Floor were considered experts in their Mother World.
How could they tolerate being called trash by this person?
A disciple from the Second Floor raised his eyebrows and said: "Your Excellency is a God General of my Heaven Palace. Naturally, your strength is indisputable. But your Excellency is too narrow-minded to call us trash the moment you open your mouth."
"Divine Generals also came from the Land of Sword Grief. According to your conclusion, you're also considered trash?" A disciple from the first floor retorted.
The experts that led Tai Yiwei were the Heaven Palace God Generals. They were considered the commanders of the army of the Tai Yi Heaven Palace.
The status of these God Generals was one level lower than Tian Jiedu. There were also several disciples from the Second Floor training in the Sword Grief Ground. If they were experienced enough, they could be conferred the title of God General.
Even though God Generals were strong, the disciples from the Sword Grief Ground would not be submissive to them.
Many disciples from the Sword Grief Ground burst out with their auras. They were also overbearing. But the expression of the crew-cut man did not change. A crafty smile appeared on his face, "You, you, and you …"
Every time he pointed at someone, the golden swords beside them would shine. When he pointed at Luo Zheng, the golden swords beside Luo Zheng would also shine.
This time, the situation in the Qi Bei Prefecture was extremely dangerous. They lacked high-end experts.
After hearing that the disciples from the Sword Grief Ground had come, many God Generals returned to the North Xuan City. According to the rules set by the higher-ups, each of them could choose five assistants.
The crew-cut man was called Su Kui. He was the first to return to the North Xuan City.
He knew that the battlefield was bloody and cruel. Under the situation of similar strength, he was naturally willing to choose those with calm temperament. Hence, he spoke arrogantly the moment he arrived, infuriating the disciples from the Sword Grief Ground.
And Luo Zheng and the others were calm. Naturally, they were chosen by Su Kui.
Su Kui had originally chosen the Evil Deities. But when he met the eyes of the Evil Deities, Su Kui's heart could not help but skip a beat. Relying on the resolve he gained from fighting for a long time, he instinctively resisted the Evil Deities. In the end, he passed this person.
"The five of you, follow me. As for the other friends … I have offended you previously!"
Su Kui bowed deeply to the crowd. In the blink of an eye, his attitude became very sincere.
The disciples of the Sword of Grief were not fools. They immediately understood the kid's intention. Many rolled their eyes while others smiled helplessly. These Godly Generals of Tai Yiwei were too cunning.
When Luo Zheng got up, he glanced at the Evil Deities and transmitted his thoughts to the Evil Deities. "If someone chooses you, you will do your best to assist them. When you encounter danger, your life is the most important thing."
"Yes, Lord." Evil Deities nodded.
Not long after Luo Zheng and the other four left with Su Kui, two more Immortal Generals entered the pavilion.
As soon as these two Divine Generals entered, they used the same strategy as Su Kui. It seemed that they also wanted to intimidate everyone.
However, the people of the Sword of Grief had already experienced the same trick once. Most of them looked at them as if they were idiots.
In this way, the disciples of the Sword of Grief continued to rush over from the Dragon City. At the same time, they were also led away by the Divine Generals.
These Divine Generals were mostly fierce and resolute, but for some reason, they always ignored the Evil Deities when selecting people. As these people were led away, the Evil Deities still sat in the pavilion, unmoving like a mountain.
… …
The Qi North Region was full of smoke and miasma. For many years, it was shrouded in smoke.
After coming out of Beixuan City, Luo Zheng and the others followed Su Kui all the way north.
"The Golden Crow Clan has taken over the Mountain River Fortress, and millions of people have died. The Taiyi Guards have also suffered heavy casualties, with 30,000 of them dead. Even more than 30 Divine Generals have died," Su Kui explained the situation as he flew.
All the Tai Yiwei in the Tai Yiwei Heavenly Palace added up to no more than 80,000 people. More than a third of them had been lost. This loss was indeed too great.
And the number of Divine Generals was no more than 200.
The death of more than 30 was something the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace could not bear.
"The Mountain River Fortress is easy to defend and hard to attack. There is a transmission formation inside. Even if it is broken, they should be able to retreat easily," the pale-faced scholar behind Luo Zheng asked.
This pale-faced scholar was called Gu Hai, ranked 33rd on the Second floor.
Before Su Kui answered, Luo Zheng said, "Is it the World Cage?"
When Luo Zheng went to the Mountain View Region, he had experienced the World Cage. This cage could lock the space of a large region, and no one could perform greater teleportation.
Su Kui nodded, "Yes, it is the World Cage."
"Could it be that even an existence like Divine General Su is unable to break free from the World Cage?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
As far as Luo Zheng knew, Phoenix had the ability to break free from the World Cage and perform greater teleportation. The Divine Generals were weaker than Phoenix, but not by much.
Su Kui sighed, "If the World Cage targeted the entire region, it would not be able to trap us Divine Generals, but Di Jun led the Golden Crows to launch a surprise attack and drove us Human Race to this corner of the Mountain River Fortress. The power of the cage only targeted the area of one fortress.
We can't break free... "
Su Kui was also in the Mountain River Fortress at that time. Under the leadership of other seniors, he fought and retreated, and was lucky enough to escape.
But Su Kui also had a younger brother who was also a Divine General, but he had fallen in the Mountain River Fortress.
Speaking of this, Su Kui's fierce eyes faintly revealed a sadness...
After occupying several large regions, the Golden Crows finally stopped in the Qi Bei Region.
Now, Tai Yiwei's task was to desperately block the Golden Crows in the Qi Bei Region.
"From the northwest side of Beixuan City, there are seven formations and twelve passes. I'm in charge of guarding the Inferior Zhen Pass, which is also the most dangerous pass. Everyone, please follow me and be careful," Su Kui said with a smile.
Hearing Su Kui's words, the faces of several disciples of the Land of Sword Grief turned ugly.
The situation was already very dangerous, and they still had to guard the most dangerous Inferior Zhen Pass. Wouldn't their lives be easily lost here?

There are twelve God Generals in it, and there are seventeen in total, which is far more than the other eleven passes. Moreover, once the Golden Crow invades Xiaozhen Pass en masse …
If the Golden Crows enter this pass, the other passes will also come to rescue. "
Gu Hai also nodded in agreement, "Qi Bei Region is the last pass of the Heavenly Palace. The Heavenly Palace will definitely block the Golden Crows here. There should be many seniors from the Seven Mountains here. Everyone, don't be pessimistic."

Not far behind our Inferior Zhen Pass is the Taihao Formation. There are experts from the Taihao Mountain guarding it, "Su Kui said confidently.
"Taihao Mountain?"

Chapter 3282
The conflict between Luo Zheng and the Great Hao Mountain was not small.

A few days ago, the Golden Crow launched an attack, but it was blocked by the Heavenly Net. Let's go down. "

Luo Zheng was a Heavenly General with a golden sword in his hand.

After the person on the lower level was scolded by the four-star Tai Yiwei, he did not dare to say anything and immediately went back.

The army guarding Qi Bei Province was divided into three types. The strongest type was the God Generals, such as Su Kui and Luo Zheng. The second type was the Tai Yiwei. Chapter 3283

The first three days of Luo Zheng's arrival at Biaozhen Pass were peaceful. Not a single Golden Crow appeared.
Several Divine Generals, including Su Kui, were deeply worried about this. They felt that the calmer it was, the more a storm was brewing.
During these three days, Mount Taihao sent three people to inspect the pass. The seventeen Divine Generals of Biaozhen Pass naturally had to come out to welcome them.
Luo Zheng had never seen these three people from Mount Taihao before. During the inspection, their gazes would intentionally or unintentionally sweep past Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng naturally sensed this, but he didn't show it. Instead, he was sneering inside.

Chapter 3284
The difference in strength between the Banshees within the Godly Nest was huge.

As long as the Golden Crows did not break through the Heavenly Net Great Array, they would not be able to harm the people in the nine array cores. This way, the people in the array cores would be in an invincible position.

The entire Formation Eye was a circular Magic Array. It was filled with faint rays of light. What made Luo Zheng feel strange was that these rays of light seemed to have a mass. They were like a layer of oil diffusing in the air, circling around him.
When Luo Zheng wanted to take a step towards the edge of the Formation Eye, the entire Formation Eye unexpectedly moved along with Luo Zheng.
"This Formation Eye can actually move by itself?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
When Luo Zheng took a step back, the Formation Eye also retreated. When he took a step to the left, the Formation Eye also moved to the left.

"This Formation Eye can move with me. What if there are two people?" Luo Zheng asked.
"If there are two people, we need to move together. Otherwise, both of us will stand at the edge of the Formation Eye while the Formation Eye stays in place," Zhu Xuan answered while walking in the opposite direction of Luo Zheng.
In this way, the Formation Eye would naturally not move if the two of them stood at opposite ends of the Formation Eye.
Seeing Luo Zheng nod, Zhou Xuan continued, "In addition, this Formation Eye can also help us transform into weapons."
As he spoke, he stretched out his hand and released the power of Shen Jun.
"Whoosh whoosh …"
A transparent longsword floated out from outside the Formation Eye.
Zhu Xuan held the longsword in the air and suddenly slashed towards the distance.
The transparent longsword also slashed out a ray of sword light, which flew into the distance …

Luo Zheng nodded slightly and stretched out his hand. After releasing the power of Shen Jun, a longsword floated outside the Formation Eye. This longsword was under Luo Zheng's control.

Luo Zheng clasped his hands together and the two swords turned into four swords, four swords turned into eight swords, eight swords turned into sixteen …
Countless long swords floated around Luo Zheng. Each of them contained ten godly jun of power. There were a total of one hundred and twenty-eight transparent long swords, which cost Luo Zheng over one thousand godly jun of power.
More than a thousand Shen Jun's power was nothing to a Divine General.
However, as the longswords danced around the Formation Eye, they were like swarming locusts. There was no sense of disorder at all.
Zhu Xuan and the other Divine Generals in the Formation Eye were slightly taken aback when they saw this. Zhu Xuan then smiled and said, "Divine General Luo Zheng's attainments in the Strength Divine Dao are already quite deep. I'm truly impressed."

Gu Hai stood to the side and didn't say anything, but he was laughing coldly in his heart.
Flashy but useless?
Flashy but useless. If he could become the number one cultivator on the Second Floor in just a few months, then no one in the world could be considered as being flashy but useless.
After the seventeen people finished their preparations in the nine Formation Eye, the "dark clouds" in front of them grew closer and closer.
"Caw caw caw caw …"

Chapter 3285
These Golden Crows had extremely strong penetrating power. If they hit the formation eye, they would be able to put considerable pressure on the inferior barrier.

The four disciples of the Land of Sword Grief used different sword skills. Their sword skills were ingenious and full of beauty, but they were slightly lacking in power.

Although the Golden Crow's intelligence is low, but do not underestimate their agility.
If he were to try his luck, he might not even be able to hit a single Golden Crow. If he was lucky, he might be able to hit a few …
But when Zhu Xuan took a closer look, her eyes widened.
The number of Golden Crows Luo Zheng's side was more than a hundred times more than Zhu Xuan's.
However, each of Luo Zheng's transparent swords seemed to have a soul as they kept piercing upwards.
"Pa pa pa pa …"
Every time the sword pierced through a Golden Crow, the Golden Crow would explode into a bloody mist.
There were more than a hundred bloody mists lingering in the sky, almost forming a strange yet beautiful scenery!
Every time the swords pierced upwards, thousands of Golden Crows were killed. As Luo Zheng went back and forth a few times, perhaps more than a hundred thousand Golden Crows had died under his swords.
These Golden Crows were low in intelligence, but they were not afraid of death. The fiercer Luo Zheng killed, the more the Golden Crows were replenished. However, no matter how many Golden Crows rushed forward, they were still not enough to replace the number of Golden Crows killed by Luo Zheng. The sky above Luo Zheng was forcefully cleared out …
…
"That Immortal General is so powerful!"
"He can control more than a hundred swords by himself, which is equivalent to a hundred Immortal Generals!"
"Great, now we don't have to do anything!"
When the Nirvana Realm cultivators at the lower level of the Lesser Zhen Pass saw this scene, they were so excited that they were about to cry.
The nine formation eyes looked indestructible, but this formation went through the Heavenly Net and was located in the Lesser Zhen Pass.
If the formation eyes were to withstand too many attacks, the entire Lesser Zhen Pass would collapse.
Therefore, once the formation eyes were under too much threat, Su Kui would let the Tai Yiwei release the Nirvana Realm cultivators at the lower level. That would be the most tragic moment for these Nirvana Realm cultivators.
However, Luo Zheng had killed all the Golden Crows by himself, so he naturally didn't need these Nirvana Realm cultivators. Therefore, it was natural for these Nirvana Realm cultivators to be happy.
"Hey, Yue Baihao, you said he's really your Banner Lord?" an old man asked with his eyes wide open.
Yue Baihao nodded proudly and said, "That's right! He was once from our Dao Sword Palace! He was the one who sent us to Dragon City! "
"How many years have you been in Dragon City? He grew from a mere Banner Lord to such a level?" another muscular man asked with disbelief.
Although Yue Baihao had previously called Luo Zheng as a Banner Lord and Luo Zheng had agreed, these people still found it hard to believe.
After all, a considerable portion of these Nirvana Realm cultivators were from the Center God Province. They had a certain understanding of the many experts in Tai Yi Heaven Palace. In a few years … it was already unbelievable for a Banner Lord to become an Alliance Lord. To become a Tai Yiwei, one needed great luck and great talent. Luo Zheng had directly become
A Divine General!
Even though they had seen it with their own eyes, they still found it hard to believe.
"If you don't believe me, you can ask Lu Gou and Da Hui. They were all born in Dragon City and have seen Banner Lord before!" Yue Baihao said.
When Luo Zheng was a Banner Lord, he had defeated the Alliance Lord. He had left a deep impression on the Banner Lords outside Dragon City. There were quite a number of people who knew Luo Zheng.
"To be honest, if Banner Lord Luo Zheng didn't admit it, I wouldn't believe that they are the same person!"
"Even though he easily defeated Situ Xiu back then, I didn't expect him to grow to this extent. It's unbelievable …"

"Shua shua shua!"

The three sword lights tore through the sky, leaving behind traces of spatial cracks as they slashed towards that Golden Crow Corpse Spirit.

The Sword Dao divine ability Su Kui cultivated was abnormally tyrannical. But when the sword lights landed on the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit, they did not even leave a scratch.

Su Kui revealed a look of disappointment when he saw this.
When he was defending the Inferior Zhen Pass, Su Kui was prepared to die.

Once Su Kui had such a thought, his fighting spirit immediately decreased, but he still continued to fight.

As for Su Kui below, he was completely dumbfounded as he watched Luo Zheng effortlessly seize the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit.
Putting aside Luo Zheng's strange means, since the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit was a weapon of the Golden Crow Clan, it was impossible to snatch it. But Luo Zheng just lightly jumped and the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit under his feet was already under his control. This was simply inconceivable!

High up in the sky.

No matter how tough the Heavenly Net was, if it kept burning like this, it would eventually collapse. It just needed a long time.

This was even more illusory than a dream!

Phoenix's heart was filled with grief and indignation. She just wanted to tear Luo Zheng apart and swallow him whole. Her shoulders were trembling slightly.

Wisps of flame that were as thin as gossamer were ignited on the surface of her body.

"You are indeed an opponent worthy of my respect," Phoenix said as she grabbed the flaming spear. "I can give you a chance to challenge me and even kill me. How about it?"
Phoenix's voice carried a trace of magic, tempting Luo Zheng to agree to this duel.
Although Luo Zheng's cultivation was at a disadvantage, he controlled hundreds of Golden Crow corpse spirits!
In terms of fighting strength, a Golden Crow corpse spirit was actually not much weaker than Phoenix. Luo Zheng had a great advantage...

The other Golden Crows turned around and faced Phoenix. As long as Luo Zheng willed it, they would besiege Phoenix.

Luo Zheng revealed an interested expression and said, "I'm interested."
Phoenix's eyes flashed. As long as she could take down Luo Zheng with her own strength, then the plan could proceed as planned.
Just when Phoenix thought she had grasped a glimmer of opportunity, Luo Zheng said again, "As if!"
"Whoosh …"

"Want to escape?
Not so easy! "
"Whoosh!"
Phoenix controlled the Golden Crow corpse spirit to avoid the blue flames and chased after Luo Zheng.
