No matter what, as long as she caught Luo Zheng, she could intercept these Golden Crow corpse spirits.

Why is he looking at me like this?
What did I miss?
Whatever!

Chapter 3299

Two incense sticks …
Three incense sticks …
After the two arrays attacked each other for an hour, a huge crack suddenly appeared on the surface of the Heavenly Net.
"Crack!"
It was as if an exquisite glass cup had cracked open.
Gui Ling, Phoenix, and the others in the Six Rivets Array were just about to rejoice when they saw this. However, the six mystical patterns on the Six Rivets Array also started to crack like fish scales.
"Peep, peep, peep …"
The Six Rivets Array was also unable to withstand the power and started to collapse.
"They can't take it anymore!"
"I hope the Heavenly Net Array can trap them to death …"
"…"
Most of the low-level cultivators did not like this battle. The chances of them surviving were too small. Even if they won, the rewards they received would not be worth the risk!
Gan Gaohan's old face became serious when he saw this. "Comrades of the Seven Mountains, the victory of our Heavenly Palace depends on this!"
"Buzz, buzz, buzz, buzz …"
The arrays in the Seven Mountains all lit up with a monstrous light. Everyone was on high alert.
Luo Zheng's thoughts also changed slightly.
Over a hundred Golden Crow Corpse Spirits started to gather azure light in their mouths. Blue flames were about to burst out at any moment.
"Boom!"
The Heavenly Net was the first to collapse.
The crack that stretched from the ground to the sky was directly pulled open. The entire array started to disappear as the crack extended.
However, the moment the Heavenly Net collapsed, the six arrays in the Six Rivets Array also collapsed into a sparkling and translucent powder …
"Go!"
Luo Zheng was the first to react.
Seeing the Six Rivets Array shatter, the blue flames in the mouths of the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits turned into pillars of light and shot toward the center of the Six Rivets Array.
"Whoosh!"
"Whoosh!"
"Whoosh!"
The experts around the Supreme One Sect all rose from the ground. The massive Heavenly Chasm Swords were also pulled out.
Gan Gaohan, Qiu Yinhe, and the others wielded the Heavenly Chasm Swords with ease.
However, when Li Bianxue pulled out the massive Heavenly Chasm Swords, a trace of strain appeared on her pretty face. However, she still gritted her teeth and followed the rhythm of her seniors.
Apart from the Supreme One Sect, the Great Light Array, Great Gold Array, and Great Beauty Array all launched their attacks. Both sides had reached the most critical moment. Everyone was giving it their all.
Right at this moment, a purple light soared into the sky from the Great Light Array.
Xu Shou's face flashed with a hint of sinister excitement. The purple light that soared into the sky did not fall into the broken Six Rivets Array. Instead, it split into six huge purple swords and pierced towards the other six arrays!
Since Xu Shou had chosen to rebel, he would naturally strike at the most critical moment.

Chapter 3303
The purple longsword descended from the sky and headed straight for the six arrays. The expressions of the powerhouses in the other six arrays changed drastically.
"What's Taihao Mountain doing!"
"Xu Shou is rebelling!"
"To turn around and attack at this time, he really hid his strength well!"
The seven mountains had been accumulating power to fight against the Golden Crows in the Six Rivets Array. However, the Taihao Formation had used the accumulated power on their comrades.
"Brother Gan, don't be distracted. I'll handle it!"
Ning Xuyuan flew up, and the Heavenly Chasm Sword under his feet shone with golden light.
Whoosh!
The Heavenly Chasm Sword turned around and shot straight up into the sky.
Xu Shou was powerful, and the Taihao Formation was powerful as well. However, Xu Shou's attack was split into six parts. Ning Xuyuan could still take it if he tried his best.
For example, Mount Taijin and Mount Taixiu could also take it, but because of Mount Taihao, they would be distracted.
The moment the Six Rivets Array shattered, a strong spatial fluctuation erupted from within the array. The group of people teleported and disappeared in an instant!
The blue flames spat out by the Golden Crow Zombie Spirit and the thunderbolts released by the other mountains missed!
The Heaven and Earth Cage had already descended from the beginning of the battle in Qinbei State.
However, people like Phoenix, Qiu Yinhe, and Elder Gan were still able to break free from the Heaven and Earth Cage.
However, as the battle continued to advance southward, the Heaven and Earth Cage's range shrank.
The smaller the range, the stronger the Heaven and Earth Cage's power.
When the Heaven and Earth Cage's range shrank to ten thousand miles, even people like Elder Gan couldn't use the Great Space Shift.
However, the Golden Crow Clan still had a divine ability that could resist the Heaven and Earth Cage!
This was Gui Ling's second secret plan!
Hum!
Gui Ling and the other eight figures appeared in the Taixiu Formation.
The moment Gui Ling appeared, many powerhouses in the Taixiu Formation were stunned.
When they saw all the Golden Crows disappear, they were secretly on guard. However, they never thought that the Jinwu people would be so bold as to teleport directly into the formation.
Gui Ling glanced at the Mount Taixiu Master, who was close by, and spat out two words. "Break the formation!"
Then, the pitch-black wings flapped and swept towards the Mountain Lord of Mount Taixiu.
The Mountain Lord of Mount Taixiu squinted his eyes and swung his blood-red longsword at Gui Ling's head. The two cultivators were locked in a fierce battle.
It was also Gui Ling's idea to move the Golden Crows into the various formations in batches.
Although the Heavenly Net Formation had been torn apart, the seven formations were still guarding the seven formations.
Ru Tai Yi was the "Thirteen Spirit Sword Formation of the Heavenly Moat". The power of the thirteen Heavenly Moat Swords was not to be underestimated.
The Supreme Beauty Array was the "Bloody Storm Array" …
Another example would be the Supreme Gold Formation …
Through Greater Teleportation, Gui Ling, Phoenix, and more than a hundred people could indeed teleport away. After all, Skynet had been broken.
However, the Golden Crow Clan's goal was not only to break the Heavenly Net, but also to take down this batch of Mountain Chieftains.
Breaking the Seven Mountains and Seven Formations was a must. Of course, the Great Hao Formation was not part of this.
While Gui Ling and the Mountain Lord of Mount Taixiu were locked in a fierce battle, the others attacked one after another and tore the entire Supreme Beauty Array into pieces.
On the Taiyi Sect's side, Luo Zheng saw the Jinwu cultivators disappear in an instant when he commanded the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit.
The next moment, a pair of azure blue wings suddenly appeared in front of Luo Zheng. At the end of the wings was Phoenix.
Phoenix was the executor of the Jinwu. Gui Ling was the real think tank of the Jinwu. When Gui Ling proposed a new plan, Phoenix agreed without hesitation.
She hated Luo Zheng to the core and could not tolerate Luo Zheng living for another breath.
Of course, there were other Jinwu cultivators who teleported along with Phoenix. However, they were dealing with Gan Gaohan, Qiu Yinhe, and other powerful cultivators.
"You don't have a choice now," Phoenix said with a sinister smile. Her snow-white and delicate face had a unique charm even when it was sinister.
"Whoosh …"
As soon as Phoenix finished speaking, her two wings had already enveloped Luo Zheng.
The pair of wings was like a pair of large hands that "hugged" Luo Zheng.
At the same time, the flaming spear in Phoenix's hand stabbed directly at Luo Zheng!
No matter how crafty Luo Zheng was, he had no chance of winning in the face of absolute power.
Under the restraint of the Heaven and Earth Cage, this fellow could not possibly teleport away like last time …
The fellow who had opposed her time and time again was finally going to die in her hands. Phoenix actually heaved a sigh of relief in her heart, as if she had finally killed a strong opponent.
With a flap of Phoenix's wings, Luo Zheng's body leaned forward. Just as the flaming spear was about to enter his body, Luo Zheng took in a deep breath. With a roll, he attempted to dodge the spear.
But Phoenix's reaction was much faster than Luo Zheng's reaction. No matter how Luo Zheng rolled left and right, that small and lethal flaming spear in her hand would always follow him like a shadow. It was as if it was not obstructed at all.
"Xiu —"
Accompanied by the whistling sound of the flaming spear, the tip of the spear tapped on Luo Zheng's chest.
"Feng!"
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had become a flaming man.
Luo Zheng had remained unscathed in the flames several times. Phoenix did not expect this flame to incinerate Luo Zheng.
However, this spear contained the power of 15,000 Godly Catties!
Phoenix had personally witnessed Luo Zheng breaking through to the Chaotic Origin Realm. He had only left the Chaotic Origin Great World for a few months and was still at the Early Stage Chaotic Origin Realm!
Let alone 15,000 Divine Jun, just 6,000 to 7,000 Divine Jun was enough to touch the limit of his Chaotic Origin Spirit, causing his fleshy body to crumble into pieces.
But some things would always exceed one's expectations.
"Ding!"
When Phoenix's spear struck Luo Zheng's body, it merely emitted a crisp sound.
The Chaotic Origin Spirits within his body had withstood this spear strike. The Chaotic Origin Spirits began weakening rapidly. But just as they were about to fall into exhaustion, that True Dao Intent rapidly stirred once again. It gathered within every Chaotic Origin Spirit, rapidly restoring the Chaotic Origin Spirits to their original state!
"Mn?"
Phoenix stared at Luo Zheng dumbfoundedly. And Luo Zheng even had the time to smile at Phoenix.
"Die!"
Phoenix did not care about anything else as she unleashed all the energy in her body. The energy gathered on the flaming spear had reached a terrifying 25,000 Divine Jun. This was already close to her limit.
She could not understand how Luo Zheng managed to block her first spear strike. She did not want to figure it out either. Instead, she directly unleashed a strength of 20,000 Divine Jun!
"Sou —"
The flaming spear was propelled by a powerful force. It let out a sharp whistle as it flew towards Luo Zheng's head.
But facing this spear, Luo Zheng still had a faint smile on his face.
Could it be that he could still withstand this spear strike of hers?

Chapter 3304
Those Golden Crow Zombie Spirits were completely under Luo Zheng's command.
When Luo Zheng was trapped by Phoenix, all the scattered Golden Crow Zombie Spirits had already surrounded him and Phoenix.
However, Phoenix's movements were indeed very fast. Before the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits could spit out blue flames, Phoenix had already stabbed Luo Zheng with her spear.
When Phoenix realized that her spear had no effect, the blue flames from the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits had already swept over her.
Even though Phoenix was also good at controlling fire, she couldn't bathe in flames like Luo Zheng.
She couldn't stab her spear down no matter what …
Phoenix's extremely slender body fluttered backward like a piece of weightless paper. She passed through the gaps between the blue flames, and Luo Zheng was baptized by the blue flames.
Phoenix originally planned to increase the distance between them and then find another opportunity to take down Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng obviously didn't plan to let Phoenix leave so easily!
Just as she passed through the blue flames, three Golden Crow Zombie Spirits had already surrounded her. The three Golden Crow Zombie Spirits spread out their wings like sharp blades.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The power of the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits' casual slashes also reached 20,000 Shen Jun. Phoenix couldn't resist them, and her body was still fluttering along with her wings.
Just as she dodged the three Golden Crow Zombie Spirits, more than ten Golden Crow Zombie Spirits rushed toward her!
"Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh … "
In addition to these Golden Crow Corpse Spirits that intended to engage in close combat, there were even more Golden Crow Corpse Spirits that spat out streams of blue flames in her path!
Just as Phoenix flipped over the back of a Golden Crow Zombie Spirit, several more Golden Crow Zombie Spirits came at her head to grab her.
Over a hundred Golden Crow Corpse Spirits took turns to fight the Phoenix Woman, completely entangling her!
"Get lost!
Phoenix suddenly stretched out her hands. A pair of delicate hands erupted with power that didn't match her size. She grabbed two Golden Crow Zombie Spirits and swung them back and forth.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
As the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits circled around, they were like a huge windmill, sweeping away the other Golden Crow Zombie Spirits.
But during this cleansing process, another figure stealthily approached.
That person was Luo Zheng!
He turned into a sharp arrow and pierced down diagonally from above, aiming his sword at Phoenix's back.
"Cha!"
Under the protection of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, Chaotic Origin's Sword, Luo Zheng's sword didn't injure Phoenix. But Phoenix's face revealed a look of shock.......................
Her Spirit of Chaotic Origin would likely become fatigued after a few more sword strikes …
.... this brat's's.
.......... a. Luo Zheng.
"Sou!"
Phoenix flapped her wings in an attempt to keep Luo Zheng behind. But Luo Zheng fled even faster than a rabbit after that strike. In the blink of an eye, he disappeared behind the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits. Closely following that, more Golden Crow Zombie Spirits surrounded him …
After Luo Zheng controlled these Golden Crow Zombie Spirits, he had been thinking of how to make use of them.
The Golden Crow Zombie Spirits were impervious to water, fire, and weapons. Their blue flames were even more terrifying. Their weakness was that they were too big, and their speed was too slow. Against a powerful existence like Phoenix, they could only rely on numbers!
But how to lure an expert like Phoenix into the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits was another problem.
Yet Phoenix actually used Great Teleportation to jump in. This was exactly what Luo Zheng wanted.
Luo Zheng was afraid that Phoenix would use Great Teleportation to escape. But looking at the current situation, she should not be able to use Great Teleportation alone …
"Peng!"
Following a muffled explosion, another Golden Crow Zombie Spirit was smashed away by Phoenix. But more Golden Crow Zombie Spirits surrounded her.
These Golden Crow Zombie Spirits were like a moving great array, trapping Phoenix layer by layer.
After Phoenix passed through two Golden Crow Zombie Spirits, Luo Zheng once again descended like a ghost.
"Shua!"
Yet another sword slashed down from Phoenix's back. If not for the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, this sword would have sliced off Phoenix's wings.
When Phoenix turned around to grab Luo Zheng, what awaited her were those Golden Crow Zombie Spirits that could not be torn apart and could not be caught …
At the same time when Luo Zheng was fighting with Phoenix, the battle between the Golden Crow Clan and the Great Yi Heaven Palace was also in full swing.
Gui Ling's plan was indeed good. The experts of the seven mountains did not expect that the Golden Crow Clan would take the initiative to teleport into the great arrays of their respective mountains.
In the blink of an eye, the Great Brightness Array, Great Gold Array, and Great Elegance Array were destroyed by the Golden Crow Clan.
But the experts of the seven mountains were not weak.
Even if they lost the support of the arrays, they could still fight back.
"Weng …"
The Mountain Master of the Great Brightness Mountain shook his sword, causing a blood light to shine.
An enormous blood world spread out. Facing such a tough battle, the Mountain Master of the Great Brightness Mountain directly descended into this world!
Luo Zheng's Green Jade Civilization's Weapon of Civilization and Evil Deities' Heaven Zhi Civilization's Weapon of Civilization were both limited to the 13th and 11th Layer Heavens. The power of the Weapon of Civilization was terrifying, but due to the pressure from the Paramita Realm, Luo Zheng and Evil Deities could not unleash its full power.
The Weapon of Civilization of the Great Brightness Mountain Master came from the 30th Layer Heavens, the Paramita Token of the Desire Realm. The power it unleashed was not something the Color Realm could compare with.
"Blood Descend!"
That blood light spread out, enveloping an area of half a million kilometers!
Within half a million kilometers was a blood world!
Everyone's eyes were filled with red …
"It's the Mountain Master of the Great Brightness Mountain's Blood World!"
"The rules of this area have been changed by him!"
"It's said that this blood world can suppress the power of all other bloodlines and use it to strengthen one's own bloodline!"
"Whoosh!"
The Mountain Master of the Great Brightness Mountain wielded his sword and turned into a blood light, piercing through a Jinwu cultivator.
"Do you think you're the only one who can descend into a world?"
Ghost Spirit let out a sinister laugh from afar. He spread his black wings, and black feathers floated into the air.
These black feathers were like blooming magic on a piece of white paper, spreading rapidly …
Soon, another world covered the blood-red world!
Black inky blobs floated around the entire world. These blobs of ink were like malicious spirits, flying toward living beings in all directions.
"Whoosh —"
A Tai Yiwei cultivator could not dodge in time and was invaded by the blob of ink. His entire body instantly turned black.
He looked at his hands and feet, fear written all over his face.
However, this fear only lasted for a short while.
Black spikes protruded from his back. His eye sockets sunk in, and his body expanded rapidly. His entire body twisted and turned into an indescribable monster.
"Come out, Illusory Court Monsters, hahaha!" Ghost Spirit laughed loudly.
The Weapon of Civilization he carried was a summoning type. Once it was activated, everything in the world would become a sacrifice.
The more sacrifices, the stronger the Illusory Court Monsters!
